Result: FastGAN生成的合成图像FID稳定收敛，真实再现叶片形态和侵染模式；数据增强显著提升所有分类模型的鲁棒性；ViT模型取得最高准确率和F1分数，EfficientNet表现均衡，ResNet-50较VGG16有中等提升；混淆矩阵分析显示先进架构减少了假阴性并改善了病害检测能力。

Conclusion: 基于FastGAN的数据增强能有效提升高光谱植物病害分类性能，而基于Transformer的模型在区分健康和受侵染叶片方面提供最可靠的判别能力。

Abstract: Early detection of aphid infestation in crops is essential for preventing yield loss and reducing unnecessary pesticide use. Hyperspectral imaging combined with Spectral Information Divergence (SID) analysis offers a non-destructive approach for monitoring plant health; however, deep learning methods applied to hyperspectral data are often limited by small dataset sizes. In this study, a data-efficient generative adversarial network (FastGAN) was employed to augment a hyperspectral SID dataset of faba bean leaves containing healthy and aphid-infested samples. The trained generator produced 10,000 synthetic images preserving structural and spectral characteristics of real samples. Image quality was evaluated using Frechet Inception Distance (FID), demonstrating stable convergence and realistic reconstruction of leaf morphology and infestation patterns. The augmented dataset was used to train four classification architectures: VGG16, ResNet-50, EfficientNet, and Vision Transformer (ViT). Results showed that dataset augmentation significantly improved classification robustness, with performance progressively increasing from classical convolutional networks to transformer-based models. The ViT model achieved the highest accuracy and F1-scores, while EfficientNet provided strong balanced performance and ResNet-50 showed moderate improvements over VGG16. Confusion matrix analysis confirmed reduced false negatives and improved disease detection when using advanced architectures. The findings demonstrate that FastGAN-based augmentation effectively enhances hyperspectral plant disease classification and that transformer-based models provide the most reliable discrimination between healthy and infested leaves.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Damage Assessment: Recyclable Material Detection in Aerial Disaster Imagery Using a Lightweight Patch-Based Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21279)
*Mahmoud Hazem,Karim Hammoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级方法，用于从灾害后航拍图像中高效检测可回收材料，并提供了一个公开可用的标注材料图像数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着各种性质的灾害日益增多，需要从航拍图像中识别受灾区域，这些区域含有丰富的可回收材料，可用于生态目的，但目前缺乏高效的检测方法和标注数据集。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级方法，用于从灾害航拍图像中高效检测可回收材料。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在定位可回收材料方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效检测可回收材料，并提供了一个公开可用的标注材料图像数据集以支持相关研究。

Abstract: Nowadays, more and more disasters of different natures are appearing. Several disaster assessment approaches have been developed in order to identify damaged areas from aerial images. These damaged areas contain rich material that could be recycled towards several ecological purposes. In this paper, we present a lightweight approach that permits the efficient detection of recyclable material. Experimental results show the potential of the proposed approach towards localizing recyclable materials. Accordingly, we provide a rare dataset of material images that we labeled towards supporting the development of recyclable material detectors. The dataset of labeled material images is publicly available at: anonymous.

</details>


### [72] [Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Object Pose Estimation with Contrastive Alignment and Pseudo-Label Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21287)
*Nidhal Eddine Chenni,Arunkumar Rathinam,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAPLR提出一种面向无监督域适应的物体姿态估计方法，通过跨域配对、对比对齐和伪标签精炼三个模块，在局部区域对齐姿态敏感特征，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UDA方法依赖全局特征匹配或图像翻译，忽略了特征表示中嵌入的与姿态相关的特定信息，导致域对齐时丢失对姿态估计至关重要的几何线索。

Method: CAPLR包含三个关键组件：(1) 高效跨域配对策略，利用中间特征在无监督下识别域间姿态相似的图像对；(2) 对比对齐，在中间和任务特定表示的局部区域进行特征对齐；(3) 一致性伪标签精炼，通过鼓励稳定目标预测提高可靠性。

Result: 在多个具有多样性和挑战性的物体姿态估计基准上取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CAPLR通过局部区域对齐姿态敏感特征，有效解决了现有UDA方法忽视姿态特定信息的问题，显著提升了跨域姿态估计的准确性。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) enables robust transfer of knowledge from simulated to real environments while exploiting a subset of unlabeled target data to improve real-world performance. Existing UDA methods for Object pose estimation often rely on global feature matching, multi-stage larger frameworks, or image translation pipelines, which tend to overlook the pose-specific information embedded in feature representations. To bridge this limitation, we introduce CAPLR that targets the adaptation of pose-sensitive features in localized regions, ensuring that domain alignment preserves the geometric cues essential for accurate pose estimation. CAPLR achieves UDA with three key components: (1) Efficient Cross-Domain Pairing strategy leveraging intermediate features to identify pose similar image pairs across domains without supervision; (2) Contrastive Alignment to perform feature alignment at localised regions in both intermediate and task-specific representations; and (3) Consistency-Based Pseudo-Label Refinement to improve reliability by encouraging stable target predictions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAPLR achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple well-known object pose estimation benchmarks featuring diverse and challenging scenarios.

</details>


### [73] [NoduLoCC2026: Lung Nodule Localization and Classification Contest from Chest X-Ray Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21290)
*Adnan Mustafic,Halim Benhabiles,Adnane Cabani,Kristhian André Oliveira Aguilar,Romain Amigon,Clément Bardin,Chiara Bentifece,Marin Boehm,Kévin Bouchard,Laura Burattini,Diedre Carmo,Fahima Idiri,Matthis Lahargoue,Ilaria Marcantoni,Hicham Messaoudi,Cyril Meyer,Farid Meziane,Léon Morales,Letícia Rittner,Agnese Sbrollini,Léonard Zipper,Karim Hammoudi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NoduLoCC2026挑战赛，旨在解决胸部X光图像中的肺结节检测和定位问题。研究提供了数据集，来自5个国际团队提交了解决方案。分类任务表现良好，最佳方法平衡准确率0.72，AUC-ROC 0.79；定位任务更具挑战性，最佳方法仅在53%的测试图像上正确预测结节数量，中位距离为12.83mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺结节检测和定位是胸部X光图像分析中的关键任务，现有方法在定位精度上仍存在显著不足，亟需系统性的评估和挑战来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 组织NoduLoCC2026挑战赛，提供专用数据集，邀请5个国际团队提交解决方案，并在外部测试数据集上进行评估。

Result: 分类任务最佳方法平衡准确率0.72，AUC-ROC 0.79；定位任务最佳方法在53%的测试图像上正确预测结节数量，中位距离12.83mm。

Conclusion: 当前方法在分类任务上表现尚可，但定位任务仍面临巨大挑战，定位精度和鲁棒性有待进一步提升。

Abstract: We propose NoduLoCC2026, a challenge on lung nodule detection and localization in chest X-ray images. We have provided a dataset for both tasks and received submissions from 5 international teams. The participating teams' solutions are presented in this work along with results on an external dataset used for testing. Proposed methods show good performance on the classification task. The best method shows a balanced accuracy score of 0.72 and AUC-ROC of 0.79. We highlight the limitations of current approaches for the localization task, with the best approach having predicted the correct number of nodules on 53\% of the test images with a median distance of 12.83mm, showing that it is a more challenging task than the first one. The challenge website is available via https://gt-i2mdp.github.io/website/nodule_challenge.html.

</details>


### [74] [Lightweight 3D Feature Pretraining by Bayesian Inversion of 2D Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21292)
*Marwane Hariat,Gianni Franchi,David Filliat,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Casper3D是一个轻量级概率框架，通过将多视图2D基础模型嵌入转换为潜在3D语义表示，在嘈杂环境下比简单多视图池化产生更稳定的3D语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法简单地对多视图2D语义特征进行池化，未能处理视图间噪声和歧义，导致3D语义表示不稳定。

Method: 将视图级语义特征视为底层3D语义状态的噪声观测，使用基于集合的变分模型结合相对姿态进行多视图推理，通过预测新视角的语义观测进行训练，保持与视觉和文本语义空间对齐。

Result: Casper3D在嘈杂和歧义设置下产生比简单多视图池化更稳定的3D语义表示。

Conclusion: Casper3D是一种轻量级、主干无关的概率框架，适用于语言对齐和自监督嵌入，有效提升3D开放词汇语义理解的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present Casper3D, a lightweight probabilistic framework for converting noisy multi-view 2D foundation-model embeddings into a latent 3D semantic representation. We model view-level semantic features as noisy observations of an underlying 3D semantic state and infer this state with a set-based variational model that incorporates relative pose during multi-view reasoning. Casper3D is trained by predicting held-out semantic observations from novel viewpoints, while remaining aligned with visual and text semantic spaces for open-vocabulary 3D understanding. The framework is backbone-agnostic and applies to both language-aligned and self-supervised embeddings. Experiments show that Casper3D produces more stable 3D semantics than simple multi-view pooling, especially in ambiguous and noisy settings.

</details>


### [75] [SCOPE: Scale-Consistent One-Pass Estimation of 3D Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21300)
*Zheng Zhang,Lihe Yang,Tianyu Yang,Chaohui Yu,Yixing Lao,Xiaoyang Guo,Biao Gong,Fan Wang,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种从单目长视频序列中估计3D几何结构的新方法，通过生成仿射不变3D点图并引入视点不变几何学习、外观不变学习和频率调制定位三大创新，在保持几何精度和时间一致性的同时，实现了单次处理数百帧的能力，在ScanNet上相对点图误差降低24.2%，时间对齐误差降低34.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理数百帧的长单目视频序列时，难以同时维持几何精度和时间一致性，亟需一种能够在整个序列中保持尺度一致的3D几何估计方法。

Method: 提出SCOPE方法，生成仿射不变3D点图并共享整个序列的参数；引入视点不变几何学习（多视角点在统一参考系中对齐）、外观不变学习（指数时间尺度上强制一致性）和频率调制定位（外推到远超训练长度的序列）三大创新。

Result: 在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法，在ScanNet上相对点图误差降低24.2%，时间对齐误差降低34.9%，能有效处理复杂相机轨迹和光照变化，单次高效处理长序列。

Conclusion: SCOPE通过尺度一致的单次估计框架，有效解决了长单目视频序列的3D几何估计难题，在精度和时间一致性上均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: We present SCOPE (Scale-Consistent One-Pass Estimation of 3D Geometry), a novel approach for estimating 3D geometry from extended monocular video sequences, where existing methods struggle to maintain both geometric accuracy and temporal consistency across hundreds of frames. Our approach generates affine-invariant 3D point maps with shared parameters across entire sequences, enabling consistent scale-invariant representations. We introduce three key innovations: viewpoint-invariant geometry aligning multi-perspective points in a unified reference frame; appearance-invariant learning enforcing consistency across exponential timescales; and frequency-modulated positioning enabling extrapolation to sequences vastly exceeding training length. Experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate significant improvements, reducing relative point map error by 24.2% and temporal alignment error by 34.9% on ScanNet compared to state-of-the-art methods. Our approach handles challenging scenarios with complex camera trajectories and lighting variations while efficiently processing extended sequences in a single pass. Project page: https://scope3d.github.io/.

</details>


### [76] [A Test-time Actor-Critic Approach to News Images Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21304)
*Damianos Galanopoulos,Vasileios Mezaris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACIG方法，一种基于强化学习中Actor-Critic范式的测试时、模型无关的图像生成方法，在MediaEval NewsImages 2026挑战中通过反馈循环迭代优化提示词，最终在排行榜上取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对新闻标题自动生成相关图像的任务，现有方法在生成图像与标题语义匹配度上存在不足，需要一种能够自主评估并迭代优化的图像生成策略。

Method: 受Actor-Critic强化学习范式启发，提出ACIG方法：首先生成图像创建提示词（Actor），然后生成图像，通过评估模块（Critic）对生成结果进行评价，若不满足要求则反馈优化提示词，形成闭环迭代过程。该方法为模型无关，仅在测试时运行。

Result: 在MediaEval NewsImages 2026挑战赛中获得最佳成绩，位列排行榜第一。

Conclusion: ACIG通过引入测试时的反馈循环机制，有效提升了新闻标题到图像的生成质量，验证了Actor-Critic范式在图像生成任务中的适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces the CERTH-ITI solution for the MediaEval NewsImages 2026 challenge, which focuses on generating images related to news headlines. Inspired by the Actor-Critic paradigm in reinforcement learning, we present a test-time, model-agnostic Actor-Critic Image Generation approach (ACIG). ACIG generates prompts for image creation, produces the images, evaluates the generated results, and if needed refines the image generation prompts accordingly in a feedback loop. ACIG achieved the best results in the NewsImages 2026 challenge, according to the challenge's leaderboard.

</details>


### [77] [WildBox: A Dataset and Benchmark for Aerial Monocular 3D Detection of African Savanna Wildlife](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21309)
*Vandita Shukla,Kilian Meier,Lucie Laporte-Devylder,Camille Rondeau Saint-Jean,Jenna M. Kline,Blair R. Costelloe,Devis Tuia,Fabio Remondino,Benjamin Risse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildBox是一个面向无人机视频中野生动物单目3D检测的数据集和基准，包含7个非洲稀树草原物种的237,505个3D边界框标注。实验表明，开放词汇的2D基础模型在零样本下可实现可用的野生动物定位（50.55 AP@50），但零样本3D检测完全失效（0.00 AP），深度估计是主要瓶颈。微调后恢复性能（8.68 AP-BEV@0.50），而粗到细的课程学习（如先合并斑马类预训练再微调细分）能以更少计算量提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机技术在野生动物监测中日益普及，但现有方法主要依赖2D检测，缺乏从单目无人机视频中获取3D信息（如动物体长、高度）的能力。现有3D检测数据集（如KITTI、Omni3D）主要针对城市场景，缺乏面向野生动物领域的标注数据。因此，作者构建WildBox以填补这一空白，系统评估开放词汇单目3D检测方法在野生动物场景下的能力与局限。

Method: 构建包含237,505个3D边界框标注的WildBox数据集，涵盖7个非洲稀树草原物种（分为6个基准类别），采用KITTI/Omni3D兼容的每段尺度归一化相机坐标系格式，并保持实例身份跨段一致。评估两种开放词汇单目3D架构（OVMono3D-LIFT和DetAny3D），在零样本、地面真值2D框提示和微调三种协议下测试。提出粗到细课程学习方法（先在合并斑马类预训练，再在细分类上微调）。

Result: 零样本2D检测达50.55 AP@50，但零样本3D检测为0.00 AP（即使使用地面真值2D框提示）。微调后AP-BEV@0.50为8.68±0.47，AP3D宏平均为13.17±0.69。深度贡献了微调后84%和零样本下99%以上的归一化Hausdorff距离误差。粗到细课程学习在总计算量更少的情况下提升了宏平均3D性能，对两个斑马亚类提升最大。

Conclusion: 单目航空深度估计是野生动物3D检测领域的主要未解决问题；2D基础模型可提供可用的零样本定位，但缺乏向3D的泛化能力；WildBox数据集可为这一方向的研究提供基准支持。数据集、评估代码和基线检查点均已开源。

Abstract: We introduce WildBox, a dataset and benchmark for monocular 3D detection of wildlife from drone video, comprising 237,505 3D bounding box annotations across seven African savanna species grouped into six benchmark classes. Annotations follow a KITTI/Omni3D-compatible format in a per-segment scale-normalised camera frame, with instance identities maintained across each segment. We evaluate two open-vocabulary monocular 3D architectures, OVMono3D-LIFT and DetAny3D, under zero-shot, ground-truth 2D box prompt, and supervised fine-tuning protocols. Open-vocabulary 2D foundation models provide usable zero-shot wildlife localisation (50.55 AP@50), but zero-shot 3D detection collapses to 0.00 AP across both architectures and every 2D-input condition tested, including ground-truth 2D box prompts, thus isolating the failure to the 3D stage. Fine-tuning on WildBox recovers performance to 8.68 +/- 0.47 AP-BEV@0.50 and 13.17 +/- 0.69 AP3D macro. Depth contributes 84% of normalised Hausdorff distance after fine-tuning and over 99% in zero-shot, identifying monocular aerial depth as the dominant open problem in this regime. A coarse-to-fine curriculum, i.e. pretraining on a merged zebra class before fine-tuning on the Grevy's/plains split, improves macro 3D performance with less total compute, with the largest gains on the two zebra subclasses. WildBox is released with video-level splits, evaluation code, and baseline checkpoints to enable progress in 3D wildlife perception from drone video.

</details>


### [78] [LEViL: Label-Efficient Video Learning via Zero-Shot Distillation over VLM-Generated Pseudo-Label Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21358)
*Aslı Çelik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免标注视频预训练框架，利用视觉-语言模型生成语义伪标签进行预训练，并结合目标标签集感知微调，在有限标注条件下提升视频动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有监督视频预训练依赖大规模标注数据集，成本高且难以扩展，且源域与目标域的相似性影响迁移效果。本研究旨在消除对人工标注预训练数据的依赖，实现标签高效的视频学习。

Method: 1) 预训练阶段：VLM为未标注视频生成文本描述并构建语义伪标签空间，冻结的视频-语言模型生成零样本软目标分布，学生视频编码器学习语义丰富的表征。2) 微调阶段：结合目标域标注视频的监督学习与目标标签集上的零样本蒸馏，保留VLM语义指导的同时适配目标任务。

Result: 在UCF101和HMDB51上，所提框架在所有评估的有限标注设定下优于对比的半监督视频动作识别方法。免标注预训练阶段学到的表征在完整数据微调中也能提供有效初始化。

Conclusion: 提出了一种标签高效的视频学习框架，结合免标注预训练与目标标签集感知微调，有效降低了视频动作识别对人工标注的依赖，且预训练表征具有良好的迁移性。

Abstract: Supervised video pretraining is a common transfer learning practice for improving downstream action recognition performance. However, it requires large-scale labeled source datasets, and the effectiveness of the learned initialization is influenced by the similarity between the source and target domains. Constructing such labeled pretraining datasets for different target domains is costly and difficult to scale. To address these limitations, this study proposes a label-efficient video learning framework that combines annotation-free video pretraining with target-label-set-aware fine-tuning. During pretraining, a vision-language model (VLM) generates textual descriptions of unlabeled videos, which are processed to construct an interpretable semantic pseudo-label space. A frozen video-language model then produces zero-shot soft target distributions over this space, allowing a student video encoder to learn semantically rich representations without manual source annotations. During downstream adaptation, target-label-set-aware fine-tuning combines supervised learning from labeled target videos with zero-shot distillation over the actual target label set, helping preserve VLM-derived semantic guidance while adapting the pretrained encoder to the target task. Experiments on UCF101 and HMDB51 show that the proposed framework outperforms the compared semi-supervised video action recognition methods across all evaluated limited-label regimes. Moreover, the annotation-free pretraining stage learns transferable representations that provide an effective initialization for full-data fine-tuning, despite relying on a comparatively modest unlabeled pretraining pool.

</details>


### [79] [Graph-of-Differences: Anatomy-Structured Difference Alignment for Medical Image Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21368)
*Nichula Wasalathilaka,Abhijit Das,Imran Razzak,Dwarikanath Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-of-Differences (GoD)方法，通过构建解剖图结构进行医学图像再识别，利用图级差异对齐目标将检索信号锚定在解剖结构上，并提供基于命名图节点的可验证解释。在眼底和胸片数据集上，Rank-1分别提升7.1和3.1个百分点，零样本外推也验证了方法的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像再识别（MedReID）面临两个关键问题：一是容易陷入捷径学习（shortcut learning），二是缺乏基于解剖结构的可审计决策，临床医生无法验证模型判断是否基于正确的解剖区域。

Method: 1) 将每张图像表示为解剖图，节点对应命名的解剖区域；2) 对图像对建立软节点对应关系，计算匹配解剖节点间的差异；3) 设计图级差异对齐目标，使解剖匹配的差异与全局骨干网络差异对齐；4) 基于命名图节点定义解释，通过节点插入/删除测试进行定量审计。

Result: 在内部基准测试中，GoD在眼底图像上Rank-1提升7.1个百分点，在CXR上提升3.1个百分点；在零样本外部迁移中也有进一步提升，验证了解剖锚定对准确性和泛化性的双重提升。

Conclusion: 将身份比较锚定在显式的解剖结构上，不仅能提升MedReID的检索精度和泛化能力，还能提供可审计的结构级解释，替代不稳定的像素热力图，增强临床可解释性。

Abstract: Medical image re-identification (MedReID) enables longitudinal patient linkage but remains vulnerable to shortcut learning and often produces decisions that clinicians cannot audit against named anatomy. We propose Graph-of-Differences (GoD), which grounds identity comparisons in explicit anatomical structure. Each image is represented as an anatomy graph whose nodes correspond to named anatomical regions; given an image pair, soft node correspondence is established, and differences are computed over matched anatomy. A graph-level difference alignment objective ties these anatomy-matched differences to the global backbone difference, ensuring the retrieval signal is anchored in homologous structures rather than arbitrary spatial tokens. Explanations are defined over named graph nodes and quantitatively audited via node insertion/deletion tests, replacing unstable pixel heatmaps with verifiable structure-level evidence. On internal benchmarks, GoD improves Rank-1 by +7.1 pp on fundus and +3.1 pp on CXR over a strong frozen-backbone baseline, with further gains on zero-shot external transfers confirming that anatomy grounding improves both accuracy and generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/GoD.git.

</details>


### [80] [FLM-Occ: Feed-forward Likelihood Maximization for Efficient Indoor Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21373)
*Guangcheng Chen,Lihuang Fang,Huaqi Tao,Yicheng He,Li He,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FLM框架，将占用预测重构为体素分布估计，通过前向最大化似然训练混合模型，仅用32个超二次曲面（先前最优方法的2.7%）实现更高精度，速度提升3.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯原语的室内占用预测方法依赖体素分类训练，仅施加局部约束而缺乏对原语分布的全局监督，导致在空区域预测虚假原语，损害表示和计算效率。

Method: 提出前向似然最大化（FLM）框架：将占用预测重构为体素分布估计，训练网络预测混合模型以最大化地面真值占用体素的似然；通过将混合权重定义为归一化原语体积来隐式施加单纯形约束，并推导新的体素化公式，实现网络端到端训练。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet上，FLM-Occ仅用32个超二次曲面（先前最优方法的2.7%）实现更高精度，运行速度快3.7倍。

Conclusion: FLM通过全局似然最大化替代局部体素分类，有效消除虚假原语，显著提升稀疏表示的效率与精度。

Abstract: Recent indoor occupancy prediction methods adopt Gaussian primitives as a sparse 3D representation for computational efficiency. However, their training relies on voxel classification, which imposes only local constraints and lacks global supervision on the distribution of the primitives. Therefore, they inevitably predict spurious primitives in empty regions, undermining both representational and computational efficiency. To address this, we propose Feed-forward Likelihood Maximization (FLM), a novel framework that reformulates occupancy prediction as voxel distribution estimation. In FLM, a network is trained to predict a mixture model that maximizes the likelihood over ground-truth occupied voxels in a feed-forward manner. To enable end-to-end training of networks and voxelization of a standard mixture model, we define mixture weights as normalized primitive volumes to implicitly enforce simplex constraints and derive novel voxelization formulas. Based on FLM, our FLM-Occ, a novel method that is capable of relocating randomly initialized primitives over long distances to model a scene. On Occ-ScanNet, FLM-Occ achieves superior accuracy using only 32 superquadrics, 2.7% of the prior SoTA, while running 3.7 times faster.

</details>


### [81] [OSOG: A Differentiable, Physics-Informed Synthetic Data Engine for Micro-Optical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21381)
*Caio Silva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OSOG，一个高性能、完全可微的前向建模引擎，用于计算显微镜中的合成数据生成。它基于物理波动光学模型，通过PyTorch-native的SoA架构实现亚线性缩放的实时数据集生成（4万粒子<50ms），并在零样本迁移到真实显微图像和逆向渲染中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算显微镜中的深度学习受限于密集标注数据的稀缺。现有合成数据方法中，传统图形引擎基于几何光线追踪无法捕捉显微光学现象，而波动光学公式在深度学习所需规模下的计算可追踪性面临巨大系统挑战。

Method: OSOG基于衍射和相位延迟的物理模型，将连续光程差（OPD）计算映射到高度优化的、PyTorch原生的结构体数组（SoA）架构中，实现完全可微的前向建模。同时提出DiffOSOG，利用端到端可微性通过课程引导的逆向渲染恢复连续光学参数。

Result: 1) YOLOv11-OBB在OSOG生成数据上训练后，可零样本迁移到真实高度遮挡的溶菌酶显微图像；2) DiffOSOG通过逆向渲染精确恢复连续光学参数；3) OSOG绕过顺序光线追踪的O(N)瓶颈，实现亚线性缩放，在<50ms内合成4万个复杂波动光学粒子（>20 FPS）。

Conclusion: OSOG通过提供快速、可扩展且物理可信的张量管线，实现了真正的实时、随需应变的显微数据集生成，为计算显微镜中的深度学习数据稀缺问题提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning in computational microscopy is severely constrained by the scarcity of densely annotated datasets. While synthetic data generation has bridged this gap in macroscopic computer vision, traditional graphics engines rely on geometric ray-tracing, failing to capture the micro-optical phenomena required for microscopy. Conversely, while wave-optics formulations exist, rendering them computationally tractable at the scale required for deep learning remains a massive systems challenge. To address this, we introduce the Optical Synthetic Object Generator (OSOG), a high-performance, fully differentiable forward-modeling engine. Drawing on established physical models of diffraction and phase retardation, OSOG maps continuous Optical Path Difference (OPD) calculations into a highly optimized, PyTorch-native Structure-of-Arrays (SoA) architecture. We validate this computational framework across three axes: First, object detection models (YOLOv11-OBB) trained purely on OSOG-generated data achieve robust zero-shot transfer to real-world highly occluded Lysozyme micrographs. Second, we introduce DiffOSOG, demonstrating that the engine's end-to-end differentiability allows for the exact recovery of continuous optical parameters via curriculum-guided inverse rendering. Finally, OSOG bypasses the $\mathcal{O}(N)$ bottlenecks of sequential ray-tracing, demonstrating sub-linear scaling by synthesizing 40,000 complex wave-optic particles in under 50 milliseconds (\>20 FPS). By providing a fast, scalable, and physically grounded tensor pipeline, OSOG enables true real-time, on-the-fly dataset generation.

</details>


### [82] [EnTrust: Modeling Inter-Modal Conflict for Trustworthy Multimodal Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21384)
*Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Abhijit Das,Behzad Bozorgtabar,Zongyuan Ge,Sudipta Roy,Deepak Nayak,Mauricio Reyes,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EnTrust是一个多模态医学图像分割框架，通过显式建模模态间冲突作为不确定性来源，将多模态特征解耦为共享解剖共识、模态特定线索和空间定位冲突信号，结合扩散生成模型和信任图谱实现像素级校准不确定性，在四个基准上达到SOTA分割精度，校准误差降低40%，单个模型性能超越5x深度集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型处理多模态不一致的方式存在两种不足：确定性融合会平均掉模态间的分歧，而与融合过程解耦的后验不确定性估计无法解释预测不可靠的根本原因。临床需要理解预测为什么不可靠。

Method: 提出EnTrust框架：(1) EnFuse模块将多模态特征解耦为共享共识F_c、模态特定F_{u,m}和冲突信号F_{cf}三个分量，通过交叉协方差目标强制独立性；(2) SegDiff扩散生成分割模型，使采样假设在模态不一致区域产生发散；(3) TrustMap通过集成熵、冲突引导扰动探测和学习校准头将假设发散转化为校准的像素级不确定性。

Result: 在脑部、心脏、病灶和肿瘤四个基准上达到SOTA分割精度，校准误差相比最强基线降低40%，单个模型性能超越5x深度集成，内存占用约一半。

Conclusion: EnTrust通过显式建模模态间冲突作为不确定性来源，实现了高精度且可解释的不确定性量化，为多模态医学图像分割提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal medical imaging fuses complementary anatomical and functional information, yet modalities frequently disagree in pathologically heterogeneous regions. Current segmentation models handle this in one of two inadequate ways: deterministic fusion that averages away disagreement, or post-hoc uncertainty estimation decoupled from the fusion process that produces it. Both obscure the clinically critical question: why is this prediction unreliable? We present EnTrust, a framework that treats inter-modal conflict as the primary source of predictive uncertainty. Our EnFuse module decomposes multimodal features into three disentangled components: shared anatomical consensus (F_c), modality-specific cues (F_{u,m}), and spatially localized conflict signals (F_{cf}), with independence enforced via a cross-covariance objective. This structured decomposition conditions SegDiff, a diffusion-based generative segmentation model whose sampled hypotheses diverge specifically in regions of modal disagreement. TrustMap then translates this hypothesis divergence into calibrated, pixel-wise uncertainty using ensemble entropy, conflict-guided perturbation probing, and a learned calibration head, enabling clinicians to understand not only where predictions are uncertain, but why. Across four benchmarks spanning brain, cardiac, lesion, and oncology domains, EnTrust achieves state-of-the-art segmentation accuracy while reducing calibration error by 40% compared to the strongest baseline. Notably, it outperforms 5x deep ensembles using a single model at roughly half the memory footprint. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/EnTrust.git.

</details>


### [83] [MIRCaps: A Large-Scale Mixed-Domain Dataset with Image-Level and Region-Level Captions for Fine-Grained Vision-Language Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21419)
*Arlindo Luciano Tulumba Roberto,Hyungjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含141,364张图像、约98万图像级描述、174万区域级描述和139万边界框注释的大规模多模态数据集，用于增强视觉语言模型在通用和CCTV视频监控场景下的细粒度视觉理解能力。实验表明，轻量级VLM（如SmolVLM-256M-Instruct、BLIP、BLIP2、Qwen2.5-VL 3B-Instruct）可通过该数据集有效微调，提升图像描述和物体检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合域图像-描述数据集在通用和CCTV视频监控场景下仍较为有限，无法支持VLM学习细粒度视觉属性（如物体类别、尺寸、颜色、动作、状态及环境上下文）。

Method: 构建大规模多模态数据集，每张图像平均配7个图像级描述（描述场景不同方面）和7个区域级描述（对应每个标注边界框），覆盖物体类别、估计尺寸、颜色、动作、状态及环境上下文等细粒度属性。

Result: 轻量级VLM（SmolVLM-256M-Instruct、BLIP、BLIP2、Qwen2.5-VL 3B-Instruct）通过该数据集微调后，在图像描述和物体检测两个下游任务上表现有效。

Conclusion: 该数据集有效弥补了混合域VLM训练数据的不足，且支持轻量级模型微调，有助于推动通用和CCTV监控场景下的视觉语言模型研究。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), mixed-domain image-caption datasets for both general-purpose and CCTV-based video surveillance systems remain limited. To address this gap, we introduce a large-scale multimodal dataset comprising 141,364 images, 981,947 image-level captions, 1,742,264 region-level captions, and 1,391,779 bounding box annotations. Each image is associated with an average of seven image-level captions describing different aspects of the overall scene, as well as seven region-level captions for each annotated bounding box. These complementary caption types are designed to help VLMs learn fine-grained visual attributes, including object categories, estimated sizes, colors, actions, states, and surrounding environmental context. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the dataset on two important downstream tasks: image captioning and object detection. Experimental results show that lightweight VLMs, including SmolVLM-256M-Instruct, BLIP, BLIP2, and Qwen2.5-VL 3B-Instruct, can be effectively fine-tuned using our dataset. Our dataset and code are publicly available at https://zenodo.org/records/20418601.

</details>


### [84] [Synergistic Dual-Branch Adaptation for Multi-modal Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21446)
*Yuxun Qu,Minyu Zhou,Yongqiang Tang,Chenyang Zhang,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对广义类别发现（GCD）任务中现有双分支多模态方法存在的跨模态协同粗糙、文本偏差未处理、监督粒度不足等问题，本文提出即插即用的协同双分支适配（SDBA）框架，通过跨模态协同适配器和邻域互学习模块，在编码过程中注入视觉信息增强文本特征，并利用双向KL散度约束局部邻域分布一致性，在六个基准数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCD双分支方法中，两模态独立编码导致文本偏差和噪声未被处理，且互学习策略仅基于全局类级锚点，缺乏细粒度关系监督，跨模态协同粗糙不完整。

Method: 提出SDBA框架，包含两个组件：(1) 跨模态协同适配器，在双分支中插入轻量适配器，并在每层编码器中向文本适配器注入视觉信息以增强文本特征学习；(2) 邻域互学习模块，通过双向KL散度约束两分支的局部邻域分布一致性，为旧类和新类提供细粒度关系监督。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上取得最先进性能，在不同基线上的持续改进验证了框架的广泛可扩展性。

Conclusion: SDBA作为一种即插即用增强框架，可兼容现有双分支方法（如GET和TextGCD），有效解决了跨模态协同不充分的问题，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) aims to classify old categories and discover new ones from unlabeled data. Recent multi-modal approaches introduce retrieved or synthesized texts into a dual-branch architecture to provide semantic cues complementary to visual features. However, the cross-modal synergy in existing dual-branch methods remains coarse and incomplete: the two modalities are encoded independently with the bias and noise in the derived text left unaddressed during encoding, and existing mutual learning strategies operate only on global class-level anchors, lacking fine-grained relational supervision. To address these limitations, we propose the Synergistic Dual-Branch Adaptation (SDBA) framework, which serves as a plug-and-play enhancement compatible with existing dual-branch methods such as GET and TextGCD. SDBA comprises two components: the cross-modal synergistic adapter inserts lightweight adapters into both branches and further injects visual information into the text adapter at each encoder layer to enhance text feature learning during encoding; the neighborhood mutual learning module enforces consistent local neighborhood distributions between the two branches via bidirectional KL divergence, providing fine-grained relational supervision for both old and new classes. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, and consistent improvements on different baselines validate the broad scalability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [85] [Technical Report for ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge: Exploring Query-Based Segmentation and Increased Spatial Context for Outdoor Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21456)
*David Pascual-Hernández,Roberto Calvo-Palomino,Inmaculada Mora-Jiménez,Jose María Cañas-Plaza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了在ICRA 2026 GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战中的提交方案，基于SegFormer基线逐步改进，采用更大训练裁剪尺寸、Mask2Former查询架构和测试时增强，最终在测试集上达到69.6% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与GOOSE 2D细粒度语义分割挑战，建立强基线以推动户外环境细粒度语义分割研究。

Method: 从SegFormer基线出发，逐步增大训练裁剪尺寸、采用基于查询的Mask2Former架构，并应用测试时增强。

Result: 最终提交在挑战测试集上达到69.6% mIoU。

Conclusion: 基于查询的分割显著优于基线模型，增大训练裁剪尺寸有助于保留场景上下文以进行细粒度语义区分，该方案为户外细粒度语义分割提供了强基线。

Abstract: In this report, we present our submission to the GOOSE 2D Fine-Grained Semantic Segmentation Challenge, organized as part of the Workshop on Field Robotics at ICRA 2026. The challenge combines data from the GOOSE and GOOSE-Ex datasets, which comprise more than 13k images captured from 4 distinct camera setups, annotated using a hierarchical taxonomy of 56 fine-grained classes and 11 broader categories. Starting from SegFormer as a baseline, we progressively improve segmentation performance through increased training crop sizes, a transition to the query-based Mask2Former architecture, and test-time augmentation. Our experiments show that query-based segmentation significantly outperforms the baseline model. Furthermore, increasing the crop size used during training yields substantial gains, highlighting the relevance of preserving scene context for fine-grained semantic disambiguation. Our final submission, using test-time augmentation, achieves an mIoU of 69.6% on the challenge test set, providing a strong baseline for fine-grained semantic segmentation in outdoor environments. To facilitate reproducibility and future research, code and weights will be made publicly available at https://github.com/RoboticsLabURJC/outdoor-fine-grained-segmentation .

</details>


### [86] [Native space based pipelines outperform template space based pipeline in subcortical segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21463)
*Tomás Lima,Daniel Novák,Eduard Bakštein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了原生空间与模板空间在丘脑底核、红核和黑质等皮层下核团分割中的性能，开发了基于UNet的7T和3T MRI分割流程。结果表明原生空间分割在7T数据上显著优于模板空间（STN Dice: 0.775 vs 0.713），增加模板分辨率无改善；但7T模型迁移到3T时性能大幅下降，合成3T数据仅带来微弱提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化皮层下核团分割方法多依赖模板空间配准，可能影响患者特异性精度，尤其是在小结构中。需要评估原生空间方法是否能提供可测量的优势，特别是在运动障碍疾病的背景下。

Method: 开发了两种基于UNet的STN、RN和SN分割流程（原生空间 vs 模板空间），使用来自五个公开数据集的7T和3T MRI数据，在原生空间中对人工标签进行评估；研究了模板分辨率的影响；测试了7T训练模型向3T临床图像的迁移能力，以及使用解耦表示学习方法生成合成3T训练数据来缩小域差距的效果。

Result: 在7T数据上，原生空间分割一致优于模板空间：STN的Dice为0.775±0.055 vs 0.713±0.051（1 mm模板），HD95为0.79±0.24 mm vs 1.17±1.10 mm。RN和SN也有类似优势。增加模板分辨率未改善精度。迁移到3T图像时所有模型性能大幅下降，合成3T数据仅带来微弱改善且不降低7T性能。

Conclusion: 原生空间分割更适合需要患者特异性解剖保真度的应用（如帕金森病手术规划）；缩小7T到3T的域差距仍然是开放挑战，需要针对皮层下结构的域自适应方法。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of subcortical regions is critical for neurosurgical planning and functional research. Most automated methods rely on template space coregistration, which may compromise patient-specific accuracy, particularly in small structures. We identify a need to evaluate whether native space approaches offer a measurable advantage, which we evaluate in the context of movement disorders. We developed two UNet-based segmentation pipelines of the Subthalamic Nucleus (STN) - a common surgical target in Parkinson's Disease - and the neighbouring Red Nucleus (RN) and Substantia Nigra (SN). We collected 7T and 3T MRI data from five public datasets. The pipelines were evaluated in the native-space against manual labels. We further investigated the effect of the template resolution. Motivated by the hypothesis that models may better learn target boundaries in higher field, we tested the transferability of 7T-trained models to 3T clinical images, and whether synthetic 3T training data - generated via a disentangled representation learning method - could help bridging this domain gap. On held-out 7T data, the native pipeline consistently outperformed the template one. For the STN, native-space Dice reached 0.775 +- 0.055 versus 0.713 +- 0.051 (1 mm template), with HD95 of 0.79 +- 0.24 mm versus 1.17 +- 1.10 mm, respectively. Similar advantages were observed for the RN and SN. Increasing template resolution did not improve accuracy. When applied to 3T images, all models showed a considerable performance drop. Adding synthetic 3T data yielded only modest improvements, though without degrading 7T performance. Native-space segmentation is preferable for applications requiring patient specific anatomical fidelity, such as the surgical planning in PD. Bridging the 7T-to-3T domain gap remains an open challenge, motivating future work on domain adaptation tailored to subcortical structures.

</details>


### [87] [Semi-Supervised Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21493)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemiVLA提出了一种半监督VLA适应框架，通过自蒸馏教师-学生架构从少量带动作标签的轨迹和大量无标签轨迹中学习，利用可靠性控制器和瓶颈投影对齐更新生成可信伪动作，在LIBERO和CALVIN上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖大量带动作标签的演示数据进行环境适配，标注成本高昂。为降低对动作标签的依赖，研究在半监督场景下（仅少量轨迹含动作标签）进行VLA适应，其中缺失的监督信号是必须视觉定位、语言一致、物理可行且时间稳定的具身动作信号。

Method: 提出SemiVLA，一个自蒸馏教师-学生框架。(1) 设计VLA特定可靠性控制器，评估视觉-语言对齐、动作可行性和时间过渡一致性；(2) 采用瓶颈投影对齐更新（Bottleneck-Projected Alignment Update）更新教师模型，避免噪声反馈污染；(3) 在无标签轨迹上生成可靠伪动作进行学习。

Result: 以OpenVLA为骨干，在LIBERO和CALVIN上一致提升多种PEFT策略。10%标注轨迹下，SemiVLA+Selective LoRA在LIBERO上平均成功率达89.0%，比有监督LoRA高出8.0个百分点，且不增加推理成本。

Conclusion: SemiVLA有效解决了半监督VLA适应中伪动作可靠性问题，显著降低了对动作标注数据的依赖，为VLA模型在实际场景中的低成本部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to predict actions directly from visual observations and language instructions, but adapting them to new environments still depends on costly action-labeled demonstrations. To reduce this dependence, we study semi-supervised VLA adaptation under limited supervision signals, where only a small portion of trajectories contain robot actions and the remaining trajectories provide action-unlabeled vision-language observations. Unlike standard semi-supervised learning, the missing supervision is an embodied action signal that must be visually grounded, language-consistent, physically feasible, and temporally stable. To address this problem, we propose SemiVLA, a self-distilled teacher-student framework that learns from reliable pseudo-actions on unlabeled trajectories. SemiVLA introduces a VLA-specific reliability controller to assess vision-language alignment, action feasibility, and temporal transition consistency, and further updates the teacher through a Bottleneck-Projected Alignment Update to avoid noisy feedback contamination. With OpenVLA as the backbone, SemiVLA consistently improves multiple PEFT strategies across LIBERO and CALVIN. Under 10\% labeled trajectories, SemiVLA with Selective LoRA achieves 89.0\% average success on LIBERO, outperforming supervised LoRA by 8.0 points without extra inference cost.

</details>


### [88] [Compressing Observation History into Agent Memory: Distilling Transformers into Recurrent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21562)
*Philippe Weinzaepfel,Christian Wolf,Bülent Mert Sariyildiz,Guillaume Bono,Gianluca Monaci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种蒸馏方法，通过将全历史Transformer的压缩策略转移给循环Transformer，使后者在保持线性时间复杂度的同时显著缩小与全历史模型的性能差距，应用于长时域流式视觉与机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer在长序列处理中具有计算优势，但其性能远落后于全历史Transformer，作者认为这种差距并非源于架构限制，而是由于循环模型在没有完整历史的情况下学习压缩信息的难度更大。

Method: 设计一个显式将观测历史压缩为固定大小瓶颈表示的教师模型（全历史Transformer），通过用该瓶颈表示直接监督学生模型（循环Transformer）的记忆状态，对齐两种压缩机制，实现蒸馏。

Result: 训练出的循环潜在机器人记忆模型具有线性时间复杂度，同时显著缩小了与全历史Transformer之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏对齐压缩机制可以有效弥合循环Transformer与全历史Transformer之间的性能鸿沟，为长时域流式应用提供实用方案。

Abstract: Transformers are AI's workhorse with strong performance in modeling sequential data, but their computational cost becomes prohibitive when processing long sequences. We target long-horizon streaming vision and robotics applications like map-free pose estimation, where it is particularly impractical to store and maintain a history of observations. Recurrent Transformers address this limitation by maintaining fixed-size memory but their performance lags behind that of transformers operating over the full observation history. We argue that this gap does not stem from architectural limitations, but from differences in how these models learn to compress past information. Without access to an observation history, recurrent models must explicitly decide what to retain in memory at each step, a significantly harder learning problem. In this work, we propose a distillation approach that transfers the compression strategy of a classical full-history transformer to a recurrent variant. We enable this by designing a teacher model that explicitly compresses its observation history into a fixed-size bottleneck representation. By directly supervising the student's memory with this bottleneck representation, we align the two compression mechanisms. We show that this approach allows to train a recurrent latent robotic memory with linear-time complexity while substantially narrowing the performance gap to full-history transformers.

</details>


### [89] [A Smart Classroom Behavior Analysis Framework with a New Highly Congested Classroom Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21568)
*Wei Xu,Maoxiang Chu,Yuelong Fan,Guanghao Liao,Yinxiang Yu,Zhi Chen,Haotian Wang,Yutian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含50,229个实例的密集课堂行为数据集HCCB，并提出了基于YOLO的ODER-HSFNet检测框架，通过遮挡感知变形边缘矫正器(ODER)、超图状态空间融合模块(HSSF)和遮挡校准检测头(OCDetect)三个创新模块，在密集遮挡场景下显著提升了课堂行为检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大班课堂场景中存在密集实例共现、非对称遮挡、深度尺度变化以及远距离目标的细粒度语义退化等挑战，现有数据集和通用检测器无法充分表征和解决这些问题。

Method: 提出ODER-HSFNet框架，包含三个核心模块：1) ODER：遮挡感知变形边缘矫正器，增强遮挡下的边界证据；2) HSSF：超图状态空间融合模块，整合局部结构增强、状态空间上下文建模和高阶关系聚合；3) OCDetect：遮挡校准检测头，抑制低质量Pre-NMS候选框并减少遮挡边界和邻近实例的误检。

Result: 在HCCB数据集上达到60.60% mAP50:95和80.12% mAP50，在SCB-D3-S数据集上达到57.36%/74.65%，优于主流YOLO系列方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的ODER-HSFNet针对密集课堂行为检测设计，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性，为拥挤场景下的细粒度行为识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Student behavior detection is important for intelligent classroom analysis but remains challenging in large-class scenarios due to dense instance co-occurrence, asymmetric occlusion, depth-wise scale variation, and fine-grained semantic degradation in distant targets. Existing classroom behavior datasets and general-purpose detectors are insufficient to characterize and address these challenges. This paper constructs the Highly Congested Classroom Behavior (HCCB) dataset, containing 50,229 student behavior instances across seven categories: reading, writing, heads up, sleeping, looking around, bowing head, and using phone. HCCB provides a challenging benchmark that integrates dense distributions, severe occlusion, scale variation, and fine-grained behavioral semantics. To address these issues, we propose ODER-HSFNet, a YOLO-based detection framework tailored to highly crowded classrooms. At its core, ODER-HSFNet introduces three task-specific innovations: the Occlusion-aware Deformable Edge Rectifier (ODER), which strengthens boundary evidence under occlusion; the Hypergraph-State Spatial Fusion (HSSF) module, which integrates local structure enhancement, state-space contextual modeling, and high-order relation aggregation; and the Occlusion-Calibrated Detection Head (OCDetect), which suppresses low-quality Pre-NMS candidates and reduces false positives from occlusion boundaries and neighboring instances. Experiments on two classroom behavior detection datasets show that ODER-HSFNet outperforms mainstream YOLO-series methods, achieving 60.60%/80.12% mAP50:95/mAP50 on HCCB and 57.36%/74.65% on SCB-D3-S. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of the proposed design for highly crowded classroom behavior detection.

</details>


### [90] [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of VLMs for Zero-shot Procedural Mistake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21579)
*Serdar Ozsoy,Lars Doorenbos,Federico Spurio,Gianpiero Francesca,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ZeProM，一个统一的零样本程序性错误检测框架，利用单一预训练视频-语言模型同时完成错误检测和时间动作分割，在 EgoPER 和 CaptainCook4D 基准上接近甚至超越全监督方法，例如在 EgoPER 五个任务上平均 EDA 提升 4.4 点、F1@.5 提升 2.0 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序性错误检测方法依赖多阶段流水线，各模块需独立训练且需要任务特定的标注数据集，限制了泛化能力。为此，作者希望提出一个统一的零样本方法，摆脱对监督数据和复杂流水线的依赖。

Method: 提出 ZeProM（Zero-shot Procedural Mistake detection）框架，采用单个预训练视频-语言模型（VLM）统一完成程序性错误检测和时间动作分割，无需任何微调或任务特定训练，实现零样本推理。

Result: 在 EgoPER 和 CaptainCook4D 两个基准上评估，ZeProM 在零样本设定下接近甚至超越全监督方法。在 EgoPER 五个任务上，平均 EDA 提升 4.4 点，F1@.5 提升 2.0 点，优于最强监督方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明统一方法在程序性错误检测领域具有潜力，有望推动该领域从复杂多阶段流水线向更通用、更简洁的解决方案转变。

Abstract: Procedural mistake detection is important for quality control and user assistance across many disciplines. Recent work in this field has achieved significant gains by using the reasoning capabilities of Video-Language Models (VLMs) as components within multi-stage pipelines, which consist of separate modules for supervised temporal action segmentation, error detection, and explainability. Consequently, they remain dependent on tailored training datasets and require task-specific training, limiting their wider applicability. To remedy this, we introduce zero-shot procedural mistake detection and propose a unified Zero-shot Procedural Mistake detection (ZeProM) framework that jointly solves procedural mistake detection and temporal action segmentation with a single pre-trained VLM. By evaluating our framework on two canonical mistake detection benchmarks, EgoPER and CaptainCook4D, we find that ZeProM can perform these tasks successfully, while approaching, or even outperforming, the performance of fully supervised methods. For instance, we achieve a 4.4 point improvement in EDA and a 2.0 point improvement in F1@.5 on average over all five EgoPER tasks compared to the strongest supervised methods. Overall, our results show the potential of unified methods for procedural mistake detection, and we hope this will steer the field away from highly complex pipelines and toward more generally applicable solutions.

</details>


### [91] [Radial Basis Function Networks as Projection Heads in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21590)
*Andreas Schliebitz,Heiko Tapken,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用径向基函数网络（RBFN）替代自监督学习中的MLP投影头，并引入无需标签的质量度量指标SNS，证明RBFN投影头在多个SSL架构和数据集上竞争性优异，且SNS与线性探测指标强正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习中，常规做法是训练后丢弃MLP投影头，且需通过带标签数据的线性探测评估骨干网络质量，这一流程计算效率低且浪费已学习的信息。

Method: 用RBFN替换MLP投影头；提出Scale-Normalized Separation (SNS)无标签质量度量，利用RBF核中心和形状参数评估表征质量；在五种SSL架构和四个数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: RBFN投影头是MLP的竞争性替代品；推荐使用三层高斯径向基函数激活的RBF层；SNS与逻辑回归指标呈现强到极强的正相关。

Conclusion: 训练后的RBFN投影头可作为骨干网络表征质量的可靠代理，无需标签和单独分类器；同时发布基于Open Images V7的新PyTorch兼容数据集以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) typically relies on a backbone encoder followed by a small multilayer perceptron (MLP) projection head, which is conventionally discarded after training, while backbone quality is assessed via costly linear probing on labeled data. We argue that this approach including discarding the projector is rather computationally wasteful. Instead, we propose replacing the MLP head with a radial basis function network (RBFN), whose interpretable center and shape parameters can be exploited to judge representation quality without labels or a separate classifier. To this end, we introduce Scale-Normalized Separation (SNS), a novel label-free quality metric derived solely from the kernel centers and shapes learned during training. Across five canonical SSL architectures (MoCo, SimCLR, BYOL, SwAV and SimSiam) and four image classification datasets, we show that RBFN projection heads are competitive drop-in replacements for standard MLP projectors. We recommend constructing them with three RBF layers activated by the Gaussian radial basis function. Moreover, SNS exhibits strong to very strong positive correlation with established logistic regression metrics, demonstrating that a trained RBFN projector can act as a reliable proxy for backbone representation quality. We additionally publish a novel PyTorch compatible image classification dataset based on Google's Open Images V7 to facilitate reproducible research into representation learning.

</details>


### [92] [Boundary-by-Mask: Few-Shot Instance Segmentation with Mask-Conditioned Boundary Learning for Texture-Poor Industrial Parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21594)
*Yutaka Yoshinaga,Naoya Chiba,Koichi Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Boundary-by-Mask框架，利用基础模型编码器提取特征，通过轻量级SDF头预测边界感知距离图，在弱纹理工业场景实现少样本实例分割，支持通过替换掩码灵活定义分割目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例分割模型在工业场景中面临挑战：实例定义不一致、领域差距大（弱纹理、上下文线索有限），直接应用现有模型不可靠。

Method: 使用基础模型编码器提取丰富视觉特征，训练轻量级SDF头预测边界感知距离图，再通过SDF到掩码的重建过程获得分割掩码。仅需少量RGB图像和对应实例掩码作为输入。

Result: 在工业零件和边界模糊的食品项目上表现出强少样本泛化能力、特征贫乏条件下的鲁棒性，以及对掩码级别目标的精确控制。

Conclusion: 通过显式估计轮廓实现低纹理、颜色均匀表面上的可靠实例分离；通过替换掩码灵活指定分割目标（整体或子部分）；像素级浅层MLP头实现快速训练。

Abstract: Recent advances in large pre-trained models have led to remarkable progress in instance segmentation on general images. However, industrial scenarios remain challenging. Instance definitions are often application-specific and inconsistent, and the domain gap from general imagery is substantial due to weak textures and limited contextual cues. Consequently, a direct application of existing models is unreliable. We propose Boundary-by-Mask, a few-shot instance segmentation framework that supervises boundaries instead of interior appearance. Given a few RGB images and corresponding instance masks, the method extracts rich visual features using a foundation-model encoder and trains a lightweight Signed Distance Function (SDF) head to predict boundary-aware distance maps. Segmentation masks are obtained through an SDF-to-mask reconstruction process. By explicitly estimating contours, the framework achieves reliable instance separation even on low-texture and color-uniform surfaces. The instance definition is conditioned by the instance mask. Replacing the mask specifies the segmentation target, such as the whole object or a sub-part. A pixel-wise shallow MLP head enables rapid training. Experiments on industrial parts and food items with ambiguous boundaries show strong few-shot generalization, robustness in feature-poor conditions, and precise control over mask-level targets.

</details>


### [93] [$φ$-Scene: Physically Grounded Image-to-3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21596)
*Haodong Li,Lulu Shao,Haolin Lu,Yu Fu,Yen-Ru Chen,Seemandhar Jain,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ϕ-Scene 提出了一种基于物理的开放词汇、组合式单图像到3D场景重建方法，通过拓扑驱动的物理装配（拓扑排序、SDF穿透优化、刚体仿真）将场景视为稳定物理系统而非简单几何预测，在3D-Front数据集上达到最优性能，显著减少穿透伪影并提升物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D场景重建方法主要将重建视为视觉和几何预测问题，忽略了物理有效性，导致输出中存在浮动物体、相互穿透或不稳定接触等伪影，限制了在仿真、机器人和交互环境中的可用性。

Method: 提出 ϕ-Scene 方法，将重建视为拓扑驱动的物理装配过程：首先推断物体间的支撑关系并进行拓扑排序，然后按照拓扑顺序，通过SDF优化解决物体与已稳定支撑环境之间的穿透，最后使用刚体仿真在真实物理约束下将物体稳定为接触配置。

Result: 在3D-Front数据集上，ϕ-Scene在域外方法中表现最佳，与域内基线高度竞争；人工和VLM评估在视觉质量、参考对齐和物理合理性方面均偏好ϕ-Scene；专用物理合理性指标显示ϕ-Scene显著减少穿透伪影并大幅降低仿真后漂移量。

Conclusion: 将物理约束显式引入3D场景重建是提升结果有效性和实用性的关键途径，ϕ-Scene证明了拓扑排序与物理仿真结合能有效生成更稳定、物理合理的3D场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing compositional 3D scenes from a single image is a fundamental challenge in 3D world modeling. Recent methods can recover high-fidelity, complete 3D objects and predict plausible scene arrangements, but most still treat scene reconstruction primarily as a visual and geometric prediction problem. Their outputs may therefore contain floating objects, interpenetrations, or unstable-contact artifacts, limiting their physical validity and downstream usability in simulation, robotics, and interactive environments. We present $φ$-Scene, a physically grounded approach to open-vocabulary and compositional image-to-3D scene reconstruction. The key premise is that a reconstructed scene should not be treated merely as a set of objects with predicted poses, but as a stable physical system. Accordingly, $φ$-Scene formulates reconstruction as topology-driven physical assembly: it infers how objects support one another, orders them accordingly, and progressively settles each object against its already stabilized support context. For each object in topological order, SDF-based optimization first resolves penetrations against the pre-settled support context, and rigid-body simulation then settles the object into a stable contact configuration under real-world physical constraints. Experiments on 3D-Front show that $φ$-Scene achieves the strongest overall performance among out-of-domain methods and remains highly competitive with in-domain baselines. Human and VLM evaluations further show strong preference for $φ$-Scene in visual quality, reference alignment, and physical plausibility. Finally, dedicated physical plausibility metrics covering static contact quality and dynamic stability demonstrate that $φ$-Scene substantially reduces penetration artifacts while producing much lower post-simulation drift, indicating more stable and physically grounded 3D scenes.

</details>


### [94] [$μ$Match: Foundation Models for Semi-supervised Learning and Domain Adaptation in EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21605)
*Marei Freitag,Olesia Korchevaia,Luca Freckmann,Anwai Archit,Constantin Pape*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出μMatch框架，将视觉基础模型与半监督学习相结合，用于电子显微镜图像分割，在多个任务上显著超越基线，有望大幅减少标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子显微镜能够以纳米级分辨率分析细胞超微结构，但现有的基础模型在EM领域的应用局限于特定细胞器（如线粒体），主要因为EM分割任务多样且标注数据稀缺。目前EM分割仍依赖监督学习，需要大量人工标注，限制了超微结构分析的广泛应用。

Method: 提出μMatch框架，采用学生-教师（student-teacher）半监督学习方法，结合多种视觉基础模型（SAM、SAM2、μSAM、DINOv2/v3），在电子显微镜图像上进行半监督学习和域适应。

Result: 在多个挑战性EM分割任务（线粒体、细胞核、神经突分割）上，μMatch相比强基线方法取得了一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: μMatch框架证明了将基础模型与半监督学习结合可以有效降低EM分割对人工标注的依赖，为减少EM领域的标注工作量指明了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision foundation models have substantially advanced computer vision, enabling state-of-the-art performance in zero- and few-shot settings. They have been successfully applied to biomedical imaging tasks ranging from organ segmentation in computed tomography to cell segmentation in light microscopy. Electron microscopy (EM) is a central modality for analyzing cellular ultrastructure due to its nanometer-scale resolution. However, the application of foundation models in EM has so far been limited to specific organelles, such as mitochondria, largely due to the diversity of segmentation tasks and the scarcity of comprehensively annotated data. As a result, EM segmentation still predominantly relies on supervised learning, requiring extensive manual annotation and limiting ultrastructural analysis. To address this gap, we propose $μ$Match, a framework for semi-supervised learning and domain adaptation that leverages foundation models. We implement state-of-the-art student-teacher-based methods and evaluate multiple foundation models (SAM, SAM2, $μ$SAM, DINOv2/v3) on challenging EM tasks, including mitochondrion, nucleus, and neurite segmentation. Our results demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and highlight a path toward substantially reducing the annotation effort in EM.

</details>


### [95] [T-MOR: Learning Motion-Aware Skeleton Representations for Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21607)
*Di Yang,Mahmoud Ali,Quan Kong,Gianpiero Francesca,Francois Bremond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出T-MOR，一种从骨骼序列中学习可迁移动作表示的框架，利用视频和语言的多模态对比学习，在仅使用轻量级骨骼输入的情况下，在多个动作识别基准上取得一致性提升，并具备强大的少样本和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）主要依赖图像或视频的外观级监督，未能显式建模人体运动，而这正是细粒度、以人为中心的动作识别任务所必需的（因为动作由时间结构的、物理具身的身体运动定义）。

Method: 提出T-MOR框架，采用多模态对比学习对齐骨骼运动与视觉、文本表示，训练时使用视频、骨骼和文本三元组数据，推理时仅需轻量级骨骼输入；同时构建包含超过100万同步视频-骨骼-文本三元组的大规模预训练数据集PoseCap-1M。

Result: 在Toyota Smarthome, Penn Action, UAV-Human, TSU, Charades等多个数据集的动作分类和帧级时间检测任务上实现一致性性能提升，并在少样本和零样本设置下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 以运动为中心和具身化的表示方法对于可迁移的动作理解是有效的，T-MOR通过骨骼运动模态桥接视觉与语言，实现了高效且可泛化的动作识别。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP have recently achieved strong performance on a wide range of visual understanding tasks. However, most existing models rely primarily on appearance-level supervision from images or videos, and do not explicitly model human motion, which is essential for fine-grained and human-centric action recognition task as actions are defined by temporally structured and physically grounded body movements. To address this problem, we propose Transferable skeleton MOtion Representation (T-MOR), a motion-aware framework that learns transferable action representations from skeleton sequences with the aid of video and language supervision during training. T-MOR adopts a multi-modal contrastive learning scheme that aligns skeleton motion with visual and textual representations, while performing inference using only lightweight skeleton inputs. To support large-scale pre-training, we construct PoseCap-1M, a new dataset that contains over one million synchronized video, skeleton, and text triplets covering diverse human activities. We evaluate T-MOR on a range of human-centric action recognition benchmarks, including action classification and frame-wise temporal detection. Experimental results show that T-MOR consistently improves performance across multiple datasets, such as Toyota Smarthome, Penn Action, UAV-Human, TSU, and Charades. In addition, T-MOR demonstrates strong generalization ability in few-shot and zero-shot settings, highlighting the effectiveness of motion-centric and embodied representations for transferable action understanding.

</details>


### [96] [CurvSegFlow: Time-Conditioned Flow Matching for Robust Segmentation of Curvilinear Structures in Noisy Biomedical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21608)
*Sidi Mohamed Sid'El Moctar,Achraf Ait Laydi,Alexandre Beber,Marcus Braun,Zdenek Lansky,Yousef El Mourabit,Helene Bouvrais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CurvSegFlow提出了一种基于时间条件流匹配的渐进式分割框架，通过U-Net骨干网络、三重损失函数和时间嵌入，将分割建模为从噪声初始化到目标结构的动态细化过程，在多个显微图像和公开数据集上实现了优于现有模型的曲线结构分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像中曲线结构（如细胞骨架网络）因几何细长、拓扑复杂、信噪比低和密集交叉，现有分割方法常导致碎片化或不准确的结果。

Method: 提出CurvSegFlow框架，基于时间条件流匹配，将分割建模为通过学习的速度场从噪声初始化逐步细化到目标结构的动态过程；采用U-Net骨干网络、三重损失函数和时间嵌入来指导跨重建阶段的细化过程。

Result: 在多种合成和真实微管数据集、以及视网膜血管、角膜神经、冠状动脉等公开基准上，CurvSegFlow在精度和结构连续性上达到了竞争性或更优的表现，尤其在低信噪比条件下优势明显。

Conclusion: 基于流的迭代细化为曲线结构分割提供了一种鲁棒且通用的框架，能在挑战性成像条件下提升分割质量，并跨模态泛化而无需架构修改。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of curvilinear structures remains challenging in biomedical imaging due to their thin geometry, complex topology, and sensitivity to noise. This is particularly critical for microscopy images of cytoskeletal network, where low signal-to-noise ratios and dense filament crossings often lead to fragmented or inaccurate segmentation. In this work, we propose CurvSegFlow, a segmentation framework based on time-conditioned flow matching. Instead of predicting a segmentation mask in a single pass, the method models segmentation as a dynamic process that progressively refines a noisy initialization into the target structure through a learned velocity field. The proposed model combines a U-Net backbone with triple-term loss function and temporal embeddings to guide the refinement process across reconstruction stages. This formulation enables gradual error correction and improves the continuity of thin structures. CurvSegFlow is evaluated on multiple synthetic and real microtubule datasets, as well as on public benchmarks of retinal vessels, corneal nerves and coronary arteries. Across datasets, the method achieves competitive or superior performance compared to established segmentation models, with consistent improvements in precision and structural continuity, particularly under low signal-to-noise conditions. These results show that flow-based iterative refinement provides a robust and general framework for curvilinear structure segmentation. Overall, the proposed approach improves segmentation quality in challenging imaging conditions and generalizes effectively across modalities without architectural changes.

</details>


### [97] [Cross-Modal Corroboration for Annotation-Free Wildlife Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21613)
*Bharath Pillai,Varun Viswapriyan,Christopher Stewart,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Jenna Kline*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用专家知识中的物种活动模式作为无标注验证信号的多模态野生动物监测方法，通过视觉（BioCLIP 2+零样本检测）和声学（细粒度分类器）两个独立管道分别获取每小时活动曲线，并与已知行为先验进行三方一致性验证，以最小标注量实现对Milu鹿群活动规律的有效监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景下的野生动物监测面临严重的标注稀缺问题，亟需一种无需大量人工标注即可实现自动化验证的监测方案。

Method: 视觉管道采用BioCLIP 2进行零样本物种检测，结合切片推理处理受限相机视角，并通过几何方法进行地理定位；声学管道通过微调分类器检测物种发声；两者独立生成每小时活动曲线，并与文献行为先验进行三方一致性对齐验证。

Result: 在Milu鹿繁殖群的验证中，视觉和声学两个模态独立恢复的活动模式均与已知鹿科行为生态学一致，且所需人工标注极少。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于在视觉和声学模态中均可检测、且行为先验已有文献记载的物种，为大规模、自验证的野生动物监测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling wildlife monitoring for real-world conservation deployments requires automated analysis of smart sensors that operate under severe annotation scarcity. We propose leveraging expert knowledge of species activity patterns as an annotation-free validation signal for multimodal monitoring pipelines. We operationalize agreement as the alignment of independently derived hourly activity curves both with each other and with published behavioral priors-a three-way convergence that rules out shared-data confounds and dataset-internal correlation as alternative explanations. Our vision pipeline combines zero-shot species detection via BioCLIP 2, sliced inference to handle deployment-constrained camera positioning, and geometry-based geographic localization from camera trap imagery. Our acoustic pipeline detects species vocalizations via a fine-tuned classifier. We validate the pipeline on a breeding herd of Milu deer and demonstrate that both modalities independently recover activity patterns consistent with known deer behavioral ecology with minimal manual annotation. The framework applies to species detectable in both visual and acoustic modalities for which behavioral priors are documented in the literature, suggesting a practical path toward self-validating wildlife-monitoring pipelines at conservation scale.

</details>


### [98] [A DVDrive Approach for doScenes Instructed Driving Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21623)
*Zijian Fu,Xiangyang Chu,Mengshi Qi,Huadong Ma,Guanghao Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于OmniDrive的指令条件轨迹预测方法，通过引入DVPE风格的分域视觉感知模块，在doScenes挑战中生成6秒未来轨迹，有效减少了跨视角干扰并提升了语言指令与局部视觉信息的对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中需要根据自然语言指令（如“向左变道”）预测未来轨迹，现有方法在处理多视角视觉信息时存在跨视角干扰，导致语言指令与相关视觉证据对齐不足。

Method: 基于OmniDrive视觉-语言-动作驾驶代理，在nuScenes指令标注数据上训练；创新点在于引入DVPE风格的分域视角感知模块，将查询特征与图像令牌分组到局部视角空间，在各视角内进行可见性感知的交叉注意力计算，替代全局跨视角注意力。

Result: 在doScenes Instructed Driving Challenge中提交了该方法，生成了由12个未来航点表示的6秒自我车辆轨迹。

Conclusion: 分域视角感知设计减少了无关的跨视角干扰，增强了模型将语言指令与局部驾驶相关视觉证据对齐的能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Instruction-conditioned trajectory prediction is an emerging problem in autonomous driving, where a model predicts the future ego trajectory not only from visual scene context and historical motion, but also from a natural-language maneuver instruction. This paper presents our submission to the doScenes Instructed Driving Challenge, built upon OmniDrive, a vision-language-action driving agent with 3D perception, reasoning, and planning capabilities. We adapt OmniDrive to the doScenes setting by training it on instruction-annotated nuScenes scenes and generating a 6-second ego trajectory represented by 12 future waypoints. To improve multi-view visual grounding, we further introduce a DVPE-style divided-view perception module into the OmniDrive perception head. Instead of attending globally to all camera features, the proposed module groups query features and image tokens into divided local view spaces and performs visibility-aware cross-attention within each view. This design reduces irrelevant cross-view interference and helps the model better align language instructions with local driving-relevant visual evidence. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/feel12348/doscenes-omnidrive.

</details>


### [99] [Chehre: An Emoji-Prompted Video Dataset for Perceptually Diverse Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21657)
*Bita Azari,Zoe Stanley,Avneet Batra,Poorvi Bhatia,Hali Kil,Manolis Savva,Angelica Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Chehre是一个由表情符号驱动的面部表情视频数据集，包含2111个高质量视频（203名表演者，902名标注者验证），覆盖40种表情。通过将面部运动迁移到合成面部保护隐私，并定义了主导表情识别和分布表情识别两个基准任务。评估显示现有视觉-语言模型性能远低于人类水平（最高Top-1准确率仅32.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部表情识别数据集多聚焦于静态图像、基本情绪类别或单一确定性标注，缺乏对动态面部表情以及个体间感知多样性的探索。

Method: 1) 以40种面部表情符号提示参与者录制动态面部视频；2) 将面部运动迁移到合成面部以保护隐私；3) 由独立标注者使用表情符号和标签进行多轮标注；4) 定义主导表情识别和分布表情识别两项基准任务；5) 采用随机采样和角色提示方法评估视觉-语言模型的多预测能力。

Result: 最佳模型在主导表情识别任务上Top-1准确率仅32.5%；在分布表情识别任务上Spread Ratio远低于人类参考值，表明两项任务均具有挑战性。

Conclusion: Chehre为评估多样性、动态性和分布性的面部表情识别提供了新基准，揭示了现有模型在捕捉人类感知多样性方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Facial expressions are nonverbal social signals used in human interaction, but facial expression recognition datasets often focus on static images, basic emotion categories, or single deterministic annotations. We introduce Chehre, an emoji-prompted video dataset for analyzing dynamic facial expressions across a wide range of expressions for exploring inter-individual perceptual diversity. In Chehre, participants were prompted to express and record 40 facial emojis. Later, their facial motions were transferred onto synthetic faces to preserve privacy. A separate group of annotators analyzed the anonymized videos using emoji and label annotations, resulting in 2,111 high quality videos collected from 203 performers and validated by 902 annotators. We define two benchmark tasks: dominant expression recognition, which tests whether models recover the top human-rated labels, and distributional expression recognition, which tests whether models capture the diversity of human responses. We benchmark recent vision-language models using random sampling and persona prompting to generate multiple predictions per video. Results show that both tasks are challenging: among the models evaluated, the best-performing model achieves only 32.5% Top-1 accuracy on dominant expression recognition and a Spread Ratio well below the human reference on distributional recognition. Chehre provides a benchmark for evaluating diverse, dynamic, and distributional facial expression recognition

</details>


### [100] [UnityShots: Memory-Driven Multi-Shot Audio-Video Generation with Boundary-Aware Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21661)
*Jiehui Huang,Yuechen Zhang,Bin Xia,Jiahao Wang,Xu He,Zhenchao Tang,Meng Chu,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnityShots是一种基于LTX-2.3的记忆驱动多镜头音视频生成系统，通过固定大小的长期记忆（LTM）和短期记忆（STM）槽、边界条件门控融合、参考说话人音频标记以及离散切分类型先验（AdaLN），实现了跨镜头的主体外观、场景上下文和说话人身份的一致性，在多个基准上超越了开源方法并媲美最强闭源系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么在固定长度序列上端到端训练无法扩展，要么逐镜头生成时记忆库线性增长，要么在LLM规划器下编排预训练生成器但缺乏多镜头感知的主干网络，无法实现真正的跨镜头记忆保持。

Method: 基于LTX-2.3，视频流维护两个固定大小的记忆槽：LTM锚定开场镜头、STM保持前一镜头尾部，每个切分处通过边界条件门控融合视觉切分概率和节拍追踪信号进行更新；音频流通过参考说话人标记注入保持音色；离散切分类型先验通过AdaLN学习，作为推理时控制过渡强度的旋钮。

Result: 在I2V、T2V和R2V三种条件下，UnityShots在所有跨镜头一致性指标上领先开源基线，并在多镜头维度上匹配最强的闭源系统。同时发布了包含200个跨文化多镜头序列的基准数据集。

Conclusion: 固定大小的记忆槽机制能够有效解决多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头一致性问题，既避免了记忆线性增长的扩展瓶颈，又提供了显式的过渡控制能力。

Abstract: Generating a coherent multi-shot video requires structured cross-shot memory. Subject appearance, scene context, and speaker identity must persist across cuts. Existing approaches either train end-to-end over fixed-length sequences and cannot scale, generate shot-by-shot with memory banks that grow linearly, or orchestrate pretrained generators under an LLM planner without a multi-shot-aware backbone. We present UnityShots, a memory-driven multi-shot audio-video generation system built on LTX-2.3, trained on annotated cinematic and music-video shots. The video stream maintains two fixed-size slots, a long-term memory (LTM) slot anchored to the opening shot and a short-term memory (STM) slot holding the immediately preceding tail, both updated at every cut by a boundary-conditioned gate that fuses visual cut probability and beat-tracker signals. The audio stream injects a reference speaker token at every shot to preserve vocal timbre without a sliding audio bank. A discrete cut-type prior, learned through AdaLN, becomes an inference-time control knob over transition strength. We release a benchmark of $200$ multi-cultural multi-shot sequences spanning six ethnic regions and ten or more languages, with per-shot reference identities, reference audio, and per-boundary transition labels. Evaluated across I2V, T2V, and R2V conditioning modes, UnityShots leads open-source baselines on every cross-shot coherence metric and matches the strongest closed-source system on the multi-shot axes.

</details>


### [101] [Enlight: Fast Low-Light Image Enhancement via Multi-Objective Optimization and Shadow-Aware Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21674)
*Nirjhor Datta,M. Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ENLIGHT是一种无需训练的低光照图像增强框架，通过推理时直接优化感知目标实现零样本增强，采用全局到局部的两阶段优化策略（全局光照调整+阴影感知局部优化），并提供Fast和Ultrafast两种模式以平衡质量与效率。实验表明其在多个数据集上取得有竞争力的感知质量，且推理时间显著低于深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法依赖大规模训练数据和监督信号，且推理过程缺乏可解释性，因此作者希望设计一种无需训练、零样本、可解释的低光照图像增强方法，在提升可见度的同时保持结构一致性和控制噪声放大。

Method: 两阶段全局到局部优化策略：(1)全局光照调整——通过多目标优化（熵、梯度保持、噪声正则化）提升整体可见度，保持结构一致性；(2)阴影感知局部优化——通过掩码选择性增强低强度区域，避免过度曝光。提供Fast（完整多目标）和Ultrafast（轻量近似）两种模式，支持进化算法和局部搜索等多种优化器。

Result: 在BAID、Backlit300、LIME、MEF、NPE、DICM六个数据集上，ENLIGHT在MUSIQ、NIQE、BRISQUE等感知质量指标上达到具有竞争力的性能，且推理时间显著低于深度学习方法。定性结果展示了对比度提升、结构细节保持和受控的噪声放大。

Conclusion: ENLIGHT作为一种无需训练、零样本的低光照图像增强框架，以显著更低的推理时间实现了与学习方法相当的感知质量，是一种实用且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: We present ENLIGHT, a fast and training free framework for low-light image enhancement based on direct optimization of a perceptual objective. Unlike deep learning approaches that require large scale training data and supervision, ENLIGHT operates in a zero-shot manner by optimizing image quality at inference time. The method employs a two stage global to local optimization strategy. In the first stage, ENLIGHT performs global illumination adjustment to improve visibility while maintaining structural consistency and avoiding excessive noise enhancement. In the second stage, a shadow aware refinement selectively improves low-intensity regions through masked local optimization, enhancing visibility without overexposure. To balance quality and efficiency, we introduce two modes: Fast, which uses a multi-objective formulation combining entropy, gradient preservation, and noise regularization, and Ultrafast, which reduces computational cost via a lightweight approximation of the same objective. The framework is optimizer agnostic and supports both evolutionary and lightweight local search methods. Experiments on BAID, Backlit300, LIME, MEF, NPE, and DICM demonstrate that ENLIGHT achieves competitive perceptual quality (MUSIQ, NIQE, BRISQUE) with significantly lower inference time. Qualitative results further show improved contrast, preserved structural details, and controlled noise amplification, making ENLIGHT a practical and interpretable alternative to learning based methods.

</details>


### [102] [VT-DUDA: Visual Token Conditioning for Diffusion-guided Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21700)
*Xuan Qi,Daniele Berardini,Dario Serez,Vito Paolo Pastore,Vittorio Murino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-DUDA，一种基于视觉令牌条件的扩散引导无监督域适应方法，通过将源图像映射为视觉令牌序列并与文本嵌入拼接作为条件，生成更准确的带标签目标风格图像，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的UDA方法依赖文本提示或域适配模块控制生成，条件设计不够精细，导致目标风格合成不够充分，影响下游分类性能。

Method: 将源图像映射为紧凑的视觉令牌序列，与对应文本嵌入沿交叉注意力上下文维度拼接，形成混合条件上下文输入到潜在扩散模型中，同时使用目标域适配器分支进行合成，支持推理时通过令牌选择和强度调整操纵条件信号。

Result: 在Office-31、Office-Home和VisDA-2017数据集上，VT-DUDA在平均目标域准确率上优于强判别式和基于扩散的UDA基线方法。

Conclusion: 在基于生成的UDA中，更强的条件接口能够提高合成目标风格数据对下游任务的有用性。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) aims to learn a target-domain classifier from labeled source data and unlabeled target data under distribution shift. Recent diffusion-based UDA methods approach this problem by synthesizing labeled target-style images and training on the resulting synthetic data. However, their performance depends heavily on the conditioning design: class prompts provide only coarse guidance, while domain adaptation modules mainly control appearance, which may leave target-style synthesis insufficiently specified. We propose VT-DUDA, a visual-token conditioning framework for diffusion-guided UDA. Instead of relying only on text prompts, VT-DUDA uses source images to provide additional instance-level visual context for target-style synthesis. Specifically, VT-DUDA maps each source image to a compact sequence of visual tokens and forms a hybrid conditioning context by concatenating these tokens with the corresponding text embeddings along the cross-attention context dimension of a latent diffusion model. This provides instance-dependent conditioning beyond text alone, while synthesis is performed with the target-domain adapter branch. Because guidance is represented explicitly as a token sequence, the same interface also permits inference-time manipulation of the conditioning signal through token selection and token-strength adjustment. The proposed method preserves the standard diffusion objective and can be integrated into existing adapter-based diffusion frameworks without modifying the backbone. Across Office-31, Office-Home, and VisDA-2017, VT-DUDA improves average target-domain accuracy over strong discriminative and diffusion-based UDA baselines. The results suggest that, in generation-based UDA, a stronger conditioning interface can improve the downstream usefulness of synthetic target-style data.

</details>


### [103] [Structural Assessment for Understanding and Guiding Dataset Distillation in Discrete Token Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21705)
*Yue Cao,Jianyang Gu,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev,Yu Hu,Jozsef Hamari,Zheng Liu,Mohsen Zardadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过离散视觉分词器的视角研究数据集蒸馏的有效性，发现token组成的平衡性（结构分数）比全局分布匹配更能影响蒸馏数据集的性能，并利用高结构分数样本指导扩散模型蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据集蒸馏方法虽能降低训练成本，但不同蒸馏数据集效果差异的原因尚未被充分理解，缺乏评估蒸馏数据集有效性的原则性指标。

Method: 利用离散视觉分词器提供的有限词汇表进行token级统计分析，提出结构分数（structural score）来衡量token组成的充分性；通过量化分析验证平衡token组成与验证性能的正相关性。

Result: 平衡的token组成能带来更高的验证性能；与原始数据的分布偏离不一定损害性能；高结构分数的样本可有效指导扩散模型的数据集蒸馏。

Conclusion: token组成是影响数据集有效性的关键因素，为数据集蒸馏提供了超越分布相似性考量的原则性补充视角。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) has proven to reduce training cost while preserving accuracy. While promising, the factors that make one distilled dataset more effective than another remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate this question through the lens of discrete visual tokenizers. Whereas many prior DD efforts emphasize matching global data distributions, we suggest that the effectiveness depends on which semantic concepts are captured and how they are composed. Discrete visual tokenizers provide a finite vocabulary that enables direct statistical analysis of such compositional structure. Through quantitative analysis of token-level statistics, we introduce the structural score to measure the adequacy of token compositions. We observe that distilled datasets with balanced token composition yield higher validation performance. On the other hand, divergence from the original data does not necessarily harm performance. We further show that samples with high structural scores in the discrete token space can effectively guide diffusion-based DD. Our findings highlight the importance of token composition in dataset effectiveness, offering a principled complement to distributional similarity considerations in DD.

</details>


### [104] [HPP: Hierarchical Programmatic Probing for Long Video Understanding by Decoupling Perception and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21734)
*Awais Rauf,Ahmed Hasssan,Greg Slabaugh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化程序化探测（HPP）框架，通过将长视频理解解耦为迭代式程序化探索，将语义感知与高阶时序推理分离，在长视频理解任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将感知与多步推理耦合在单次前向传播中，受限于LLM在隐空间中发现和执行多步策略的有限能力，成为长视频理解的瓶颈。

Method: HPP框架：(1) 将长视频理解重构为对层次化分割视频的迭代程序化探索；(2) 编码能力的LLM在交互式编码环境中规划并执行多步策略，按需调用VLM进行局部感知；(3) 引入信息密度感知的层次化分割、延迟交互语义检索和结构化探测函数实现由粗到细的时序定位。

Result: 在LongVideoBench上验证了解耦策略的有效性，并在EgoSchema、VideoMME和MLVU等多个长视频基准上展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 将感知与推理解耦为迭代程序化探测能有效突破VLM在长视频理解中的能力瓶颈，该框架在多种长视频基准上均表现优异。

Abstract: Understanding long videos requires fine-grained perception and multi-step, higher-order reasoning over complex, long-range spatio-temporal dynamics. Vision-language models (VLMs) encode video frames into visual tokens and attempt to perform both perception and multi-step planning latently, within a single forward pass. This coupled formulation, however, is bottlenecked by the LLM's limited capacity to discover and execute multi-step strategies in its latent representations. To address this bottleneck, we propose Hierarchical Programmatic Probing (HPP), a framework that decouples semantic perception from higher-order temporal reasoning by reformulating long video understanding as iterative, programmatic exploration of a hierarchically segmented video. Specifically, a coding-capable LLM plans and executes a multi-step strategy in an interactive coding environment, probing the video for information and invoking a VLM for localized perception on demand. To make probing tractable over long videos, we introduce three components: information-density-aware hierarchical segmentation, late-interaction semantic retrieval, and structured probing functions for coarse-to-fine temporal localization. We validate HPP on LongVideoBench, which requires both fine-grained perception and long-range relational reasoning, and show that decoupling the two via iterative programmatic probing yields substantial gains. Further results on EgoSchema, VideoMME, and MLVU demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach across diverse long-video benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Adversarial Domain Prompt Tuning and Generation for Single Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21736)
*Zhipeng Xu,De Cheng,Xinyang Jiang,Nannan Wang,Dongsheng Li,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出利用预训练文本到图像基础模型为单域泛化生成训练数据，并设计了一种渐进式对抗提示调优框架（PAPT），通过学习两组抽象提示（域不变类别信息和域特定风格）作为扩散模型的条件，以对抗学习机制生成多样域风格的图像，实验证明该方法优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单域泛化（SDG）中，利用数据增强或图像生成来产生域外训练数据是重要方向，但手动设计文本提示为所有可能的域生成图像不切实际，且某些域特征过于抽象难以用文字描述。

Method: 提出渐进式对抗提示调优（PAPT）框架，学习两组抽象提示：一组捕获域不变类别信息，另一组建模域特定风格；通过对抗学习机制使预训练扩散模型生成保持类别特征的各种域风格图像。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法有效，性能优于最先进的单域泛化方法。

Conclusion: PAPT框架有效解决了利用T2I模型为SDG生成训练数据时提示设计困难的问题，通过可学习的抽象提示和对抗机制实现了高质量域外数据生成，提升了模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Single domain generalization (SDG) aims to learn a robust model, which could perform well on many unseen domains while there is only one single domain available for training. One of the promising directions for achieving single-domain generalization is to generate out-of-domain (OOD) training data through data augmentation or image generation. Given the rapid advancements in AI-generated content (AIGC), this paper is the first to propose leveraging powerful pre-trained text-to-image (T2I) foundation models to create the training data. However, manually designing textual prompts to generate images for all possible domains is often impractical, and some domain characteristics may be too abstract to describe with words. To address these challenges, we propose a novel Progressive Adversarial Prompt Tuning (PAPT) framework for pre-trained diffusion models. Instead of relying on static textual domains, our approach learns two sets of abstract prompts as conditions for the diffusion model: one that captures domain-invariant category information and another that models domain-specific styles. This adversarial learning mechanism enables the T2I model to generate images in various domain styles while preserving key categorical features. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving superior performances to state-of-the-art single-domain generalization approaches.

</details>


### [106] [Quantile Adaptive Temperature Scaling for Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21749)
*Omprakash Chakraborty,Leo Fillioux,Ismail Ben Ayed,Jose Dolz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出量化自适应温度缩放（QaTS），通过将置信度映射到分位数空间并设计单调温度函数，实现了对不同置信度区域的自适应后处理校准，在多种场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准温度缩放（TS）采用全局统一的重缩放方式，无法纠正跨置信度谱的高度异质性误校准结构，尤其是在低置信度预测中（不确定性最关键的区域），正确性与置信度之间的偏差最大，而TS对此几乎无能为力。

Method: QaTS将预测置信度映射到经验分位数空间，使误校准结构显式化，并设计了一个单调温度函数，使其能够根据不同分位数区域自适应调整温度，同时保持高置信度预测的校准状态不变。该方法与重参数化的期望校准误差（ECE）目标自然对齐，产生样本级别的温度。

Result: 在多种数据集、架构、评估场景和任务中，QaTS在各类后处理校准方法中持续且显著地优于现有最先进方法，能够提供更可靠和可信的置信度估计，且不改变模型预测本身。

Conclusion: QaTS通过分位数感知的自适应温度缩放，有效解决了全局温度缩放对异质性误校准的局限，提升了校准的鲁棒性，尤其在类别不平衡和分布偏移等挑战性场景下表现优异。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often produce poorly calibrated confidence estimates, overstating their certainty even when predictions are incorrect.
