Method: 提出DiffNet架构：(1) 在每级骨干输出后应用轻量级多级差异变换，用学习到的零和滤波器的幅度响应替换原始特征，使解码器聚合多尺度的不一致性证据而非内容密集型激活；(2) 设计高效的DCT域骨干，采用频域索引感知的DCT-量化联合嵌入。

Result: 在跨域和人工文档篡改定位任务上实现SOTA，相比先前方法性能提升约30%，吞吐量较先前最佳模型提升最高7倍。

Conclusion: 通过改进DCT骨干设计和早期融合策略，DiffNet有效缩小了跨域性能差距，验证了多级差异变换和频域量化嵌入在文档篡改定位中的有效性。

Abstract: Localizing document tampering is extremely challenging, as manipulations are crafted to appear visually consistent and often leave only subtle traces that are nearly invisible to the human eye. In prior work, evaluation has been largely dominated by synthetic benchmarks that closely match the training distribution, and methods have shown steady progress under this setting. However, these gains often translate poorly to human-made forgeries and to cross-domain evaluation, where both the source documents and the tampering pipeline can change, leading to a distribution shift. In addition, since the introduction of the Frequency Perception Head for the discrete cosine transform (DCT) modality, it has become a standard choice, and subsequent work has largely focused on downstream modules and fusion strategies rather than revisiting the backbone itself. To help close this gap in cross-domain performance and improve the DCT backbone design, we propose \textbf{DiffNet}, a relatively simple yet effective RGB--DCT early-fusion architecture driven by two key design choices. First, to ensure that the decoder aggregates multi-scale inconsistency evidence rather than operating on raw, content-heavy activations, we apply a lightweight multi-level discrepancy transformation at the output of each backbone stage, replacing features with magnitude-only responses to learned zero-sum filters. Second, we design an efficient DCT-domain backbone that relies on a lightweight frequency-index-aware DCT--quantization joint embedding. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on cross-domain and human-made document tampering localization, outperforming prior methods by around 30\%, with up to $7\times$ higher throughput than the previous best model.

</details>


### [142] [Towards Accurate and Robust Surveillance Roadside IVD via Trackletized Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22299)
*Xiwen Li,Xiaoya Tang,Bodong Zhang,Tolga Tasdizen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TAVR-IVD，一种基于多目标跟踪的视听融合框架，用于检测视频中发动机空转的静止车辆，通过逐车辆轨迹分类替代全局融合，提升跨域鲁棒性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全图像、片段级融合方法存在过拟合场景背景、时序决策不稳定、缺乏显式空间先验对齐车辆与麦克风等问题，导致域迁移下鲁棒性差且数据效率低。

Method: TAVR-IVD框架：先检测车辆，将检测结果链接成轨迹（tracklet），再对每条轨迹独立分类，判断其是否为空转车辆。该方法利用轨迹提升信噪比、稳定时序决策、提供显式空间先验实现车辆与音频对齐，且检测器无关、计算高效。

Result: 在AVIVD-LT（跨天）和AVIVD-M（跨站点）两个扩展评测集上验证了部署鲁棒性，但摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹的逐车辆分类范式能够有效提升IVD任务在域迁移场景下的适应能力，同时保持检测器无关性和计算效率。

Abstract: Idling Vehicle Detection (IVD) seeks to determine, at the final frame of a video clip, whether any vehicle is idling, meaning the vehicle is stationary with its engine running, using synchronized video from a remote surveillance camera and multichannel audio captured by spatially distributed wireless microphones along the roadside. Prior full-image, clip-level fusion approaches tend to overfit scene background and full-frame context, produce unstable temporal decisions, and lack an explicit spatial prior to align vehicles with microphones, which makes them brittle under domain shift and data inefficient. Instead, we introduce TAVR-IVD, an audio-visual framework guided by multi-object tracking. Our method detects vehicles, links detections into tracklets, and classifies each vehicle by operating on its tracklet. This design raises the effective signal-to-noise ratio, stabilizes temporal decisions through tracklets, enforces an explicit spatial prior to align vehicles with microphones, and adapts across domains with limited calibration annotations while remaining detector agnostic and efficient. To evaluate deployment robustness, we further curate two evaluation extensions, AVIVD-LT and AVIVD-M, covering inter-day and cross-site shifts.

</details>


### [143] [T-IMPACT: A Severity-Aware Benchmark for Contextual Image-Text Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22339)
*Gagandeep Singh,Aaditya Yadav,Priyanka Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: T-IMPACT是一个首个发布的面向新闻风格图文对操控的严重性感知基准数据集，包含98,786个样本，覆盖原始、仅图像、仅文本及联合操控四种类型，提供校准的连续严重性分数、粗略的低/中/高标签及支撑性定位元数据。实验表明现有模型能捕捉部分真实性信号，但严重性预测仍远未达到与人类判断一致的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集主要关注真实性、上下文不匹配或操控类型，很少量化编辑行为对帖子（图文对）解读意图的改变程度，缺乏对操控严重性的细粒度评估能力。

Method: 从新闻图文对出发，提取语义锚点并进行空间定位，执行局部图像编辑和受约束的标题改写，利用有限的人工评分校准上下文影响分数，构建了包含连续严重性分数和粗略等级标签的数据集。

Result: 现有模型能够恢复部分真实性信号，但严重性预测任务显著更困难，与人类判断的对齐程度较弱。

Conclusion: T-IMPACT为超越二元真/假分类、迈向分级上下文影响的多模态操控研究提供了初步基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models and generative editing systems have made it increasingly easy to produce persuasive multimodal misinformation by altering images, text, or both jointly. However, existing datasets focus mainly on authenticity, out-of-context mismatch, or manipulation type, and rarely capture how strongly an edit changes the likely interpretation of a post. We introduce T-IMPACT, a first-release severity-aware benchmark for manipulated news-style image-text pairs. T-IMPACT contains 98,786 examples spanning pristine, image-only, text-only, and joint manipulations, with a calibrated continuous severity signal, coarse low/medium/high labels, and supporting grounding metadata. Starting from a news image-text pair, the pipeline extracts semantic anchors, grounds them spatially, performs localized image edits and constrained caption rewrites, and calibrates contextual-impact scores using limited human ratings. In this release, the calibrated continuous score is the primary severity target, while the low/medium/high bands should be interpreted as coarse operating buckets rather than balanced classes. Experiments show that current models recover some authenticity signal, but severity prediction remains substantially harder and only weakly aligned with human judgment. T-IMPACT provides an initial benchmark for studying multimodal manipulation beyond binary real/fake classification toward graded contextual impact.

</details>


### [144] [Customizing Video Portraits via Identity-ActionDecoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22347)
*Junxiong Lin,Haoran Wang,Xinji Mai,Zeng Tao,Xuan Tong,Ivy Pan,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出身份-动作解耦(IaD)框架，通过身份解耦损失和文本对齐损失，在不进行主体特定微调的情况下，实现保留身份一致性的文本到视频生成，解决现有方法因忽略面部嵌入中与身份无关信息而导致的面部运动单调或不准确问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPT2V方法（如ID-Animator和ConsimID）仅在推理时注入身份特征，忽略了面部嵌入中包含的与身份无关的信息，导致面部运动单调、不准确，且难以遵循文本提示。

Method: 提出身份-动作解耦(IaD)框架，包含两种损失函数：身份解耦损失(Identity Decoupling Loss)和文本对齐损失(Text Alignment Loss)。

Result: 无需任何主体特定微调，生成的视频能够保持跨时间维度的身份一致性，并展现丰富、可控的表情和场景变化，与输入文本紧密匹配。

Conclusion: IaD框架有效解决了身份信息与动作信息的耦合问题，在无需微调的情况下实现了更好的身份保留和文本引导的面部动态控制。

Abstract: Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation (IPT2V) seeks to synthesize a temporally coherent video from a reference image and a textual description, while simultaneously preserving the subject's identity and allowing fine-grained control over facial dynamics. Although recent methods such as ID-Animator and ConsisID inject identity features only at inference time, they ignored the ID-irrelevant information contained in Facial embedding, leading to monotonous or inaccurate facial movements that poorly follow the prompt. We introduce Identity-Action Decoupling (IaD) framework as well as two loss function Identity Decoupling Loss and Text Alignment Loss to solve this problem. Without any subject-specific fine-tuning, IaD yields videos that (1) maintain cross-temporal identity consistency and (2) exhibit rich, controllable expressions and scene variations that closely match the input text.

</details>


### [145] [Interest Entanglement: The Hidden Barrier to Blind Super-Resolution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22353)
*Junxiong Lin,Xinji Mai,Qianyu Guo,Haoran Wang,Zeng Tao,Xuan Tong,Ivy Pan,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出共享特征表示的超分辨率框架（SFR），通过解耦回归目标和感知目标的学习过程，并设计InfoSqueeze模块过滤冗余信息，有效平衡了图像超分任务中的保真度与感知质量之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超分方法通过调整权重参数来平衡保真度和感知质量，但忽略了回归目标与感知目标之间的兴趣纠缠问题，导致难以真正实现两者的有效平衡。

Method: 提出SFR框架，通过解耦不同优化目标的学习过程，探索共同的优化方向；同时提出InfoSqueeze模块，利用降维和扩展过程过滤冗余信息，将特征转换为一致空间。

Result: 在五个代表性数据集上的定量和定性实验均证实了SFR的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦优化目标并利用共享特征表示，SFR能够有效平衡回归损失和感知损失之间的频域冲突，实现更好的超分效果。

Abstract: Fidelity and perceptual quality are two inherently competing and conflicting objectives in the image super-resolution (SR) task. Different loss functions focus on these objectives to varying extents. Regression losses enhance the model's fidelity but lack sufficient attention to high-frequency details, resulting in a loss of fine details. In contrast, perception losses improve the model's visual quality but may introduce undesirable artifacts. Balancing these two optimization goals can be viewed as a Multi-Objective Optimization problem. Existing methods are limited to cautiously adjusting weight parameters between these losses, overlooking the underlying Interest Entanglement problem. To address this problem, we explore the inherent frequency-domain conflict between the regression objective and the perceptual objective, and analyze the causes of Interest Entanglement in SR tasks. According to our findings, we propose the Shared-Feature-Representation based Super-Resolution framework (SFR), which decouples the learning process of different optimization objectives, allowing the model to explore a common optimization direction for both goals and achieve an effective balance between them. To better leverage shared features, we also proposed the InfoSqueeze module, which filters redundant information through a dimensionality reduction and expansion process, effectively transforming features into a consistent space. Quantitative and qualitative experiments across five representative datasets affirm the superiority of SFR.

</details>


### [146] [Towards Error-Free Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22370)
*Shuning Chang,Weihua Chen,Jiasheng Tang,Hao Xu,Zeyu Zhang,Hangjie Yuan,Yu Lu,Ruigang Niu,Fan Wang,Bohan Zhuang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无限长度视频生成框架，通过因果注意力机制和KV缓存等技术，实现了高质量、动态且身份一致的单镜头长视频生成，有效解决误差累积和属性漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法在生成长视频时面临误差累积、属性漂移以及长视频数据稀缺等挑战，亟需一种能够生成高质量、动态且身份一致的单镜头长视频的框架。

Method: 1) 在大规模短视频数据上微调扩散模型作为视频扩展模型，自回归生成时序连贯的片段；2) 受大语言模型启发，采用片段间因果注意力机制（片段内双向注意力、片段间单向注意力）在长视频数据上进一步微调；3) 引入KV缓存机制以维持恒定内存开销；4) 提出截断整流流（T-RFlow）技术抑制误差累积。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在生成长视频时效果显著，在逼真且连贯的分钟级视频合成方面建立了新基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效解决了长视频生成中的误差累积和属性漂移问题，实现了高质量、动态且身份一致的单镜头长视频生成。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation have made minute-level synthesis possible; however, generating long videos remains challenging due to error accumulation, attribute drift, and the limited availability of long video data. In this paper, we introduce an infinite-length video generation framework that focusing on addressing these issues and produces high-quality, dynamic, and identity-consistent single-shot long videos. We first finetune a diffusion model as a video extension model on large-scale short video data to autoregressively generate temporally coherent clips. Inspired by the success of large language models (LLMs), we adopt causal attention computation between clips to further finetune this model on long video data. In this way, the tokens in one clip (short video) are computed by bidirectional attention while tokens among clips are computed by unidirectional attention. This design leverages the strengths of modern diffusion models while preserving long-term context information, effectively mitigating error accumulation and attribute drift. To achieve memory efficiency during inference, we adopt a key-value (KV) caching mechanism to maintain a constant KV memory. Furthermore, we introduce truncation-rectified flow (T-RFlow) technique to further suppress error accumulation. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Our framework establishes a new benchmark for realistic and coherent minute-level video synthesis.

</details>


### [147] [Following the Flow: Advection-Consistent Modeling for Event-based Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22378)
*Wen Guo,Fulong Cai,Wuzhou Quan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACT框架，通过物理引导的平流一致性建模解决事件相机小目标检测中因稀疏异步测量和噪声导致的时序碎片化问题，在基准数据集上IoU提升20.72%，准确率提升15.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机小目标检测面临三个关键难题：稀疏异步测量、微弱目标响应易被噪声干扰、有限空间支撑导致信号时序连续性丧失，造成预测碎片化不稳定。

Method: 提出PACT（物理引导平流一致性建模）框架，将事件演化建模为运动驱动的特征传输过程，沿估计速度场传播特征并通过平流约束强制执行轨迹级一致性，集成运动感知特征提取与可微平流传输算子。

Result: 在基准事件数据集上持续超越SOTA方法，IoU提升20.72%，准确率提升15.03%，同时保持可比的计算效率。

Conclusion: 平流一致性建模能有效保持弱事件响应随时间的延续性，防止其在复杂背景干扰下退化，为事件相机小目标检测提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Event cameras enable high-frequency visual perception with microsecond latency, offering advantages for dynamic scenes. However, event-based small object detection remains challenging due to sparse asynchronous measurements and weak object responses that are easily disrupted by noise. Limited spatial support causes small-object signals to lose temporal continuity, resulting in fragmented and unstable predictions. To address this issue, we propose a physics-guided advection-consistent modeling framework, termed PACT, which formulates event evolution as a motion-driven feature transport process. Instead of relying solely on local spatio-temporal aggregation, PACT propagates features along estimated velocity fields and enforces trajectory-level consistency through advection constraints. This design preserves weak event responses over time and prevents their degradation under complex background interference. Technically, PACT integrates motion-aware feature extraction with a differentiable advection-based transport operator, enabling coherent motion representation and effective noise suppression during temporal evolution. Extensive experiments on benchmark event-based datasets demonstrate that PACT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving improvements of 20.72\% in IoU and 15.03\% in accuracy while maintaining comparable computational efficiency. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/fulongcai/PACT.

</details>


### [148] [Structured Hyperedge Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22383)
*Edwin Kwadwo Tenagyei,Lei Wang,Ugochukwu Ejike Akpudo,Jun Zhou,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyperAdapter，一种基于超图的参数高效微调方法，通过在超边空间而非token空间进行适配，实现结构化、群组感知的视觉变换器微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的PEFT方法对每个token独立进行适配，忽略了视觉场景中token之间固有的结构化关系，导致冗余更新和空间上不一致的特征精炼。

Method: HyperAdapter构建软超图，通过原型分配将token特征聚合成潜在超边表示，在超边级别应用轻量瓶颈适配，再通过超图关联结构将更新扩散回token。

Result: 在多个视觉基准上，HyperAdapter在可比参数预算下一致优于强PEFT基线，在需要结构化推理的任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 适配空间的选择是ViT参数高效迁移中一个关键但未被充分探索的维度，结构化超边适配能有效注入归纳偏置并保持标准适配器的模块性和效率。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a practical solution for adapting large pretrained vision transformers (ViTs) to downstream tasks while updating only a small subset of parameters. However, existing adapter-based methods perform adaptation independently for each token, implicitly assuming that token refinements should be learned in isolation. This token-wise formulation overlooks the structured relationships among tokens that naturally arise in visual scenes, potentially leading to redundant updates and spatially inconsistent feature refinement. In this work, we revisit the design of parameter-efficient adapters and propose to perform adaptation in hyperedge space rather than token space. We introduce HyperAdapter, a hypergraph-based adapter architecture that enables structured, group-aware adaptation through soft token routing. HyperAdapter constructs a soft hypergraph over ViT tokens using prototype-based assignments, aggregates token features into latent hyperedge representations, applies lightweight bottleneck adaptation at the hyperedge level, and diffuses the resulting updates back to tokens via the hypergraph incidence structure. This design injects an explicit structural inductive bias into PEFT while preserving the modularity and efficiency of standard adapters. Extensive experiments across diverse visual benchmarks demonstrate that structured hyperedge adaptation consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines under comparable parameter budgets, with particularly pronounced gains on tasks requiring structured reasoning. Our results suggest that the choice of adaptation space is a critical yet underexplored dimension in parameter-efficient transfer for ViTs.

</details>


### [149] [Curvature-Adaptive Consistency Flow Matching: Autonomous Trajectory Optimization via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22394)
*Songtao Tian,Guhan Chen,Bohan Li,Jingyi Ma,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率自适应一致性流匹配（CACFM），通过强化学习代理动态探索概率流ODE轨迹，结合流分布匹配蒸馏目标，在极少量步数下显著提升扩散模型的生成质量，在FLUX和SDXL等大规模模型上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一致性蒸馏在加速扩散模型推理方面效果显著，但现有方法采用静态采样策略，无法适应动态的学习需求。作者发现Logit-Normal采样先验在标准迭代生成中有效，但在一致性蒸馏中呈现U型难度分布，优化瓶颈集中在边界阶段（初始化或最终细化），而非中间步骤。

Method: 提出CACFM框架，将蒸馏过程建模为动态决策问题：1）使用轻量级强化学习代理主动探索概率流ODE轨迹，自动构建关注关键区域的高效课程学习；2）引入流分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）目标函数，实现分布级别的匹配。

Result: 在FLUX和SDXL等大规模模型上取得SOTA结果，在极端少步数场景下有效缓解结构畸形并保留高频细节，实现了前所未有的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习驱动的自适应采样策略，CACFM克服了静态采样的局限性，为一致性蒸馏在高保真度少步生成中提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Consistency distillation has significantly accelerated the inference of diffusion models. In this work, we reveal an intriguing asymmetry: while Logit-Normal sampling priors are highly efficacious for standard iterative generation, consistency distillation exhibits a distinctly different difficulty profile (e.g., U-shaped). We identify that the primary optimization bottlenecks reside at the boundary stages (initialization or final refinement) rather than the intermediate steps. To address the limitations of static sampling in accommodating evolving learning requirements, we propose Curvature-Adaptive Consistency Flow Matching (CACFM). By formulating distillation as a dynamic decision process, CACFM employs a lightweight Reinforcement Learning agent to actively probe Probability Flow ODE trajectories, automatically constructing an efficiency-oriented curriculum that prioritizes critical regions without manual scheduling. Integrated with a novel Flow Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) objective, our approach achieves new state-of-the-art results on large-scale models such as FLUX and SDXL. It effectively mitigates structural deformities and preserves high-frequency details in extreme few-step regimes, achieving unprecedented visual fidelity.

</details>


### [150] [Multi-cancer detection using a computationally efficient CNN with transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22400)
*Vasileios E. Papageorgiou,Georgios Petmezas,Dimitrios-Panagiotis Papageorgiou,Leandros Stefanopoulos,Nicos Maglaveras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合迁移学习的轻量级CNN架构，用于多癌种检测。该模型在脑部MRI、肺部CT和肾脏CT三个数据集上分别达到90.85%、98.64%和99.92%的测试准确率，仅需20个额外微调epoch即可媲美从头训练的性能，且推理速度快（每图像每epoch约0.014秒），在计算资源受限环境下具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决深度学习在癌症检测中计算复杂度高的问题，提出一种轻量级CNN模型，使其能在资源受限环境中实现多癌种的高效、准确检测，同时通过迁移学习减少训练开销。

Method: 设计轻量级CNN架构，采用迁移学习策略：先在一个癌种上预训练，再在其他癌种上微调（仅更新分类部分，20个epoch）。使用5折分层交叉验证评估，在脑部MRI、肺部CT和肾脏CT三个数据集上测试，并与Xception、VGG16等SOTA模型对比。

Result: 脑癌分类准确率90.85±2.22%，肺癌98.64±2.43%，肾癌99.92±0.08%；微调仅需20个epoch，每图像每epoch约0.014秒（GTX 960）；性能优于Xception、VGG16等预训练架构。

Conclusion: 轻量级DL框架在不牺牲准确率的前提下能够加速癌症诊断，特别适用于计算资源有限的临床环境，展现了跨癌种、跨模态（MRI和CT）的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: This study introduces a computationally efficient convolutional neural network (CNN) architecture enhanced with transfer learning for multi-cancer detection using biomedical images. The proposed lightweight CNN model is designed to reduce computational complexity while maintaining high classification performance, making it suitable for deployment in resource-constrained environments. We evaluate this approach on three distinct tumor datasets comprising brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) and lung and kidney computed tomography (CT) scans. The model achieves test accuracy of 90.85 +- 2.22%, 98.64 +- 2.43% and 99.92 +- 0.08% for brain, lung, and kidney cancer classification, respectively, using 5-fold stratified cross-validation (CV). Transfer learning is employed by pretraining the model on one cancer type and fine-tuning it on the others, requiring only 20 additional epochs to achieve performance comparable to models trained from scratch. The fine-tuning process involves updating the classification part of the CNN and requires approximately 0.014 seconds per image per epoch using an NVIDIA GeForce GTX 960. Comparative evaluations show that the proposed model outperforms several state-of-the-art pretrained architectures, such as Xception, VGG16, VGG19, MobileNetV2 and DenseNet121. Overall, the model's effectiveness is evaluated across three types of cancer with distinct morphological characteristics, assessing its performance on both MRI and CT imaging modalities and demonstrating robust performance across diverse tasks and data types. These findings underscore the potential of streamlined deep learning (DL) frameworks in accelerating cancer diagnosis without sacrificing accuracy, especially in settings with limited computational resources.

</details>


### [151] [Gold Points Sniper: Self-guided Visual Reasoning in VLM for Fine-grained Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22409)
*Haodi Liu,Xinhang Yang,Kunda Yan,Sen Cui,Zeyu Zhang,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gold Points Sniper (GPS)框架，通过自引导多模态推理增强轻量级VLM对细粒度人类动作的理解能力，在保持事实准确性的同时达到与GPT-4o相当的性能，为家庭机器人安全理解人类行为奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇动作识别方法局限于预定义标签，视觉语言模型(VLM)在信息丰富度与事实保真度之间存在固有权衡，两者均无法实现可靠人机交互所需的深层语义理解。特别是在宽视场中人类仅占小区域的情况下，现有系统无法理解细粒度人类动作、意图和上下文线索。

Method: GPS框架包含三个核心模块：(1) Gold Points Extractor——训练VLM识别关键动作相关细节；(2) Selective Socratic Questioner——通过选择性自我提问验证和精炼这些细节；(3) Semantic Entailance Evaluator——利用语义蕴涵分类定量评估事实一致性。

Result: 在CAP基准构建的指令微调数据集上的大量实验表明，GPS增强的轻量级VLM取得了显著的性能提升，部分模型达到与专有GPT-4o相当的性能，同时保持了更优的事实准确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为家庭机器人中的细粒度动作理解建立了可靠基础，使机器人能够通过信息密集且事实有据的描述安全地解释人类行为。

Abstract: Robots operating in everyday environments must understand fine-grained human actions, intentions, and contextual cues from broad views where people occupy only small regions, a capability unmet by current systems. While open-vocabulary action recognition methods remain limited to assigning predefined labels, and vision-language models (VLMs) face an inherent trade-off between informational richness and factual fidelity in their outputs, neither approach achieves the deep semantic interpretation required for reliable human-robot interaction. We propose Gold Points Sniper (GPS), a novel framework that empowers lightweight VLMs with self-guided multimodal reasoning capabilities for fine-grained human action understanding. Our approach comprises three key modules: Gold Points Extractor trains VLMs to identify critical action-relevant details, Selective Socratic Questioner validates and refines these details through selective self-questioning, and Semantic Entailment Evaluator quantitatively assesses factual consistency using semantic entailment classification. Extensive experiments on our curated instruction-tuning dataset based on the CAP benchmark demonstrate that GPS-enhanced lightweight VLMs achieve substantial performance improvements, with some models reaching performance comparable to proprietary GPT-4o while maintaining superior factual accuracy. Our work establishes a reliable foundation for fine-grained action understanding in domestic robotics, enabling robots to safely interpret human behavior through information-dense yet factually grounded descriptions. Source code, training configurations, annotation prompts, and dataset details are released at https://github.com/Haodi-Liu/GPS-Gold-Point-Sniper.

</details>


### [152] [Gen2Balance: Generative Balancing for Long-Tailed Video Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22416)
*Prajwal Gatti,Simon Jenni,Fabian Caba Heilbron,Dima Damen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gen2Balance方法，利用文本到视频生成模型将长尾训练集转化为平衡的真实与生成视频组合，通过两阶段训练策略缓解域偏移，在长尾视频动作识别任务上显著提升性能，尤其在稀有动作上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长尾数据分布下视频动作识别训练效果不佳的问题，传统方法难以有效处理头尾类别不平衡。

Method: 利用文本到视频生成模型，基于动作描述和训练样本生成多样化提示，将不平衡数据集增强为平衡的真实与生成视频组合；采用两阶段训练策略缓解真实与生成数据之间的域偏移。

Result: 在UCF-LT和K100-LT上分别比最强基线提升5.1%和7.0%准确率；在RareAct稀有动作上提升31.9%；部分平衡策略以27%计算成本获得79%的性能增益。

Conclusion: Gen2Balance通过生成模型增强长尾数据能有效提升视频动作识别性能，且具有实际可扩展性。

Abstract: We address the problem of training on long-tailed data for video action recognition. We propose to augment the training set using a text-to-video generative model, conditioned on diverse text prompts grounded in action profiles and training exemplars. Our approach, called Gen2Balance, converts an imbalanced training set into a balanced combination of real and generated video clips. To effectively learn from such data, we employ a two-stage training strategy that mitigates domain shift and yields significant improvements. We evaluate on long-tailed versions of standard benchmarks: UCF-101 (UCF-LT) and a 100-class subset of Kinetics (K100-LT) selected to prioritise temporally challenging actions. Gen2Balance improves accuracy over the strongest baselines for long-tailed learning by 5.1% and 7.0% on the respective datasets. On rare actions from the RareAct dataset (e.g., cut keyboard), Gen2Balance improves accuracy by 31.9%, demonstrating effectiveness for scarce actions. By varying the amount of synthetic data added, we show that partial balancing already achieves 79% of the performance gains at 27% of the compute cost on K100-LT, highlighting the practical scalability of Gen2Balance.

</details>


### [153] [FlowDec: Temporal Conditional Flow Decorruptor for Robust Continuous Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22424)
*Yufei Zhang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowDec，一种为基于大模型的VLN-CE任务设计的图像恢复框架，通过时序条件流生成和动作质心引导滤波，有效应对现实世界中视觉污染对导航性能的严重影响，在导航精度和生成延迟上均超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的视觉污染（如噪声、模糊等）严重降低了基于大模型的VLN-CE代理的导航性能，而这一问题尚未得到充分研究，亟需一种鲁棒的图像恢复方案来保障代理在不可预测环境中的稳定表现。

Method: FlowDec融合了两种核心策略：（1）混合时序条件策略，将生成流路径与历史上下文对齐；（2）动作质心引导滤波，动态评估并整合输出结果，实现高效去污染与导航。

Result: 在导航准确率和生成延迟两个指标上，FlowDec均优于当前最先进的去污染方法。

Conclusion: FlowDec为不可预测现实条件下的鲁棒具身导航建立了一个高效、稳健的范式。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires agents to follow natural-language instructions in unseen scenes. While Large Models (LMs) have advanced VLN-CE, their performance remains severely degraded by real-world visual corruptions, a critical yet underexplored domain constraint. We introduce Temporal Conditional Flow Decorruptor (FlowDec), a novel image restoration framework tailored for LM-based VLN-CE. FlowDec integrates a hybrid temporal conditioning strategy to align the generative flow path with historical context and employs action-centroid guided filtering to dynamically assess and integrate outputs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowDec outperforms state-of-the-art decorruption methods in both navigation accuracy and generation latency. Our approach establishes a robust, efficient paradigm for resilient embodied navigation in unpredictable real-world conditions.

</details>


### [154] [MMGist: A Comprehensive Multimodal Benchmark for 2027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22437)
*Wenzhen Yuan,Jiacheng Ruan,Wutao Xiong,Chengping Zhao,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了18个视觉语言基准测试，发现三个主要问题：许多项目不依赖视觉线索、性能接近饱和、存在异常项。为此提出MMGist基准，包含7,262个样本，覆盖7个能力维度，通过三阶段过滤流程构建。实验表明MMGist在保留模型排序高保真度（斯皮尔曼ρ=0.98）的同时，减少69%的评估项目并提升78%的跨模型区分度。研究发现视觉逻辑是当前LVLMs的系统性弱点，专家知识维度是区分闭源与开源模型的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准测试存在三个主要问题：1) 许多项目不依赖视觉线索，无法有效衡量多模态理解能力；2) 许多项目性能已接近饱和，缺乏区分度；3) 存在异常项影响评估结果的可靠性。因此需要构建一个更高质量的基准测试。

Method: 提出三阶段过滤流水线构建MMGist基准：(1) 文本消融过滤 – 剔除不依赖视觉线索的项目；(2) 跨模型饱和过滤 – 移除性能饱和的低区分度项目；(3) 异常检测过滤 – 识别并移除异常项。最终从23,250个原始项目中精选出7,262个高质量样本，覆盖7个能力维度。

Result: 在27个领先LVLM上的实验表明：MMGist以69%的项目缩减实现了斯皮尔曼ρ=0.98的排序保真度，跨模型区分度提升78%。视觉逻辑是当前LVLMs的系统性弱点，专家知识维度是区分闭源与开源模型的关键因素。

Conclusion: 高质量评估应优先关注视觉依赖性、区分度和可靠性，而非单纯追求基准测试的规模。MMGist为LVLM评估提供了更高效、更具鉴别力的标准化基准。

Abstract: We conduct a systematic study of 18 widely used vision-language benchmarks and identify three major issues: 1) many items do not rely on visual cues and therefore fail to effectively measure multimodal understanding; 2) many items are already close to performance saturation for current LVLMs, which limits their discriminative power; 3) a small number of anomalous items affect the reliability of evaluation results. To this end, we propose MMGist, a curated benchmark that covers seven capability dimensions and contains 7,262 items. MMGist is constructed through a three-stage pipeline, which sequentially combines text-ablation filtering, cross-model saturation filtering, and anomaly detection filtering. We conduct extensive experiments on 27 leading LVLMs and compare MMGist with the raw pool of 23,250 items. The results show that MMGist preserves model rankings with high fidelity, with Spearman $ρ= 0.98$, while reducing evaluation items by 69\% and improving cross-model discrimination by 78\%. Further results indicate that Visual Logic remains a systematic weakness of current LVLMs, while knowledge-intensive dimensions such as Expert Knowledge dimensions remain important factors for distinguishing closed-source models from open-source models. These findings suggest that high-quality evaluation should prioritize visual dependency, discriminative power, and reliability, rather than simply pursuing benchmark scale.

</details>


### [155] [Curvature-aware 3D length estimation of greenhouse cucumbers using RGB-D imaging and cubic spline arc-length integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22439)
*Manveen Kaur,Rajmeet Singh,Saeed Mozaffri,Shahpour Alirezaee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CucumberVision框架，利用Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D相机和实例分割模型实现商业化温室黄瓜的非接触式长度测量。五种方法中，M5（中轴弧样条法）表现最佳（MAPE 4.13%），并发现深度流内在参数导致的12-18%长度低估误差。系统开源且可在单消费级GPU上实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业温室黄瓜生产按果长分级，驱动采收调度、劳动力分配和物流。人工测量虽准确但在商业规模下不可行，亟需非接触式自动化长度估计方案。

Method: 使用Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D相机采集数据，YOLO26n进行实例分割定位黄瓜，SAM（ViT-B骨干）细化分割掩码。评估五种方法：M1（主骨架扫描线基线）、M2（边界框深度点云PCA）、M3（SAM掩码中轴骨架化）、M4（关键点引导+YOLO26-pose五点解剖 landmarks+分段3D弧长）、M5（SAM掩码3D中轴三次样条拟合+梯形积分弧长）。所有方法共享五帧爆发深度平均、彩色流内参对齐和自适应方法选择。在7根黄瓜3种尺寸类别、48次捕获上对比线绳基准。

Result: 精度排序：M1 (MAPE 9.68%) > M2 (5.31%) > M4 (5.51%) > M3 (5.82%) > M5 (4.13%)。M5在Bonferroni校正α=0.0125下显著优于所有对比方法。发现使用深度流而非彩色流内参导致12-18%的长度低估误差。

Conclusion: CucumberVision框架能够实现商业化温室黄瓜的高精度非接触式长度测量，M5方法首次将中轴弧样条法应用于细长蔬菜测量并取得最佳效果。系统开源、实时运行，并揭示了深度-彩色流内参对齐误差这一此前未充分报告的问题。

Abstract: Commercial greenhouse cucumber production is graded by fruit length, which drives harvest scheduling, labour allocation, and logistics. Manual measurement with thread or caliper is accurate but infeasible at commercial scale. This paper presents CucumberVision, a non-contact length estimation framework using an Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D camera. A YOLO26n instance segmentation model locates cucumbers, and SAM (ViT-B backbone) refines each detection to a pixel-precise mask. Five methods are evaluated under matched conditions: (M1) a dominant-axis skeleton scan-line baseline; (M2) PCA on the bounding-box depth point cloud; (M3) SAM mask with medial-axis skeletonisation; (M4) a hybrid keypoint-guided approach using a YOLO26-pose model predicting five anatomical landmarks (KP0--KP4) with piecewise 3D arc-length; and (M5) a novel medial arc spline method fitting a cubic spline through the 3D medial axis of the SAM mask and computing arc length by trapezoidal integration -- the first such application to elongated vegetable measurement. All methods share five-frame burst depth averaging, colour-stream intrinsic alignment, and adaptive method selection with cascading fallbacks ensuring 100% coverage. A benchmark of 48 captures across seven cucumbers in three size categories (small ~8 cm, medium ~13 cm, large ~25 cm) with thread-based ground truth establishes a significant accuracy hierarchy: M1 (MAPE 9.68%) > M2 (5.31%) > M4 (5.51%) > M3 (5.82%) > M5 (4.13%). M5 significantly outperforms all competitors at Bonferroni-corrected alpha=0.0125. A secondary contribution is identifying a 12--18% length underestimation caused by using depth-stream rather than colour-stream intrinsics after rs.align(rs.stream.color) -- an under-reported error source. The complete system is released open source and runs in real time on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [156] [DreamUV: Unwrap Artist-like UV by End-to-End Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22445)
*Quanyuan Ruan,Jiabao Lei,Xingyi Du,Xifeng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamUV是一个端到端学习框架，将UV展开建模为生成式流匹配问题，通过边界感知训练和模型在环微调策略，生成符合艺术家风格的生产级UV布局，在保持竞争性畸变指标的同时实现更直的接缝和更紧的轴对齐岛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法优化手工设计的能量函数，但艺术家创作的UV展现出接缝笔直、轴对齐岛和灵活内部变形等难以显式公式化的结构模式，存在几何畸变目标与艺术风格偏好之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将UV展开建模为生成式流匹配问题，学习网格条件化的输运过程将噪声映射到类艺术家UV布局分布；提出边界感知训练策略优先考虑接缝几何；引入模型在环微调方案显式考虑采样离散化误差并稳定异构监督下的输运动力学。

Result: 在大规模专业UV布局数据集上，DreamUV生成的边界显著更直、轴对齐岛更紧，优于经典和基于学习的基线方法，同时保持竞争性畸变指标。定性结果和专业艺术家用户研究进一步验证了其符合实际生产需求。

Conclusion: DreamUV成功弥合了几何畸变目标与艺术风格偏好之间的鸿沟，生成的UV布局不仅有效，且符合实际生产要求。

Abstract: UV parameterization is a fundamental step in 3D content creation, yet producing production-ready UV layouts remains challenging due to the gap between geometric distortion objectives and the stylistic preferences of professional artists. While classical methods optimize handcrafted energy functions, artist-authored UVs exhibit structural patterns such as straightened seams, axis-aligned islands, and flexible interior deformation, properties that are difficult to explicitly formulate. In this work, we present DreamUV, an end-to-end learning framework that formulates UV unwrapping as a generative Flow Matching problem. Rather than predicting a single optimal parameterization, DreamUV learns a mesh-conditioned transport process that maps noise samples to a distribution of artist-like UV layouts. To reflect real-world authoring practices, we introduce a boundary-aware training strategy that prioritizes seam geometry, and a Model-in-the-Loop Finetuning(MITL) scheme that explicitly accounts for discretization errors during sampling and stabilizes transport dynamics under heterogeneous supervision. We evaluate DreamUV on a large-scale dataset of professionally authored UV layouts. Experiments demonstrate that our method produces significantly straighter boundaries and tighter axis-aligned islands than both classical and learning-based baselines, while maintaining competitive distortion metrics. Qualitative results and a user study with professional artists further confirm that DreamUV generates UV layouts that are not only valid, but aligned with practical production requirements.

</details>


### [157] [CVSBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Cross-view Spatial Reasoning and Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22476)
*Ruixun Liu,Lingyu Zhang,Lanxuan Xue,Kaiyu Li,Bowen Fu,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVSBench基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在卫星-街景跨视角空间推理能力。实验发现现有VLM在剧烈视角变化下无法维持物体级和布局一致性，而引入3D场景想象管线（视觉空间想象）能显著提升跨视角推理，表明显式视觉-空间表征对鲁棒空间认知的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能轻松跨视角推理场景，但VLM是否具备类似能力尚不明确。卫星-街景对具有复杂上下文和极端视角变化，是理想的测试场景，但目前缺乏相应的系统评估基准。

Method: 构建包含3,297个跨视角图像组、9,468个物体级标注和40,679个QA对的CVSBench基准，支持跨视角VQA、跨视角定位和视角识别任务。在此基础上评估多种VLM，并探索两类改进方法：基于空间推理的推理和引入认知地图输入，其中关键方法是3D场景想象管线。

Result: 现有高级VLM在剧烈视角变化下无法维持物体级和布局一致性；语言-only推理改进微弱；引入3D场景想象管线的视觉空间想象方法显著提升了跨视角推理能力。

Conclusion: 显式视觉-空间表征对于VLM实现鲁棒的空间认知至关重要，单纯语言推理不足以弥合跨视角空间推理的差距。

Abstract: Humans can effortlessly reason about scenes across different viewpoints, yet it remains unclear whether Vision-Language Models (VLMs) possess similar cross-view spatial abilities. Satellite-street scene pairs, with their complex contexts and extreme viewpoint variations, provide an ideal testbed. Motivated by this, we introduce CVSBench, a large-scale benchmark for evaluating cross-view spatial reasoning through satellite-street pairs.
  This benchmark supports multiple tasks, including cross-view VQA, cross-view grounding, and viewpoint identification. CVSBench comprises 3,297 cross-view image groups with 9,468 object-level annotations and 40,679 question-answer (QA) pairs, enabling systematic and controlled evaluation of cross-view spatial reasoning. Extensive evaluations reveal that advanced VLMs struggle to maintain object-level and layout consistency under drastic viewpoint changes. To bridge this gap towards human-like spatial cognition, we investigate two categories of approaches: spatially grounded reasoning and the incorporation of cognitive map inputs.
  Our findings demonstrate that language-only reasoning yields marginal improvements, while incorporating visual spatial imagination via a 3D scene imagination pipeline substantially improves cross-view reasoning. These results highlight the necessity of explicit visual-spatial representations for robust spatial cognition in VLMs. Our data and code are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zlyzlyzly/CVSBench.

</details>


### [158] [Physically-guided Image Generation for Multi-Projection Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22477)
*Xingyun Liu,Yuqi Li,Jinhui Xiang,Pinyan Tang,Chong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ConPhyG框架，通过将物理约束（色域、深度、边缘）注入扩散模型，实现可控的物理引导生成式多投影映射，支持协同与对抗两种范式，并在四投影仪真实系统中验证了其在几何对齐、色域利用和语义保真度上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在投影映射（PM）中的应用面临理想化2D生成与物理约束不匹配的鸿沟，现有方法难以直接集成到实际PM管线中。

Method: 提出ConPhyG框架，形式化两种生成范式：协同范式（语义与物理属性协调）和对抗范式（通过辐射补偿消除表面干扰）。在协同模式下，注入多维度物理先验（逐像素色域、深度、边缘）；在对抗模式下，采用有界数值优化实现多投影仪辐射补偿。扩展至360度多视图一致PM采用顺序生成策略。

Result: 在真实四投影仪系统上的定量和定性评估表明，ConPhyG在几何对齐、色域利用和语义保真度上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ConPhyG通过可控物理引导生成，弥合了扩散模型与PM物理约束之间的差距，支持用户在艺术自由与物理可行性之间动态平衡，并具备到360度多视图一致PM的可扩展性。

Abstract: Projection Mapping (PM) enables seamless superimposition of digital content onto real-world 3D objects, serving as a fundamental technique for immersive visualization, digital twins, and interactive art. Although text-to-image diffusion models have greatly facilitated customized content creation, directly integrating them into practical PM pipelines remains challenging due to the mismatch between idealized 2D generation and physical constraints. To bridge this gap, this paper formalizes two application-level generative paradigms: the cooperative paradigm (harmonizing generated semantics with physical attributes) and the adversarial paradigm (eliminating surface interference via radiometric compensation). Based on this, we propose ConPhyG, a unified controllable physically-guided generative multi-projection mapping framework that enables creators to interactively adjust physical constraints and flexibly switch generative paradigms. In cooperative mode, multi-dimensional physical priors (per-pixel gamut, depth, and edges) are injected into the diffusion process. In adversarial mode, the framework releases the generative potential and applies bounded numerical optimization for multi-projector radiometric compensation. It allows users to dynamically switch constraints to balance artistic freedom with physical feasibility. Furthermore, we extend ConPhyG to 360-degree multi-view consistent PM using a sequential generation strategy. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations on a real-world four-projector setup demonstrate that ConPhyG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in geometric alignment, gamut utilization, and semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [159] [Human and AI collaboration for pulmonary nodule segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22486)
*Hongqiao Dong,Wenhao Chi,Ruobing Liang,Xiaokui Yang,Wenhua Liang,Peng Hou,Wenjun Pu,Yipeng Zhao,Ping Chen,Haiping Liu,Jianxing He,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hi-Seg是一种基于SAM的肺结节人机协同分割框架，通过人类迭代式提示优化，在12个中心1,179名患者的CT扫描中达到约85%的Dice分数，优于5种SOTA深度学习模型和13种SAM变体，并能使非医学标注者达到初级医学生的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学专家标注稀缺，单纯依赖AI可能产生误导。需要探索人类（尤其是初级医学受训者或非医学人员）与AI协作的方式，以实现鲁棒的医学分割。尽管SAM在通用分割上有前景，但其在人机协作医学任务中的表现尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 提出Hi-Seg框架，基于SAM构建人机协同分割系统。人类通过试错学习和语义推理迭代优化提示，逐步引导SAM生成更高质量的分割掩码。使用来自12个中心1,179名患者的胸部CT扫描进行大规模外部验证。

Result: 所有标注者组中Hi-Seg平均Dice分数接近85%，优于5种SOTA深度学习模型10-22%，优于13种SAM变体1-29%。在提高分割准确性的同时减少了医学标注者的标注时间，经过简短训练的非医学标注者达到了与初级医学生相当的性能。

Conclusion: 人机协同分割可以减轻临床医生工作负担，实现可扩展的众包标注，通过促进基础模型安全高效地集成到常规临床实践中来变革临床工作流程。

Abstract: Medical expert annotators are scarce, and blind reliance on artificial intelligence (AI) can be misleading, motivating approaches in which humans, particularly junior medical trainees or even non-medical personnel, collaborate with AI to achieve robust medical segmentation. Although the Segment Anything Model (SAM) shows promise for general-purpose image segmentation, its performance in human-AI collaboration for specialized medical tasks has not been thoroughly evaluated. Here we present Hi-Seg, a human-in-the-loop segmentation framework for pulmonary nodules built on SAM. Humans iteratively refine prompts through trial-and-error learning and semantic reasoning, progressively guiding SAM toward higher-quality masks. Using chest CT scans from 1,179 patients across 12 centers, we conducted the first large-scale external validation of collaborative human-SAM segmentation. Across all annotator groups, Hi-Seg achieved a mean Dice score of almost 85%, outperforming five state-of-the-art deep learning models by 10-22% and 13 SAM variants by 1-29%. Hi-Seg improved segmentation accuracy while reducing annotation time for medical annotators, and briefly trained non-medical annotators achieved performance comparable to that of the junior medical student. These findings suggest that human-in-the-loop segmentation can reduce clinician workload, enable scalable crowdsourced annotation, and transform clinical workflows by facilitating the safe and efficient integration of foundation models into routine clinical practice.

</details>


### [160] [FetSelect: Task-Specific Architectures and Self-Supervised Learning for Automated Fetal Ultrasound Frame Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22487)
*Mahmood Alzubaidi,Raden Muaz,Uzair Shah,Mohammed Ammar,Khalid Alyafei,Mowafa Househ,Marco Agus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FetSelect是一个针对胎儿超声生物测量的自动帧选择框架，采用冻结视觉基础模型与混合多头设计（任务门控分类头+检测质量头），在四个测量目标上达到AUROC 0.956和相关性0.818，优于单头变体，且超声自监督预训练带来一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注通用质量评估或假设已存在合适帧的下游测量流程，针对胎儿生物测量的自动帧选择问题尚未被充分解决。

Method: 提出FetSelect框架，采用冻结的视觉基础骨干网络，结合混合多头设计：Task-Gated分类头和Detection-derived质量头，通过学习的融合机制结合两者输出。在19,019张无标签图像上使用BYOL进行超声特异性自监督预训练，在6,486张专家标注帧上微调，覆盖CRL、NT、鼻骨和Scalebar四个目标。

Result: 在974帧测试集上，平均AUROC达0.956，与专家质量标注的平均相关系数为0.818。混合融合策略优于单头变体，超声特异性自监督带来一致性能提升。在外部临床视频和509张外部CRL图像上验证了任务特异性判别能力。

Conclusion: FetSelect有效解决了胎儿超声自动帧选择问题，混合多头设计优于单头方案，超声特定自监督预训练有助于提升性能，且在外部数据上具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Automated frame selection for fetal biometry remains under addressed, with most prior work targeting generic quality assessment or downstream measurement pipelines that assume suitable frames are available. We introduce FetSelect, a task-specific framework that pairs a frozen vision foundation backbone with a hybrid multi-head design: a Task-Gated classification head and a Detection-derived quality head combined via learned fusion. We curate 6,486 expert-labeled frames across four targets: Crown-Rump Length (CRL), Nuchal Translucency (NT), Nasal Bone (NB), and Scalebar, and adapt the backbone with BYOL pretraining on 19,019 unlabeled images. On a held-out test set (974 frames), FetSelect achieves mean AUROC 0.956 and mean correlation 0.818 with expert quality annotations. Ablations confirm that hybrid fusion surpasses single-head variants, and ultrasound-specific self-supervision yields consistent gains. Evaluation on external clinical videos and 509 external CRL images demonstrates task-specific discrimination.

</details>


### [161] [Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Microscopic Plant Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22497)
*Tianqi Wei,Xin Yu,Zhi Chen,Scott Chapman,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PlantMicro是一个专门用于评估视觉语言模型在植物显微图像理解能力的基准数据集，包含5000+图像和9000+VQA对。实验表明当前VLM（如GPT-5）在细粒度识别和生物推理任务上表现差，仅略高于随机猜测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准主要关注宏观植物图像，而微观植物图像领域尚未被充分探索，亟需一个标准化的评估基准来推动该方向的发展。

Method: 收集5000+涵盖多种宿主、生物领域和成像模态的显微图像，构建9000+VQA对，设计互补性任务评估VLM在植物显微图像理解的不同方面。

Result: 当前VLM在PlantMicro上表现不佳，GPT-5在病原体分类任务上仅34.93%准确率，略高于随机猜测基线，表明现有模型在细粒度识别和生物学推理方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: PlantMicro填补了植物显微图像VLM评估的空白，揭示了当前模型在该领域的能力差距，为推进VLM实现可靠且全面的显微级别植物理解提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: Microscopic imaging provides essential visual evidence for studying plant biology and pathology at the cellular and subcellular levels. However, existing benchmarks on vision-language models primarily focus on macroscopic plant imagery, while the microscopic domain remains underexplored. To address this gap, we present PlantMicro, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) in microscopic plant imagery. PlantMicro integrates more than 5,000 images collected across diverse hosts, biological domains, and imaging modalities. Building on this diversity, we design a set of complementary tasks that capture different facets of microscopic image understanding. To support these tasks, we construct over 9,000 VQA pairs that systematically evaluate the capabilities of VLMs. Experiments on PlantMicro show that current VLMs struggle with fine-grained recognition and biologically grounded reasoning. For example, GPT-5 achieves 34.93% accuracy on the pathogen classification task, which is only modestly above the random-guessing baseline. The results highlight a significant gap in current VLMs' ability to comprehend plant microscopic images. PlantMicro provides a standardized foundation for advancing VLMs toward reliable and comprehensive microscopy-level plant understanding.

</details>


### [162] [Biological Sex Determination in Cadavers Using Deep Learning Algorithms from Computed Tomography Images of Pelvis and Skull](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22515)
*Giovanna Herculano Tormena,Davi Nascimento Araújo,Germano Coimbra Soares de Carvalho,Gustavo Bruno Centenaro,Rafael Janowski Pozzer,Rodrigo Akira Azevedo Kurosawa,Danilo Aires Alves,Filipe Thiago Xavier de Campos,Pedro Henrique Macedo dos Santos,Pedro Augusto Prado Mota,Ricardo V. Godoy,João Manoel Herrera Pinheiro,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多种深度学习模型（YOLO26、YOLO11、ConvNeXt-Tiny、EfficientNetV2、ViT-B16、VGG16、ResNet50）结合迁移学习，基于法医CT扫描的骨盆和颅骨三维重建二维投影，自动进行性别鉴定。最佳模型在骨盆区域表现优异，整体患者级准确率达95.65%，表明该方法在法医尸检中具有技术可行性，但需多中心验证其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖视觉人类学分析的性别鉴定方法在处理分解尸体时面临挑战，亟需一种客观、高速且不依赖完整软组织的自动化鉴定方法。

Method: 收集141具尸体的法医CT扫描数据，对骨盆和颅骨进行三维重建并转换为标准化二维轮廓投影；采用数据增强技术弥补样本限制；对比七种深度学习模型（YOLO26、YOLO11、ConvNeXt-Tiny、EfficientNetV2、ViT-B16、VGG16、ResNet50）结合迁移学习；设计二元分类（按性别）和四元分类（按性别+解剖区域）两种验证场景。

Result: 最佳模型在骨盆区域表现最优，整体患者级准确率95.65%，召回率92.86%，F1分数94.36%，精确率97.22%。在包含创伤伪影的案例中仍保持一致性表现。颅骨区域性能虽不及骨盆但仍在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型能够为法医尸检中的骨骼性别鉴定提供客观、高速的分析方案，技术上具有可行性。但由于数据仅来自单一机构和单一CT扫描仪，需在多中心、多设备条件下进一步验证其泛化能力。

Abstract: Sexual identification of decomposed cadavers challenges traditional methods dependent on visual anthropological analysis. This study evaluates state-of-the-art deep learning (including YOLO26, YOLO11, ConvNeXt-Tiny, EfficientNetV2, ViT-B16, VGG16, and ResNet50) with transfer learning to automatically determine biological sex from forensic computed tomography (CT) scans. We analyzed 141 autopsied cadavers from the Forensic Medical Institute of Goiânia-GO, including a broad age range and varying conditions of preservation. The three-dimensional reconstructions of the pelvis and skull were converted into standardized two-dimensional profile projections, contributing to the study of this new technical approach. Data augmentation techniques compensated for sample limitations. Two scenarios were validated: binary and quaternary classification (one class per sex vs. one class per anatomical region of each sex). The best-performing model achieved highly consistent results on the pelvis region and still satisfactory performance on the skull region, reaching an overall patient-level accuracy of 95.65%, recall of 92.86%, F1- score of 94.36%, and precision of 97.22%, maintaining consistent performance across the evaluated cases, including those with trauma-related artifacts. Results indicate the technical feasibility of the methodology, demonstrating that deep learning models can provide objective, high-speed skeletal analysis. Since the study was conducted using data from a single institution and a single computed tomography scanner, further validation across multiple centers and scanners is required to assess the generalizability of the proposed approach

</details>


### [163] [Projection-Volume Fidelity Divergence: Diagnosing and Controlling Optimization Drift in Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22525)
*Yikuang Yuluo,Ao Wang,Shen Kuan,Yujie Liu,Wang Liao,Ying Chen,Shuangyang Zhong,Yixing Huang,Fuquan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LADES提出了一种无需真实值的优化控制器，通过线性退火dropout和结构感知早停策略，解决稀疏视角CT重建中3D高斯投影优化导致体积退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角CT重建中，3D高斯泼溅方法虽然高效，但投影域优化会产生"投影-体积保真度发散"（PVFD）问题：投影质量持续提升，而重建体积反而恶化。需要一种能监控和控制体积结构而非仅仅优化投影拟合的方法。

Method: 提出LADES框架，包含两个核心组件：1）线性退火Dropout（Linearly Annealed Dropout），在训练早期施加强随机掩码以破坏过早的原语共适应，然后逐步恢复完整容量；2）结构感知早停（Structure-Aware Early Stopping），根据高斯种群增长的饱和程度而非验证PSNR来终止稠密化过程。

Result: 在稀疏视角CT重建实验中，LADES显著提升了体积保真度，抑制了结构退化，大幅缩短了训练时间，同时保持了竞争性的投影精度。

Conclusion: 稳健的高斯断层扫描需要监控和控制体积结构，而不能仅优化投影拟合。LADES通过无需真实值的优化控制机制有效解决了PVFD问题。

Abstract: Sparse-view computed tomography is a severely ill-posed inverse problem, where recent 3D Gaussian Splatting methods offer an efficient explicit representation for tomographic reconstruction. However, we find that projection-domain optimization can be misleading in this setting: the rendered projections may continue to improve while the reconstructed volume deteriorates. We identify this failure mode as Projection-Volume Fidelity Divergence (PVFD), a representation-level optimization drift caused by anisotropic Gaussian deformation and view-specific primitive co-adaptation under sparse Radon constraints. To characterize this behavior, we introduce geometry- and volume-level diagnostics that measure needle-like Gaussian degeneration and the stability of the voxelized density field. Based on these observations, we propose LADES, a ground-truth-free optimization controller for sparse-view Gaussian tomography. LADES combines Linearly Annealed Dropout, which applies strong stochastic masking in early training to disrupt premature primitive co-adaptation and gradually restores full capacity for structural consolidation, with Structure-Aware Early Stopping, which terminates densification according to the saturation of Gaussian population growth rather than validation PSNR. Experiments on sparse-view CT reconstruction show that LADES improves volumetric fidelity, suppresses structural degeneration, and substantially reduces training time while maintaining competitive projection accuracy. These results suggest that robust Gaussian-based tomography requires monitoring and controlling volumetric structure, rather than optimizing projection fit alone.

</details>


### [164] [Trajectory Forcing: Structure-First Generation with Controllable Semantic Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22527)
*Merve Kocabas,Gege Gao,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轨迹强迫（TF）框架，将生成过程显式化为可编辑的语义阶段序列（从全局布局到细节），通过聚类预训练视觉表示构建粗到细的教师层级并训练层级条件流匹配模型，支持中间状态检查与局部编辑，在保持竞争性生成质量的同时实现可解释的轨迹控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流模型的控制性以端点为中心（用户指定条件→最终输出），中间生成动态被隐藏；虽有方法利用生成顺序和过程分解来提升样本质量，但仍将中间状态视为内部计算而非可交互对象。

Method: 1) 将合成过程组织为语义结构化的阶段序列（全局布局→物体→部件→细节）；2) 每个阶段产生可解码的潜状态供检查、评估和局部编辑；3) 通过聚类预训练视觉表示（如DINOv2）构建粗到细的教师层级；4) 在每个层级训练层级条件的一步流匹配模型；5) 引入轨迹感知评估指标（结构一致性和局部可控性）。

Result: TF在保持竞争性样本质量的同时，暴露出连贯的中间状态，并支持跨语义层级的局部编辑。

Conclusion: 通过将焦点从最终图像转向生成路径本身，TF开辟了可控、轨迹感知的图像合成新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models produce strong images, yet their controllability remains largely endpoint-centric: users specify conditions and receive final outputs, while the intermediate generative dynamics remain hidden. Recent methods have begun to exploit generation order and process decomposition to improve sample quality, but still treat intermediate states as internal computation rather than objects for interaction. We propose Trajectory Forcing (TF), a trajectory-centric framework that makes the generation path explicit, semantic, and editable. TF organizes synthesis as a sequence of semantically structured stages, progressing from global layout to object-, part-, and detail-level representations. Each stage produces a decodable latent state that can be inspected, evaluated, and locally edited before the next stage begins. To instantiate this path, we derive coarse-to-fine teacher hierarchies by clustering pretrained visual representations such as DINOv2, and train a hierarchy-conditioned one-step flow-matching model at each level. We further introduce trajectory-aware metrics that measure structural consistency and local controllability beyond endpoint quality metrics such as FID. Experiments show that TF achieves competitive sample quality while exposing coherent intermediate states and supporting localized edits across semantic levels. By shifting the focus from final images to the generative path itself, TF opens a route toward controllable, trajectory-aware image synthesis.

</details>


### [165] [NegAS: Negative Label Guided Attention and Scoring for Out-of-Distribution Object Detection with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22537)
*Yingjie Zhang,Shuai Li,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NegAS框架，通过负标签引导注意力（NegA）和负标签引导评分（NegS）解决VLM目标检测器在OOD检测中的两大挑战，在COCO和OpenImages上分别降低FPR95达11.4%和25.5%，并可泛化至不同VLM检测器架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM目标检测器在OOD场景中的潜力尚未被充分探索，且存在两个具体挑战：(i) 文本引导的注意力仅增强ID前景，未充分利用背景中的潜在OOD区域；(ii) 基于sigmoid的多标签输出与传统的softmax OOD评分不兼容，需要与VLM概率输出一致的评分函数。

Method: 提出NegAS框架，包含两个核心模块：NegA（负标签引导注意力模块）利用LLM生成的视觉相似但语义不同的负标签引导注意力聚焦于潜在OOD背景区域；NegS（负标签引导评分函数）基于sigmoid设计，同时利用ID标签和负标签，使ID实例产生强响应而OOD实例响应被抑制。

Result: 在COCO数据集上FPR95降低11.4%，在OpenImages数据集上降低25.5%，同时保持ID检测精度。该方法不仅适用于YOLO-World等密集VLM检测器，还可成功适配至Grounding DINO等基于query的VLM transformer架构。

Conclusion: NegAS框架有效解决了VLM目标检测器的OOD检测难题，展现出良好的泛化能力，为VLM在安全关键应用中的部署提供了可靠保障。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection is essential for ensuring the robustness and reliability of object detection systems deployed in safety-critical applications. While prior research has mainly focused on uni-modal detectors or vision-language model (VLM) based classifiers, the potential of VLM-based object detectors in OOD scenarios remains underexplored. In this work, we take the first step toward building OOD object detection methods upon VLMs. We identify two challenges specific to VLM detectors: (i) their text-guided attention enhances foreground with ID labels but treats background uniformly, leaving potential OOD regions unexploited for separating in-distribution (ID) from OOD instances; and (ii) their sigmoid-based multi-label outputs are incompatible with softmax-based OOD scores, calling for scoring functions consistent with VLM probabilistic outputs. Hence, we introduce Negative Label Guided Attention and Scoring (NegAS). To address (i), we propose a negative label guided attention module (NegA), where LLM-generated, visually-similar but semantically-different negative labels are used to guide attention toward potential OOD background regions. To address (ii), we introduce a novel sigmoid-based OOD scoring function (NegS) that leverages both ID and negative labels, producing strong responses for ID instances and suppressed responses for OOD ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach improves OOD detection performance by a large margin while maintaining ID accuracy, e.g., reducing the FPR95 by 11.4% on the COCO dataset and 25.5% on the OpenImages dataset compared to the baseline model. While initially designed for dense VLM detectors like YOLO-World, we successfully adapt NegAS to Grounding DINO, a query-based VLM transformer and achieve significant improvements, demonstrating the generalizability of our framework.

</details>


### [166] [PolicyTrim: Boosting Intrinsic Policy Efficiency of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22540)
*Xianghui Wang,Feng Chen,Wenbo Zhang,Hua Yan,Zixuan Wang,Changsheng Li,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PolicyTrim框架，通过强化学习后训练，在不降低任务成功率的情况下，将VLA模型的动作块有效利用长度提升3倍，物理执行步骤减少51.4%，实现最高5.83倍的端到端部署加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在实际部署中受限于执行效率，先前工作主要集中在降低单步推理延迟（计算效率），但策略效率（策略可执行的动作块有效长度和完成任务的物理步骤数）尚未被充分探索。当前VLA策略存在规划不可靠性（动作块尾部预测退化）和动作冗余（生成不必要的物理步骤）两大问题。

Method: 提出PolicyTrim，一个基于强化学习的后训练框架：(1) 动态探索策略，对成功完成更长可执行长度的行为给予显式奖励，逐步将可信预测推向经验极限；(2) 冗余感知奖励，奖励用更少步骤成功完成任务的行为，同时惩罚不可复现的捷径，消除冗余物理动作。

Result: 在三个基准和三个VLA模型上的实验表明：动作块利用率提升3倍，物理执行步骤减少51.4%，端到端部署速度提升最高5.83倍，且不牺牲任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过关注策略效率而非单纯的计算效率，PolicyTrim有效解决了VLA模型在动作块预测退化和动作冗余方面的根本性问题，显著提升了实际部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified paradigm for robotic manipulation, yet their real-world deployment is often bottlenecked by execution efficiency. While existing efforts predominantly focus on compute-centric efficiency to reduce per-step inference latency, the intrinsic \textbf{policy efficiency} of these models remains largely unexplored. Policy efficiency is fundamentally affected by two factors, namely the effective executable length of predicted action chunks and the total physical steps required to complete a task. These two factors jointly determine the total number of forward inference calls during execution. We observe that current VLA policies struggle with planning unreliability and action redundancy, suffering from severe prediction degradation at the tail of action chunks and tending to generate unnecessarily redundant physical steps. To address this, we propose \textbf{PolicyTrim}, a reinforcement learning-based post-training framework that extends the reliable action chunk length and reduces redundant physical steps. For reliable chunk extension, we employ a dynamic exploration strategy that explicitly rewards the successful completion of longer executable lengths, progressively pushing the trustworthy prediction horizon to its empirical limit. For step efficiency, we design a redundancy-aware reward that directly favors successful task completions with fewer steps while penalizing unreproducible shortcuts, effectively eliminating redundant physical actions. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks and three VLA models demonstrate that PolicyTrim improves action chunk utilization by 3$\times$ and reduces physical execution steps by 51.4\%. Ultimately, our framework delivers up to a 5.83$\times$ end-to-end deployment speedup without compromising task success rates.

</details>


### [167] [MAPS: Multi-Anchor Projection Similarity for Joint Vision-Language Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22543)
*Yutong Hu,Siyuan Tan,Shaocheng Yan,Pengcheng Shi,Qingwu Hu,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多锚点投影相似度（MAPS）度量方法，将视觉-语言地理定位（VLGL）中的联合图像-文本查询建模为多锚点几何对齐问题，通过将目标特征投影到由视觉和文本查询特征构建的锚点平面上进行相似度测量，并配套设计MAPS对比损失函数，在VLGL任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理定位模型基于点对点对齐，无法处理联合视觉-语言查询中视觉与文本线索共同定义语义子空间的场景，需要一种能够捕捉联合查询子空间几何一致性的定位框架。

Method: 提出MAPS（Multi-Anchor Projection Similarity）度量，将视觉和文本查询特征在高维空间中构建锚点平面，通过目标特征在该平面上的投影长度衡量相似度；并设计基于MAPS的对比损失，驱动目标特征向对应锚点平面靠近。

Result: 在视觉-语言地理定位（VLGL）任务上取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: 提出的框架、相似度度量和训练目标三者联合构成了VLGL任务的有效解决方案，验证了几何对齐方法优于传统点对点对齐策略。

Abstract: Humans localize places by integrating perceptual cues from vision with semantic reasoning from language, forming a scene understanding that is both intuitive and structured. Although existing geo-localization models have made substantial progress in cross-view and cross-modal settings, they are largely built upon point-to-point alignment, which is insufficient for joint vision-language queries. In such queries, visual and textual cues do not simply act as independent references, but jointly define a semantic subspace for locating the target. In this paper, we formulate vision-language geo-localization (VLGL) with joint image-text queries as a multi-anchor geometric alignment problem and propose a unified framework for this setting. To realize this formulation, we propose Multi-Anchor Projection Similarity (MAPS), a new metric which constructs an anchor plane from visual and textual query features in a high-dimensional space and measures similarity by the projection length of the target feature onto this plane. Unlike cosine similarity which evaluates isolated pairwise relations, MAPS captures the geometric consistency between the target feature and the joint query subspace, providing a more discriminative ranking criterion during retrieval. To make the learned representation consistent with this geometry, we further introduce a MAPS-based contrastive loss that drives target features toward the corresponding anchor plane. The proposed framework, similarity metric, and training objective jointly yield state-of-the-art performance in VLGL.

</details>


### [168] [Venice-H1: Failure-Aware Query Re-Ranking with Multi-Scale Grid Signatures for Referring Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22546)
*Nicolò Savioli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Venice-H1，一种轻量级后处理重排序模块，用于解决指代图像分割中候选掩码选择瓶颈问题，在仅增加11.3M参数和<1ms延迟的情况下，将失败样本的mIoU提升0.89-1.40，且有害切换率低于0.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RIS系统虽能生成多个候选掩码，但最终选择仅依赖简单的启发式规则（如argmax检测分数），这在高成功率的表面下掩盖了7-18%样本的失败问题，成为主要的误差瓶颈。

Method: 提出Venice-H1，一种与RIS骨干解耦的后处理重排序模块：首先通过多尺度网格签名（4x4, 8x8, 16x16）池化每个候选掩码的紧凑空间描述子，然后输入基于Transformer的重排序器，并引入Failure Gate（ROCAUC 0.78-0.82），仅在默认选择可能次优时才进行干预。

Result: 在DeRIS-L和DeRIS-B上实现delta_fail分别为+1.40和+0.89 mIoU，16/16（分割、骨干）组合上95%置信区间均为正，有害切换率低于0.53%；零样本迁移到医学指代分割（MS-CXR、M3D-RefSeg-2D）获得+1.16和+0.51 mIoU提升。

Conclusion: Venice-H1作为一种轻量级、与骨干解耦的后处理模块，能有效填补最佳候选掩码选择的理论差距（3-11% mIoU），且具有良好的跨领域零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Modern Referring Image Segmentation (RIS) systems generate multiple candidate masks per expression but rely on a simple heuristic--typically the argmax detection score--to select the final output. We identify query selection as a failure-case bottleneck: although heuristic selection succeeds on 82-93% of samples, the residual 7-18% of failures dominate the error budget, leaving a best-query selection gap of 3-11% mIoU. We introduce Venice-H1, a lightweight, backbone-decoupled post-hoc re-ranking module that encodes each candidate through multi-scale grid signatures--compact spatial descriptors pooled onto 4x4, 8x8, and 16x16 grids--and feeds them to a Transformer-based re-ranker with a Failure Gate (ROCAUC 0.78-0.82) that intervenes only when the default choice is likely suboptimal. Instantiated on DeRIS-L and DeRIS-B, Venice-H1 achieves delta_fail of +1.40 and +0.89 mIoU with strictly positive 95% CIs on all 16/16 (split, backbone) pairs and harmful-switch rates below 0.53%. Zero-shot transfer to medical referring segmentation (MS-CXR, M3D-RefSeg-2D) yields +1.16 and +0.51 mIoU without RIS-backbone fine-tuning. The module adds approximately 11.3M parameters and under 1 ms latency.

</details>


### [169] [Training-Free Semantic Correction for Autoregressive Visual Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22550)
*Junhao Chen,Chanyu Zhu,Zheqi Lv,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Gazer框架，通过集成多模态大语言模型反馈到自回归视觉模型的采样循环中，实现生成过程中的语义错误诊断与修正，无需额外训练即可提升图像和视频合成的语义对齐与组合准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视觉模型（AVM）基于下一尺度预测进行生成，但离散尺度分解使得语义错误难以识别和修正；训练增强方法计算成本高，而现有无训练方法忽略了中间生成状态，导致语义错误未被诊断并累积到最终输出。

Method: Gazer框架包含两个协作阶段：1）反思诊断阶段（Reflective Diagnosis），从中间生成状态诊断语义错误；2）语义修正阶段（Semantic Correction），回退并纠正生成轨迹以重新对齐目标提示。该方法将多模态大语言模型反馈集成到AVM采样循环中。

Result: 在组合性图像和视频基准测试上，Gazer在无需额外训练的情况下，在多种AVM上提升了语义对齐和组合准确性。

Conclusion: Gazer是一种有效的无训练范式方法，通过在生成过程中诊断和修正语义错误，显著提升了AVM的生成质量，避免了训练方法的计算开销，且具有跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive visual models (AVMs) based on next-scale prediction have emerged as a prominent paradigm for image and video synthesis. However, decomposing the generation process into discrete scales with varying granularities in AVM makes semantic errors difficult to identify and correct, thereby undermining the quality of the final output. Prior efforts to enhance AVM can be categorized into training-based and training-free approaches. Although training-based efforts to enhance AVM generation quality come at substantial computational cost, existing training-free methods neglect intermediate generation states, leaving semantic errors undiagnosed and allowing them to accumulate into the final output. In this paper, we focus on training-free paradigms and propose Gazer, a framework that integrates multimodal large language model feedback into the AVM sampling loop for in-generation semantic correction. Concretely, Gazer operates via two cooperating stages: the Reflective Diagnosis stage diagnoses semantic errors from intermediate states, while the Semantic Correction stage rewinds and rectifies the generation trajectory to realign with the target prompt. Experiments on compositional image and video benchmarks demonstrate that Gazer improves semantic alignment and compositional accuracy across multiple AVMs without additional training.

</details>


### [170] [HiMatch-AD: DINOv3-driven Hierarchical Matching for Training-free Medical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22556)
*Jiayu Huo,Jingyuan Hong,Meng Zhou,Liyun Chen,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiMatch-AD，一种基于DINOv3的分层匹配框架，通过双分支检索正常参考、多阶段分层生成异常图以及不确定性融合机制，在无需任务特定训练的情况下实现医学图像异常检测，并在BMAD基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于训练的方法需要大量正常数据和任务特定优化，扩展性差；而免训练方法依赖简单的最近邻检索和朴素聚合策略，无法捕获层次化语义且忽略多异常响应的可靠性。

Method: 1) 双分支匹配检索语义相关的正常参考（全局CLS-token相似度+patch级表示）；2) 跨多个Transformer阶段通过聚类正常特征与查询表示比较生成层次化异常图；3) 基于不确定性的融合机制自适应加权聚合多级异常图。

Result: 在脑MRI、肝CT、视网膜OCT数据集上，HiMatch-AD一致优于基于训练的方法和DINO-based SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 多级匹配和不确定性感知融合对于可扩展的医学异常检测是有效的，免训练框架兼具高性能与灵活性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is essential for medical image analysis, where pathological regions often appear as rare deviations from normal anatomical structures. While training-based methods have achieved promising performance, they require task-specific optimization and extensive normal data, which limits scalability across modalities and institutions. Training-free approaches offer greater flexibility by leveraging pretrained visual representations, yet existing methods typically rely on simple nearest-neighbor retrieval and naive aggregation strategies, which may fail to capture hierarchical semantics and ignore the reliability of multiple anomaly responses. In this work, we propose HiMatch-AD, a DINOv3-driven hierarchical matching framework for training-free medical anomaly detection. Our method first retrieves semantically relevant normal references via dual-branch matching that jointly considers global CLS-token similarity and patch-level representations. Hierarchical anomaly maps are then generated across multiple transformer stages by comparing clustered normal features with query representations. To robustly aggregate anomaly responses, we introduce a unified uncertainty-based fusion mechanism that adaptively weights maps according to their reliability. The entire framework operates without any task-specific training. Extensive experiments on the BMAD benchmark, including brain MRI, liver CT, and retinal OCT datasets, demonstrate that HiMatch-AD consistently outperforms both training-based and DINO-based state-of-the-art methods, which highlights the effectiveness of multi-level matching and uncertainty-aware fusion for scalable medical anomaly detection.

</details>


### [171] [SeFi-Image: A Text-to-Image Foundation Model with Semantic-First Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22568)
*SeFi-Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeFi-Image，一种基于语义优先扩散的文生图基础模型，在1B/2B/5B三种规模下仅用125K A800 GPU小时（约为Z-Image的10-20%训练算力）训练，即可达到与Qwen-Image和Z-Image相当或更优的性能，并提供DMD2蒸馏的少步数加速变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练图像生成基础模型消耗大量算力资源，前期方法虽尝试利用语义引导加速训练，但仅在ImageNet等简单数据集、低分辨率和小规模模型上进行实验，缺乏在复杂文生图任务上的验证。

Method: 提出语义优先扩散（Semantic-First Diffusion）这一新型潜扩散建模范式，构建SeFi-Image模型，在1B、2B、5B三种参数量级上实例化，并采用DMD2蒸馏得到少步数turbo变体以适应不同硬件和延迟需求。

Result: 5B模型仅用125K A800 GPU小时训练（约为Z-Image的10-20%算力），在GenEval、DPG、LongTextBench、OneIG、CVTG-2K等多个基准上达到与Qwen-Image和Z-Image相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 语义优先扩散模型能显著降低文生图基础模型的训练算力需求，同时保持强竞争力；公开代码和权重可为社区提供实用见解和可部署的模型选项。

Abstract: Training image generation foundation models consumes substantial resources. Previous methods have attempted to leverage semantic guidance to accelerate the training process, yet their experiments were only conducted on simple datasets such as ImageNet, at low resolutions, and with small-scale models. In this paper, we propose SeFi-Image, a text-to-image foundation model built upon semantic-first diffusion, a novel latent diffusion modeling paradigm. We instantiate SeFi-Image at three model scales, 1B, 2B, and 5B parameters, enabling systematic study of scaling behavior and flexible deployment under varying compute budgets. Notably, our largest 5B model was trained with merely 125K A800 GPU hours, corresponding to roughly 10-20% of the training compute used by Z-Image. However, it achieves results comparable to or even superior to Qwen-Image and Z-Image. Despite this modest training compute, SeFi-Image achieves strong performance on a wide range of benchmarks, including GenEval, DPG, LongTextBench, OneIG, and CVTG-2K. Moreover, we provide DMD2-distilled few-step turbo variants for each model scale to accommodate diverse hardware constraints and latency requirements. We publicly release our code, weights and hope this work offers the community useful insights into semantic-guided diffusion modeling for T2I generation, while also providing practical and readily deployable model options.

</details>


### [172] [The Power of Light: Improving Synthetic-to-Real Domain Adaptation through Physically-Based Indirect Illumination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22574)
*Hooman Tavakoli Ghinani,Tatjana Legler,Martin Ruskowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了光照配置和背景复杂度对合成数据域迁移的影响，提出基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim和PBS的自动化合成数据生成管线SmartSDG及工业基准数据集ILLUM_INTRUCK，通过YOLOv12实验证明复杂间接光照与领域相关背景变化能丰富视觉线索、减少域差距、降低误检并加速收敛，最终提供了虚拟场景设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据生成虽解决了人工标注瓶颈，但合成到真实的域差距需要优化渲染变量，现有研究缺乏对光照和背景配置的系统性分析。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim和Physically-Based Shading (PBS)构建自动化可复现管线SmartSDG，创建多目标工业基准数据集ILLUM_INTRUCK，设计18组对照实验，使用YOLOv12框架进行定量评估。

Result: 复杂间接光照搭配领域相关背景变化能显著增强视觉线索丰富性；避免直接镜面峰值可保留关键表面纹理、缓解域差距、减少误检并加速模型收敛。

Conclusion: 提供了最大化工业自动化中目标检测鲁棒性的可操作虚拟场景设计指南，证明间接光照优于传统直接光照合成数据的有效性。

Abstract: While synthetic data generation resolves the manual labeling bottleneck in computer vision, minimizing the syn-to-real domain gap requires optimizing rendering variables. This paper presents a systematic study analyzing the impact of lighting configurations and background complexity on object detection performance. We introduce SmartSDG, an automated, reproducible pipeline built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim using Physically-Based Shading (PBS), alongside ILLUM\_INTRUCK, a new multi-object industrial benchmark dataset. Through 18 controlled experiments utilizing a state-of-the-art YOLOv12 framework, we demonstrate that complex, indirect lighting configurations paired with domain-relevant background variability significantly increase visual cue richness. Our quantitative findings show that avoiding direct specular peaks preserves crucial surface textures, mitigates the domain gap, reduces false positives, and accelerates model convergence compared to using conventional direct-light synthetic data. Ultimately, we provide actionable virtual scene design guidelines to maximize object detection robustness in industrial automation.

</details>


### [173] [MapReason-OSM: Can Vision-Language Models Make Graph-Verifiable Mobility Decisions from Street Maps ?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22597)
*Srinivas Venkatanarayanan,Clement Pakkam Isaac*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MapReason-OSM基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在地图阅读中的图可验证决策能力。通过在OpenStreetMap渲染的固定风格地图上，结合隐藏街道图与精确预言机，构建6000个实例（12000个面板）覆盖12种任务。评估7个VLM发现：模型能简单路径规划但无法进行图成本推理（单设施定位接近随机），且跨尺度一致性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图基准多评测自由文本或多选题回答，无法在底层图上验证。实际物流、配送、无障碍导航等场景需要输出可操作决策（路线、定位、停车选择），这些决策必须遵守道路网络约束，但缺乏可图验证的评估方法。

Method: 1) 从OpenStreetMap渲染10个美国城市中心区的固定风格地图，对齐两个缩放尺度；2) 叠加一致标记语法；3) 为每个面板配隐藏街道图和精确预言机；4) 构建6000个实例（两尺度共12000个面板），覆盖12种路径规划、设施选址和视觉消歧任务；5) 模型输出结构化决策后映射回图，评估有效性、合法性、最优性、约束满足度和跨尺度一致性。

Result: 7个VLM在简单路径规划上表现尚可，但在图成本推理任务上严重失败（单设施定位接近随机水平，即使前沿推理模型也不例外），且频繁出现尺度不一致问题。

Conclusion: MapReason-OSM揭示了当前VLM在需要图结构理解的可验证移动决策上的根本局限，特别是缺乏对图成本推理和跨尺度一致性的能力，为未来研究提供了明确的方向和评估工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to read maps for logistics, delivery, and accessible navigation, where the output is an actionable decision (a route, a pin, a parking choice) that must respect the road network. Yet most map benchmarks grade free-text or multiple-choice answers that cannot be verified against the underlying graph. We present \textbf{MapReason-OSM}, a benchmark and evaluation harness for graph-verifiable mobility decisions on self-rendered OpenStreetMap panels. We render fixed-style maps for ten U.S. downtowns at two aligned zoom scales, overlay a consistent marker grammar, and pair each panel with a hidden street graph and exact oracles, yielding 6{,}000 instances (12{,}000 panels across the two zooms) over 12 routing, facility-location, and visual-disambiguation tasks. Models return structured decisions that we snap back to the graph and score for validity, legality, optimality, and constraint satisfaction, plus \emph{cross-zoom consistency}. Across seven VLMs, models read maps and route simply but fail at graph-cost reasoning (single-facility pin placement is near chance even for frontier reasoning models), and are frequently scale-inconsistent. We release the benchmark, harness, and deterministic generator.

</details>


### [174] [Automated sign detection across the Electronic Babylonian Library: A large-scale dataset and end-to-end cuneiform OCR pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22608)
*Wentao Che,Esteban Garcés Arias,Asim Niaz,Andreas Bender,Enrique Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文使用基于Deformable DETR的目标检测模型，在最大规模的楔形文字符号数据集上实现了符号检测，无需语言先验即可对87,668块泥板碎片产生近290万次符号检测，较先前工作提升28-37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 楔形文字泥板数量庞大（约50万块），但仅有极少部分被亚述学家分析，亟需计算机视觉方法辅助释读，而现有方法受限于大规模标注数据的缺乏。

Method: 采用Deformable DETR目标检测模型，在173类和106类两种粒度下评估；集成自动泥板侧面提取、启发式行分组、基于n-gram的文本相似度评估，将视觉符号检测与文本结构联系起来。

Result: 在COCO风格检测指标上较先前工作持续提升28-37%；对eBL语料库中87,668块泥板碎片进行推理，产生近290万次符号检测。

Conclusion: 该方法无需语言先验，虽对泥板损坏和布局变化敏感，但为语料库级别的楔形文字分析提供了可扩展、可解释的基础，并支持未来与多模态和语言建模框架的集成。

Abstract: Learning to read cuneiform tablets is an extremely demanding task; consequently, of the roughly half million excavated tablets, only a small fraction has been analysed by Assyriologists. Computer vision offers a promising avenue for decipherment but requires large, densely annotated datasets. To address this limitation, the largest annotated cuneiform sign dataset to date is used, and a Deformable Detection Transformer (DETR)-based object detection model is evaluated under two class granularities of 173 and 106 classes. The proposed system integrates automatic tablet-side extraction, heuristic line grouping, and n-gram-based textual similarity evaluation to bridge visual sign detection and textual structure, and achieves consistent improvements of up to 28-37% over prior work on COCO-style detection metrics. At inference, the method is applied to 87,668 tablet fragments from the Electronic Babylonian Library (eBL) corpus, producing nearly 2.9 million sign detections. Although the approach operates without linguistic priors and remains sensitive to tablet damage and layout variability, it provides a scalable and interpretable foundation for corpus-wide cuneiform analysis and supports future integration with multimodal and linguistic modelling frameworks.

</details>


### [175] [OmniSpace: Efficient Geometry Awareness for Autonomous Vehicles MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22617)
*Hao Vo,Phu Loc Nguyen,Khoa Vo,Sieu Tran,Duc Minh Nguyen,Ngo Xuan Cuong,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Anh Nguyen,Duy Minh Ho Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniSpace是一种即插即用的范式，通过引入相机姿态注入器、多视图极线注意力模块和3D几何蒸馏目标，将几何知识迁移到MLLM中，仅从2D观测即可实现几何感知的空间推理，在自动驾驶相关基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在2D视觉任务上表现优异，但空间智能不足；现有的几何感知MLLM依赖推理时的辅助3D模型，导致管线复杂且存在级联失败风险。作者发现当前MLLM的瓶颈在于弱跨视图对应关系和深度估计能力。

Method: 提出OmniSpace即插即用范式，包含三个关键组件：1) Camera Pose Injector（相机姿态注入器）；2) Multi-view Epipolar Attention module（多视图极线注意力模块）；3) 3D Geometric Distillation objective（3D几何蒸馏目标），三者共同将几何知识迁移到模型中。

Result: 在规划基准（nuScenes, Bench2Drive）、风险检测（nuInstruct）、语言（Omnidrive）和泛化（DriveBench）上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: OmniSpace通过仅从2D观测进行几何感知空间推理，有效提升了MLLM的空间智能，避免了传统方法中依赖辅助3D模型的复杂性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on 2D visual tasks, yet enhancing their spatial intelligence for real-world applications such as Autonomous Vehicles (AV) remains an open challenge. Existing geometry-aware MLLMs typically rely on auxiliary 3D models at inference time, introducing pipeline complexity and the risk of cascading failures. In this paper, we present OmniSpace, a simple yet effective plug-and-play paradigm for geometry-aware spatial reasoning from purely 2D observations. Motivated by our finding that current MLLMs are bottlenecked by weak cross-view correspondence and depth estimation, OmniSpace introduces a Camera Pose Injector, a Multi-view Epipolar Attention module, and a 3D Geometric Distillation objective that jointly address these two limitations by transferring geometric knowledge into the model. Extensive experiments show that OmniSpace surpasses existing methods on planning benchmarks (nuScenes, Bench2Drive), risk detection (nuInstruct), language (Omnidrive), and generalization (DriveBench).

</details>


### [176] [DR-Mamba: Automatic Inference-Time Domain Adaptation for Document Image Binarization via Sample-Conditioned Detail-Background Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22625)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DR-Mamba是一种无需微调、无需梯度更新的推理时自适应文档图像二值化方法，通过输入依赖门控和快慢双路径路由实现跨域背景抑制，在DIBCO基准上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的二值化方法对未见过的退化域（如纸张老化、污渍、阴影等）泛化能力差，而测试时自适应方法需要梯度更新或辅助数据，效率低下。

Method: 提出DR-Mamba，将Mamba选择性扫描重解释为快慢双路径路由：快速细节路径捕获局部笔画结构，慢速背景路径累积空间持久退化响应；两路径通过输入依赖的减法门控集成以显式抑制背景干扰；并加入全分辨率细节引导重建和细笔画感知监督。

Result: 在留一年份验证协议下，DR-Mamba在DIBCO风格基准上对每个文档、每个位置进行减法抑制，显著提升了跨域鲁棒性，尤其在退化最严重的保留折上表现突出。

Conclusion: DR-Mamba通过样本条件化的细节-背景抑制框架，实现了单次前向传播的自动化推理时域适应，无需标签、微调或参数更新即可处理未知退化域的二值化任务。
