Abstract: Degraded document image binarization is sensitive to domain shifts caused by paper aging, bleed-through, stains, shadows, and uneven illumination, and the foreground-background separation of recent learning-based methods can become unstable on unseen degradation domains. We propose DR-Mamba, a sample-conditioned detail-background suppression framework that performs automatic inference-time domain adaptation for document image binarization. Unlike test-time adaptation methods that require gradient updates or auxiliary data at inference, DR-Mamba adapts to each input document through input-dependent gates within a single forward pass, requiring no target-domain labels, no fine-tuning, and no test-time parameter updates. Instead of using Mamba-style selective scanning as a single generic feature path, DR-Mamba reinterprets it as fast-slow route modeling: a fast detail route captures local stroke structures, while a slow background route accumulates spatially persistent degradation responses. The two routes are integrated through an input-dependent subtractive gate that explicitly suppresses background interference rather than fusing features by addition or concatenation. We further add full-resolution detail-guided reconstruction and thin-stroke-aware supervision to recover fine strokes lost during downsampling. Evaluated under a leave-one-year-out protocol on DIBCO-style benchmarks, where each held-out year is treated as an unseen degradation domain, DR-Mamba shows that per-document, per-location subtractive suppression improves cross-domain robustness, with particularly strong performance on the most severely degraded held-out fold.

</details>


### [177] [4DVLT: Dynamic Scene Understanding with Worldline-Centered Vision-Language Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22631)
*Chaoyue Li,Boxue Yang,Shengyao Zhou,Haoyang Wu,Rui Qian,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4DVLT任务（基于指令的4D动态场景理解）及配套基准Instruct-4D（129.4K问答对，64.7K目标实体，851场景，9类推理查询），并设计4DTrack模型，通过目标中心4D状态图、度量引导路由、双向解码和运动学校准实现世界线推理。4DTrack-Qwen3.5-9B在TGA_Top1上达62.68，超出最强VLT基线19.62点，验证了世界线中心建模对目标定位和世界线恢复的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多模态模型虽能进行丰富视觉推理但缺乏度量拓扑保持，而视觉-语言跟踪局限于碎片化的2D/3D输出和局部延续，无法实现对4D动态场景中持久世界线（绑定身份、度量3D运动和多视角2D投影）的完整理解。

Method: 1) 提出4DVLT任务：指令条件化的全观测多视角视频4D动态场景理解；2) 构建Instruct-4D基准：含129.4K问答对、64.7K目标实体、851场景和9类推理查询；3) 设计4DTrack模型：将指令条件化跟踪建模为图条件化世界线推理，通过目标中心4D状态图、度量引导路由、双向解码和运动学校准实现。

Result: 4DTrack-Qwen3.5-9B在Instruct-4D上达到62.68 TGA_Top1，超越最佳适配VLT基线19.62个点。

Conclusion: 世界线中心建模显著提升目标定位和恢复的世界线质量，验证了该方法在4D动态场景理解中的有效性。

Abstract: 4D dynamic scene understanding requires grounding language to a persistent worldline that binds identity, metric 3D motion, and synchronized multi-view 2D projections. Existing paradigms capture only part of this structure: large multimodal models reason over rich visual evidence but rarely preserve metric topology, while vision-language tracking remains tied to fragmented 2D or 3D outputs and local continuation. We therefore introduce \textbf{4DVLT}, a worldline-centered task for instruction-conditioned 4D dynamic scene understanding in fully observed multi-view video, and \textbf{Instruct-4D}, a benchmark with 129.4K question-answer pairs, 64.7K target entities, 851 scenes, and 9 reasoning-oriented query types. To address this setting, we present \textbf{4DTrack}, which casts instruction-conditioned tracking as graph-conditioned worldline inference through an object-centric 4D state graph, metric-guided routing, bidirectional decoding, and kinematic calibration. On Instruct-4D, 4DTrack-Qwen3.5-9B reaches 62.68 $\mathrm{TGA}_{\mathrm{Top1}}$ and surpasses the best adapted VLT baseline by 19.62 points. These results show that worldline-centered modeling improves both target grounding and recovered worldline quality. The project page is available at https://github.com/mikubaka88/4DVLT.

</details>


### [178] [Learning Entropy Signature for Image Representation and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22634)
*Jan Glaser,Ivo Bukovsky,Noriyasu Homma,Marcel Jirina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出学习熵签名(LES)，一种基于空间学习熵图(SLEM)中K个最大学习熵位置的图像描述符，用于紧凑的图像表示和分类，实验表明少量最大LE位置即可保留大量判别信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将学习熵从时间序列分析扩展到图像分析，并进一步从结构点提取推进到紧凑的图像表示和分类，探索神经权重学习与信息相关性之间的密切关系。

Method: 通过预训练的前馈MLP网络以固定空间顺序逐样本学习局部像素邻域来预测中心像素，生成二维空间学习熵图(SLEM)；然后从中选取K个最大学习熵位置构成学习熵签名(LES)，作为紧凑的图像描述符。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，少量K个最大LE位置即可保留显著的判别信息，验证了LES作为紧凑图像表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 学习熵可从时间序列有效扩展到图像领域，并且从结构点提取进一步推进到紧凑图像表示与分类；神经权重学习与信息相关性之间存在密切联系。

Abstract: Learning Entropy (LE) has recently been extended to image analysis through Spatial Learning Entropy Maps (SLEMs), which are two-dimensional LE distributions that highlight unusually high learning activity across an image. Unlike conventional image descriptors, SLEMs are generated by incremental, sample-wise learning of a pretrained feedforward MLP network, where local pixel neighborhoods are presented sequentially in a fixed spatial order to predict the corresponding central pixels. Consequently, the learning activity at each image location depends not only on its local structure but also on the knowledge acquired from previously processed locations. This paper introduces Learning Entropy Signatures (LES), an image descriptor derived from SLEM using the K largest LE locations. LES captures the spatial organization of learning-relevant image structures and provides a compact representation of image content based on learning weight behavior. Experimental evaluation on image classification tasks shows that a relatively small number of K largest LE locations preserve substantial discriminative information. The results indicate a close relationship between the learning of neural weights and information relevance, extending the role of Learning Entropy from time series to images and, within images, from structural point extraction to compact image representation and classification.

</details>


### [179] [MaRS: Robust Out-of-Distribution Detection via Mahalanobis Residual Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22649)
*Francesco Di Salvo,Sebastian Doerrich,Christian Ledig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MaRS（马氏残差评分），一种基于轻量级自编码器和马氏距离的后验OOD检测方法，通过捕捉重建残差中的各向异性结构，在多种医学影像模态和分布偏移场景下优于现有的置信度、距离和重建基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学图像上表现优异，但在分布偏移下可靠性下降。现有重建式OOD检测方法在潜在特征空间中表现不佳，作者认为问题不在于重建质量，而在于评分方式——标准L2残差范数会压碎各向异性的残差结构，丢失信息性偏差。

Method: 提出MaRS方法：使用轻量级自编码器学习分布内流形，然后计算重建残差的马氏距离（Mahalanobis distance），得到方差感知的OOD评分，无需标签信息。

Result: 在三种成像模态、多种分布偏移类型、不同模型家族和规模下，MaRS优于置信度、距离和重建基线方法，且完全后验、轻量级。

Conclusion: 重建式方法在潜在空间中的局限性源于评分方式而非重建质量；MaRS通过马氏距离捕捉残差各向异性结构，有效提升OOD检测性能。

Abstract: Foundation models provide highly descriptive representations for medical images, yet their reliability degrades under distribution shifts arising from changes in patients, devices, or acquisition conditions. Reliable out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is therefore essential for safe deployment. Recent post-hoc detectors efficiently exploit frozen embeddings (\emph{e.g.}, kNN), whereas reconstruction-based OOD detection in latent feature space has seen limited adoption due to inconsistent performance. In this work, we show that the limitation of reconstruction-based methods in latent space does not stem from poor reconstruction quality, but from how reconstruction errors are scored. Standard $L_2$ residual norms collapse the anisotropic residual structure, thereby suppressing informative deviations. To address this limitation, we introduce \texttt{MaRS} (Mahalanobis Residual Scoring), a label-free OOD detector that learns an in-distribution manifold using a lightweight autoencoder and measures deviation via a Mahalanobis distance on reconstruction residuals, yielding variance-aware OOD scores. Across three imaging modalities, multiple types of distribution shift, and different model families and scales, \texttt{MaRS} outperforms established confidence-, distance-, and reconstruction-based baselines, while remaining fully post-hoc and lightweight. The code is available at https://github.com/francescodisalvo05/mars.

</details>


### [180] [Prompting Diffusion Models for Zero-Shot Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22660)
*Irem Zeynep Alagöz,Nils Morbitzer,Andrea Ramazzina,Nassir Navab,Federico Tombari,Stefano Gasperini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Prompt2Seg提出了一种空间条件化框架，通过向冻结的扩散分割模型添加条件分支，将空间提示（2D高斯或置信度图）作为显式输入信号，使模型能直接响应用户意图。在有限类别上微调后，该模型零样本泛化到多种未见过的物体类型和视觉域，在七个数据集上持续优于底层扩散分割骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提示分割的基础模型难以准确分割物体区域，存在假阳性和过分割问题；而早期利用生成先验的方法仅在后期处理中使用提示，导致分割结果次优，因为过程与用户输入无关。

Method: Prompt2Seg是一个基于扩散的分割空间条件化框架，包含：1）冻结的扩散分割骨干模型；2）新增的条件分支，将空间提示（2D高斯或置信度图）作为显式输入信号，训练模型直接响应用户意图。在Hypersim和Virtual KITTI 2的有限物体类别上微调。

Result: 在七个数据集（包括标准基准和更具挑战性的绘画、自我中心视图、X射线数据等域）上评估，Prompt2Seg在所有基准测试中持续优于底层扩散分割骨干网络，并零样本泛化到广泛的未见物体类型和视觉域。

Conclusion: 生成式预训练中编码的丰富先验与原则性的空间条件化相结合，为无需大规模掩码监督即可实现广泛泛化的交互式分割提供了一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: Several disruptive research directions have recently emerged in computer vision, including foundation models achieving previously unseen zero-shot performance in scene understanding, even interactively, and generative models that synthesize extremely realistic images. The latter have also been shown to be highly effective in scene understanding tasks thanks to their rich priors. However, for promptable segmentation, foundation models struggle with accurately segmenting an object's region, leading to false positives and over-segmentation. Notably, early attempts that leverage generative priors use prompts only during post-processing, yielding suboptimal segments because the process is agnostic to the user input. In this paper, we target these limitations with Prompt2Seg, a spatial conditioning framework for diffusion-based segmentation. Prompt2Seg augments a frozen diffusion segmentation model with a conditioning branch. Our approach takes spatial prompts, represented as 2D Gaussians or confidence maps, as explicit input signals, training the model to respond directly to user intent. Fine-tuned on a deliberately constrained set of object categories drawn from Hypersim and Virtual KITTI 2, Prompt2Seg generalizes zero-shot to a wide range of unseen object types and visual domains. We evaluate on seven datasets ranging from standard benchmarks to more challenging domains, including paintings, egocentric views, and X-ray data. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Prompt2Seg consistently outperforms the underlying diffusion segmentation backbone across all benchmarks. Our results suggest that the rich priors encoded in generative pretraining, combined with principled spatial conditioning, offer a compelling path toward broadly generalizing interactive segmentation without large-scale mask supervision.

</details>


### [181] [SATURN: Symbolic Spatial Reasoning for Multi-Perspective Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22694)
*Danial Kamali,Tanawan Premsri,Shreya Rajpal,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Parisa Kordjamshidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SATURN是一个神经符号框架，通过重建3D场景、推导软性视角空间谓词，并用Python符号执行器进行组合推理，实现了对视角感知的组合空间推理，在3D FORCE和MindCube基准上显著优于现有VLM和空间模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要组合关系推理（尤其是依赖参考框架的空间关系）时表现不可靠；神经符号方法虽然提升了推理可解释性，但依赖脆弱的几何程序和噪声感知上的硬决策。

Method: SATURN包含三个阶段：1）重建近似3D场景；2）推导软性（soft）视角感知的空间谓词；3）使用无训练的Python符号执行器进行组合推理，将感知与推理分离，并通过多跳推理保留不确定性。同时提出诊断基准3D FORCE，控制推理深度、视角和视角组合。

Result: 在3D FORCE上，VLM和空间训练模型随深度和视角复杂度增加性能急剧下降，而SATURN保持稳定并超越强基线；在真实世界MindCube基准上，SATURN达到78.57%总体准确率，超过最强基线14个百分点。

Conclusion: 将感知与推理分离、保留不确定性的软性符号方法在处理视角依赖的组合空间推理任务上比端到端VLM更鲁棒和有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain unreliable when spatial reasoning requires composing relations whose meanings depend on frames of reference. Existing neuro-symbolic methods make reasoning more explicit, but often depend on brittle geometric procedures and hard decisions over noisy perception. We propose SATURN, a neuro-symbolic framework for perspective-aware compositional spatial reasoning. SATURN reconstructs an approximate 3D scene, derives soft perspective-aware spatial predicates, and composes them with a training-free Pythonic symbolic executor, separating perception from reasoning while preserving uncertainty through multi-hop inference. We also introduce 3D FORCE, a diagnostic benchmark that controls reasoning depth, view, and perspective composition across spatial arrangement grounding (SAG) and referring expression grounding (REF). On 3D FORCE, VLMs and spatially trained models degrade sharply as depth and perspective complexity increase, whereas SATURN remains stable and outperforms strong baselines. On the real-world MindCube benchmark, SATURN achieves 78.57% overall accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14 pp.

</details>


### [182] [NullFlow: One-Step Generative Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22696)
*Xiao Shi,Edward P. Chandler,Chicago Y. Park,Shirin Shoushtari,Ulugbek S. Kamilov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NullFlow提出了一种单步生成图像重建框架，通过将生成流限制在测量一致子空间中，无需单独的数据保真度校正，在单次网络评估中完成采样，在图像修复任务上匹配了最先进的扩散求解器性能，同时将推理从数百次网络评估减少到一次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的逆问题求解器需要多次网络评估和单独的数据保真度校正步骤，计算开销大。本文旨在设计一种无需迭代、无需额外校正的单步生成重建方法。

Method: 将生成流限制在测量一致子空间中，使流永远不会离开该子空间，从而无需数据保真度校正；通过学习流的平均速度实现单次网络评估采样，避免了传统流匹配方法的逐步积分。证明了该约束流的平均速度对应的训练目标的全局最优解是一个单步后验采样器。

Result: 在图像修复任务上，NullFlow匹配了最先进的扩散求解器性能，同时将推理成本从数百次网络评估降低到仅一次。

Conclusion: NullFlow是一种高效的单步生成图像重建框架，通过测量一致子空间中的约束流实现了无需迭代、无需额外校正的后验采样，大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: We propose NullFlow, a principled framework for one-step generative image reconstruction. Our key idea is to confine the generative flow to a measurement-consistent subspace. Because the flow never leaves this subspace, NullFlow needs no separate data-fidelity corrections, unlike existing solvers. NullFlow samples in a single network evaluation by learning the flow's average velocity, avoiding the step-by-step integration of traditional flow matching methods. We prove that the average velocity of this constrained flow yields a training objective whose global minimizer is a one-step posterior sampler. We show on image inpainting that NullFlow matches state-of-the-art diffusion solvers while cutting inference from hundreds of network evaluations to one.

</details>


### [183] [Catching Lies Without Sending the Video: Privacy-Preserving Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22699)
*Nikita Sharma,Pranav Sara,Karan Singla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Whissle通过提取轻量级语音和视觉特征摘要（转录、情绪、年龄、性别、意图分布、欺骗意图过滤器、流畅度、节奏、面部行为、韵律），仅用7.8倍更少的token就达到与前沿多模态模型相当的欺骗检测性能（AUC 0.741-0.755），且无需将原始媒体数据传出设备。同时指出此前报告的75%准确率存在说话者泄露问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在欺骗检测任务中，是否必须传输完整的原始视频（面部+语音）给第三方模型，还是可以通过设备端提取轻量级摘要来实现同等性能，从而保护隐私并减少传输开销。

Method: 在Real-life Trial Deception数据集上，使用Whissle设备端语音和视觉栈提取紧凑摘要（包括转录、情绪、年龄、性别、意图分布、欺骗意图过滤器、流畅度、节奏、逐帧面部行为、韵律）。采用说话者独立的评估方式，分别训练小分类器和使用前沿LLM（Claude Opus 4.8）处理摘要进行分类。

Result: 小分类器在摘要上达到AUC 0.741，与Gemini 2.5 Pro处理完整视频的性能持平；Claude Opus 4.8处理摘要达到AUC 0.755，输入token减少7.8倍。同时发现此前文献中报告的75%准确率是说话者泄露导致的虚假结果。

Conclusion: 在欺骗检测任务中，设备端提取的紧凑摘要可以替代原始媒体传输，在匹配前沿模型性能的同时显著降低传输开销和保护隐私。评估中必须注意说话者泄露问题。

Abstract: Frontier multimodal models can guess whether a person is lying from a testimony video. To do so, they stream that raw face and voice to a third-party model. We ask whether the heavy media is needed at all. On the Real-life Trial Deception dataset, Whissle on-device speech and vision stack extracts a compact digest: transcript, emotion, age, gender, intent distributions, a deception intent filter, fluency and rhythm, per-frame facial behaviour, and prosody. Under speaker-independent evaluation, we report three findings. A small classifier on this digest reaches AUC 0.741, matching Gemini 2.5 Pro on full video. Handing the digest to a frontier LLM reaches AUC 0.755 with Claude Opus 4.8 at 7.8X fewer input tokens, with no media leaving the device. The reported 75% accuracy is a speaker-leakage artifact. We release code and experiments.

</details>


### [184] [Modular Diffusion Models for Structured Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22702)
*Siddhesh Khandelwal,Björn Ommer,Leonid Sigal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出模块化扩散模型（MDMs），将扩散过程分解为针对不同结构化信息空间（如目标类别、空间位置、关系）的任务特定模块，以学习结构化输出的分布，从而捕获预测不确定性，在目标检测、实例分割和场景图生成三项任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督方法在结构化视觉识别任务中产生确定性输出，无法捕获复杂视觉场景中固有的不确定性（如遮挡、边界模糊等歧义）以及训练数据的噪声和稀疏性。

Method: 提出模块化扩散模型（MDMs），将扩散过程分解为多个任务特定模块，每个模块专注于结构化信息空间的不同方面（目标类别、空间位置、实体间关系），各模块可独立学习，推理时无缝集成，且能处理异质输出空间。

Result: 在目标检测、实例分割和场景图生成三项结构化任务上取得了有优势的实验结果。

Conclusion: 模块化扩散模型通过分解扩散过程的方式，有效学习了结构化输出的分布，为处理结构化视觉任务中的不确定性提供了一种简单且通用的框架。

Abstract: Traditional supervised methods for structured visual recognition tasks -- such as object detection, segmentation, and scene graph generation -- often produce deterministic, fixed outputs, limiting their ability to capture the inherent uncertainty in complex visual scenes. As a consequence, such point estimates are unable to capture the prediction uncertainty (or multi modality) intrinsic to these problems, often arising from natural ambiguities (e.g., ambiguity in size of partially occluded objects, local ambiguity of exact segmentation boundary, etc.) as well as noise and sparsity of training data. To address this limitation, we present Modular Diffusion Models (MDMs), a simple and novel framework that learns a distribution over structured outputs for a given input image. MDMs decompose the diffusion process into distinct, task-specific modules, each focused on capturing a different aspect of the structured information space, such as object categories, spatial locations, and inter-object relationships. This modular design allows each component to be learned independently, with seamless integration at inference without additional training. Furthermore, the modularity of MDMs enables the diffusion process to easily operate over the heterogeneous output space common in many structured learning tasks (e.g., a continuous bounding boxes and discrete class labels). Experimental results over three distinct structured tasks -- object detection, instance segmentation, and scene graph generation -- highlight the benefits of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [185] [Generative Relightable Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22718)
*Kunwar Maheep Singh,Christian Theobalt,Rishabh Dabral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRA提出了一种混合生成式方法，结合物理基础的材质重光照与概率细化，实现全身体验人物的自由视角渲染与环境贴图重光照，在感知质量上超越先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细外观细节建模本质上是一个一对多的问题，完全回归式重光照方法难以处理，因此需要生成式方法来建模这种多模态分布。

Method: 从跟踪的动画网格出发，在UV空间优化材质参数并渲染粗重光照外观；然后使用前馈模型细化纹理以捕捉姿态相关纹理动态和非理想反射光照效果；最后通过微调的视频到视频扩散模型将物理渲染转换为时间一致的高细节视频，并采用错误回收策略生成长视频。

Result: 在感知质量上优于先前的重光照人体基线方法。

Conclusion: 混合物理重光照与生成式细化是解决可重光照人体渲染中一对多问题的有效途径。

Abstract: We present Generative Relightable Avatars (GRA), a person-specific method for photorealistic free-view rendering and environment-map relighting of full-body humans. We postulate that modeling fine-grained appearance details is inherently a one-to-many problem that can benefit from a generative formulation. In contrast to fully regressive relightable avatar methods, GRA follows a hybrid approach that combines controllable, physics-grounded relighting with probabilistic refinement. Starting from a tracked animated mesh, we optimize material parameters in UV-space and render a coarse relit appearance under a target HDR environment map. Next, we refine the textures with a feed-forward model to capture pose-dependent texture dynamics and illumination effects beyond simplified reflectance assumptions. Finally, a fine-tuned video-to-video diffusion model transforms the physically grounded renderings into temporally coherent, high-detail videos while preserving 3D control, with an error-recycling strategy for generating long videos. Experimental evaluations demonstrate our method's improved perceptual quality over prior relightable avatar baselines. Project Page: https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/GRA/

</details>


### [186] [Interpretable Uncertainty Routing Separating Emotion Ambiguity from Distribution Shift in Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22725)
*Keito Inoshita,Takato Ueno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出不确定性分解方法（将不确定性分为偶然不确定性和认知不确定性）用于面部表情识别，并引入不确定性感知路由（UAR）机制，在推理时根据不确定性类型对输入进行分类处理，从而区分标注歧义和分布偏移这两种不同情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部表情识别中标注歧义和分布偏移需要不同的下游处理方式（歧义需报告不确定性，偏移需拒绝输入），但单一不确定性分数无法区分二者，因此需要将不确定性分解为偶然（aleatoric）和认知（epistemic）成分以实现差异化处理。

Method: 使用深度集成微调的DINOv2模型获取偶然不确定性和认知不确定性；通过双验证协议分别验证：用标注者分歧验证偶然不确定性，用图像损坏引起的分布偏移验证认知不确定性；提出不确定性感知路由（UAR）机制，在推理时根据不确定性类型路由输入。

Result: 偶然不确定性与标注者分歧的Spearman相关系数为0.66（95% CI: 0.64-0.68）；认知不确定性检测图像损坏引起的分布偏移，在最严重损坏级别平均AUROC为0.699；UAR在匹配的分布外拒绝率下保留约1.8倍更多的歧义分布内面部图像。

Conclusion: 不确定性分解的价值在于分离不同类型的 uncertainty 以实现可解释的差异化动作选择，这是单一不确定性分数或标签分布学习方法无法实现的。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition (FER) is inherently ambiguous: human annotators frequently disagree, and models deployed in real environments face distribution shift. Crucially, these two conditions demand different downstream actions, as ambiguous in-distribution faces should be reported with their ambiguity whereas out-of-distribution inputs should be rejected. However, a single uncertainty score conflates the two. In this study, uncertainty decomposition into aleatoric and epistemic components for FER is investigated, and Uncertainty-Aware Routing (UAR), an inference-time routing mechanism that exploits the separation, is introduced. Specifically, aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties are obtained from a Deep Ensemble of fully fine-tuned DINOv2 models and are each validated against an independent external signal: aleatoric against human annotator disagreement, and epistemic against distribution shift induced by image corruptions. The proposed dual-validation protocol reveals that aleatoric recovers annotator disagreement with Spearman correlation 0.66 (95% CI: 0.64-0.68), and epistemic detects corruption-induced shifts, achieving average AUROC of 0.699 at the highest corruption severity. UAR retains approximately 1.8 times more ambiguous in-distribution faces than single-uncertainty routing at a matched out-of-distribution rejection rate. A strong label-distribution-learning baseline achieves comparable disagreement recovery but cannot separate ambiguity from shift and therefore cannot route, establishing that the value of decomposition lies in the separation enabling interpretable and differentiated action selection.

</details>


### [187] [RaysUp: Ultra-light Universal Feature Upsampling via Geometry-Aware Ray Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22749)
*Yuchuan Ding,Linfei Li,Lin Zhang,Ying Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RaysUp提出了一种超轻量、任务无关、VFM无关的特征上采样框架，通过将特征重建提升到几何感知的光线域，利用空间解耦引导编码、任意分辨率交叉注意力、光线位置编码和几何感知邻域注意力等模块，在仅用AnyUp 16%参数和约7倍更快推理速度的情况下，实现了多种密集预测任务上的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）的patch化或池化输出本质上是低分辨率的，限制了其在需要细粒度、像素级推理的任务中的有效性。现有特征上采样方法要么降低语义保真度，要么依赖于VFM特定的重训练和庞大架构，影响效率和可扩展性。

Method: RaysUp包含四个核心组件：1）空间解耦引导编码器（Spatially Decoupled Guidance Encoder）用于方向感知的引导编码；2）任意分辨率交叉注意力（Any-Resolution Cross-Attention）实现分辨率灵活的重建；3）光线位置编码（RayPE）通过6D Plücker光线坐标注入隐式3D几何先验；4）几何感知邻域注意力（Geometry-Aware Neighborhood Attention）在保持几何一致性的同时实现内容自适应的双边聚合。

Result: 在多种密集预测任务上的大量实验表明，RaysUp仅使用AnyUp 16%的参数，推理速度约快7倍，同时取得了SOTA性能，显著改善了精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: RaysUp作为一种实用且可扩展的通用特征上采样解决方案，在精度和效率之间实现了大幅改进的平衡。

Abstract: Pre-trained Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have become central to modern computer vision due to their powerful semantic representations and strong generalization ability. However, their patchified or pooled outputs are inherently low-resolution, limiting their effectiveness in tasks requiring fine-grained, pixel-level reasoning. Existing feature upsampling approaches either degrade semantic fidelity or rely on VFM-specific retraining and heavy architectures, hindering efficiency and scalability. To address these challenges, we propose RaysUp, an ultra-lightweight, task-agnostic, and VFM-agnostic feature upsampling framework that reconstructs high-resolution feature maps at arbitrary resolutions. Unlike conventional 2D interpolation or attention-based schemes, RaysUp lifts feature reconstruction into a geometry-aware ray domain. Specifically, we introduce a Spatially Decoupled Guidance Encoder for direction-aware guidance encoding, an Any-Resolution Cross-Attention mechanism for resolution-flexible reconstruction, and a novel Ray Positional Encoding (RayPE) that injects implicit 3D geometric priors via 6D Plucker ray coordinates. Finally, a Geometry-Aware Neighborhood Attention module further ensures content-adaptive bilateral aggregation while preserving geometric consistency. Extensive experiments across diverse dense prediction tasks demonstrate that RaysUp achieves state-of-the-art performance while using only 16% of the parameters of AnyUp and delivering approximately 7x faster inference. These results highlight a substantially improved accuracy-efficiency trade-off and establish RaysUp as a practical and scalable solution for universal feature upsampling. Code is available at https://github.com/MAP-RaysUp/RaysUp.

</details>


### [188] [READ More than What You See: Reinforcement Learning for Accurate and Coherent Audio Description Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22766)
*Bo Fang,Xinyao Zhang,Yuxin Song,Hui Zhang,Hang Zhou,Antoni B. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: READ是首个基于强化学习（RL）的音频描述（AD）生成框架，通过序列级优化与多项奖励函数（参考匹配、长度、格式、上下文连贯性奖励），在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD生成方法存在两大问题：一是基于提示的现成多模态模型常偏离AD风格；二是基于训练的下一个词预测方法未能充分挖掘模型能力，且倾向于生成通用表达。为此，作者提出了首个RL框架READ以优化AD生成。

Method: READ采用强化学习框架，将AD生成建模为序列级优化问题，设计了三类奖励函数：参考匹配奖励（对齐参考描述）、长度奖励（控制输出长度）、格式奖励（规范输出格式），并额外引入上下文感知的连贯性奖励（coherence reward）以促进叙述连贯性。

Result: 在MAD-Eval、CMD-AD和TV-AD三个基准上，READ在多种评估指标上均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 强化学习是生成准确且连贯的音频描述的有效范式，READ为该方向提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Audio Description aims to generate concise narrations of essential visual content in audio-visual media for blind and low-vision audiences. Existing methods either rely on prompting off-the-shelf multimodal models, which often mismatch AD style, or partially optimize training-based systems with next-token prediction, which under-explores model capacity and biases generation toward generic expressions. We present READ, the first reinforcement-learning (RL) framework for training-based AD generation. READ formulates AD as sequence-level optimization with reference-matching, length, and format rewards, and further introduces a dedicated coherence reward under context-aware supervision to promote narratively coherent descriptions. Experiments on MAD-Eval, CMD-AD, and TV-AD show that READ substantially outperforms prior methods across diverse evaluation metrics. Our results highlight RL as a promising paradigm for accurate and coherent AD generation. Our codes, models, and benchmark results will be publicly available.

</details>


### [189] [LoCC: Detection and Localization of Lip-Syncing Deepfakes via Counterfactual Frame Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22772)
*Soumyya Kanti Datta,Shan Jia,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LoCC检测框架，通过逐帧评估与反事实估计的一致性来检测唇语同步深度伪造，在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 唇语同步深度伪造的伪影集中在嘴部区域且随时间动态演化，现有方法缺乏精细的时空建模能力，难以准确检测此类局部伪造。

Method: 基于教师-学生学习范式，为每一帧从其时间邻域生成反事实估计，通过评估帧与反事实估计的一致性来检测局部不一致性，实现段级和帧级的细粒度检测与定位。

Result: 在LAV-DF、AVDF1M、FakeAVCeleb、KODF等多个基准数据集上超越现有方法，在不同压缩级别和数据集间表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LoCC通过反事实一致性检测有效捕捉唇语同步伪造的时空局部伪影，为深度伪造检测提供了细粒度的解决方案。

Abstract: Lip-syncing deepfakes are among the most challenging forms of manipulated media because their artifacts are localized almost exclusively to the mouth region and evolve dynamically over time. Detecting such deepfakes requires precise temporal and spatial modeling of lip motion. In this paper, we propose LoCC, a novel detection framework that performs fine-grained detection and localization of lip-syncing deepfakes at both segment and frame levels. Unlike prior approaches that analyze videos holistically, our method evaluates whether each frame aligns with a counterfactual estimate generated from its temporal neighbors. Real videos exhibit strong and stable consistency, whereas lip-sync deepfakes introduce localized inconsistencies. Following a teacher-student learning paradigm, our model effectively captures these frame-level discrepancies and achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods on multiple benchmark lip-syncing deepfake datasets, including LAV-DF, AVDF1M, FakeAVCeleb, and KODF, and generalizes well across compression levels and datasets.

</details>


### [190] [Visual Geometry Transformer in the Wild: Distractor-Free 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22787)
*Tianbo Pan,Xingyi Yang,Shizun Wang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGTW，一种端到端的鲁棒多视图3D重建框架，通过分离和抑制干扰物区域，在不一致的真实世界视图下实现无干扰物点云重建，无需额外3D监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端多视图3D重建方法依赖静态假设（场景无干扰物、完美几何对应），在真实场景中因瞬态干扰物和遮挡而失败。

Method: 提出干扰物感知训练（DAT）策略，在注意力机制中分离干净特征与干扰物污染特征，并强制跨视图特征一致性；使用辅助掩码预测头和新收集的像素级干扰物掩码数据集进行训练。

Result: 在多种真实场景下达到SOTA性能，展现出鲁棒的泛化能力，且计算高效，无需额外3D监督，兼容现有流程。

Conclusion: VGTW有效解决了真实世界中因干扰物导致的多视图3D重建失败问题，为实用化重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current end-to-end multi-view 3D reconstruction methods achieve impressive results, but rely on a restrictive static assumption: the scenes is entire distractor-free with perfect cross-view geometry. This reliance on idealized inputs causes even the most advanced methods to fail in real-world settings, where transient distractors and occlusions present. To address this, we propose Visual Geometry Transformer in the Wild (VGTW), an end-to-end framework for robust reconstruction from inconsistent views. At its core, we isolate and suppress distractor-affected regions while preserving the consistent components across views. Specifically, we introduce a Distractor-aware Training (DAT) strategy that separates clean features from distractor-contaminated ones in the attention mechanism while enforcing feature consistency across images. To enable this, we train the model with an auxiliary mask prediction head, using supervision from a new dataset we collected with pixel-level distractor masks. The resulting VGTW model is a feed-forward network that directly outputs clean, distractor-free point clouds. Remarkably, it requires no additional 3D supervision, remains computationally efficient, and is compatible with existing pipelines. Extensive experiments validate our approach, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance and robust generalization in diverse, real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [Learning Adaptive Dynamical Features via Multi-$τ$ Liquid-Mamba for All-in-one Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22801)
*Hu Gao,Changshuo Wang,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-τ Liquid-Mamba模块，通过输入条件化的多时间尺度液体离散化改进选择性状态空间建模，并构建MLMIR网络用于全能图像恢复，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Mamba的图像恢复模型依赖单一状态演化时间尺度，难以适应空间异质性和任务依赖的退化模式，限制了其在全能图像恢复中的适应性。

Method: 提出Multi-τ Liquid-Mamba自适应状态空间模块，引入输入条件化的多时间尺度液体离散化，通过多个动态分支调节有效离散化步长，并根据退化感知门控权重自适应融合各分支响应，同时保持Mamba的线性复杂度和选择性扫描机制。

Result: 在多个图像恢复基准上，MLMIR在全能图像恢复任务中一致达到最先进性能，在任务对齐恢复设定中也保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: Multi-τ Liquid-Mamba是一种即插即用模块，可无缝集成到现有Mamba架构中，通过多时间尺度动态建模显著提升图像恢复性能。

Abstract: Image restoration aims to recover high-quality images from degraded observations. Recent Mamba-based image restoration models have demonstrated strong potential in modeling long-range dependencies with linear complexity. However, most existing designs still rely on a single state-evolution timescale, which limits their adaptability to spatially heterogeneous and task-dependent degradation patterns in all-in-one image restoration. In this paper, we propose Multi-$τ$ Liquid-Mamba, an adaptive state space module that introduces input-conditioned multi-timescale liquid discretization into selective state space modeling. Instead of changing the overall selective scan pipeline, the proposed module modulates the effective discretization steps of multiple dynamical branches and adaptively fuses their responses according to degradation-aware gating weights. This design allows the model to capture both fast-varying local details and slowly evolving global structures while preserving the linear scaling property of Mamba with respect to sequence length. Importantly, Multi-$τ$ Liquid-Mamba modulates the effective transition dynamics while preserving the original selective parameterization and hardware-efficient selective scan mechanism, making it a plug-and-play module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing Mamba-based architectures. Built upon this framework, we develop a Multi-$τ$ Liquid-Mamba Image Restoration Network (MLMIR) for all-in-one image restoration. Extensive experiments on a wide range of restoration benchmarks demonstrate that MLMIR consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in all-in-one image restoration while remaining highly competitive in task-aligned restoration settings.

</details>


### [192] [CoVStream: Edge-Cloud Collaboration for Understanding of Long Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22804)
*Xu Liu,Guikun Chen,Zihao Yan,Kanzhi Wu,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoVStream是一种边缘-云协作框架，通过边缘端压缩视频为视觉特征和语义描述、云端构建实体图和全局视觉上下文、仅在用户查询时激活大模型推理，在长视频流理解任务中实现87.6%的带宽节省同时保持99.2%的云端基线准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频处理方法（采样-编码-推理）忽略了视频流常由计算受限设备产生的事实，导致云卸载带来高带宽开销，而端侧处理受限于边缘硬件能力，亟需一种兼顾带宽效率与推理能力的解决方案。

Method: 采用边缘-云协作架构：边缘节点将原始视频流蒸馏为紧凑视觉特征和语义描述后传输至云端；云端将这些数据整合为实体图和全局视觉上下文，仅在用户查询到来时激活重型推理模型。

Result: 在VideoMME-Long、LVBench和RTV-Bench三个基准上，CoVStream在LVBench上降低87.6%的带宽消耗，同时保留了99.2%的云端基线准确率。

Conclusion: CoVStream有效解决了长视频流理解中计算受限设备与高质量推理之间的矛盾，实现了带宽效率与推理精度的良好平衡。

Abstract: Long, continuous video streams are an increasingly critical driver of multimedia intelligence. Existing efforts often handle long videos with a sample-encode-reason approach using large models. However, they overlook a crucial deployment fact: the stream is often produced by computationally constrained devices. This forces an untenable compromise: cloud offloading unlocks strong reasoning but incurs prohibitive bandwidth overhead, while on-device processing remains limited by edge hardware capacity. Therefore, we propose CoVStream, the first edge-cloud collaborative framework for understanding long video streams. The edge node distills raw video streams into compact visual features and semantic captions for transmission to the cloud, minimizing bandwidth costs, while the cloud server integrates this data into an entity graph and global visual context, activating the heavy reasoning model only when a user query arrives. Experiments on VideoMME-Long, LVBench, and RTV-Bench show that CoVStream reduces bandwidth usage by 87.6% while retaining 99.2% of the cloud baseline accuracy on LVBench.

</details>


### [193] [Policy-as-Data: Learning Generalizable HOI Diffusion Models from Simulated Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22806)
*Shujia Li,Jianshu Hu,Haiyu Zhang,Yunpeng Jiang,Haoyuan Jin,Xinyuan Chen,Yaohui Wang,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理仿真器的HOI生成框架，利用强化学习策略生成任务导向数据，并通过粗到精的重定向过程弥合仿真模型与参数化人体模型之间的表征差距，实现对未见物体的泛化和长时程生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法依赖昂贵的动作捕捉数据集，可扩展性差、功能多样性有限，且模型难以泛化到未见物体并保持长时程物理一致性。

Method: 提出名为\ours的框架：(1) 在物理仿真器中通过强化学习训练策略，生成任务导向的合成数据；(2) 提出粗到精的重定向过程，弥合仿真简化模型与标准参数化人体模型间的表征差距；(3) 在增强数据集上训练生成模型，实现可泛化的HOI生成。

Result: 综合实验表明，该方法在未见物体泛化、长时程生成能力、动态多样性和物理合理性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 基于物理仿真器的合成数据生成策略能有效克服HOI领域的数据稀缺瓶颈，提升模型泛化能力和物理一致性。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic Human-Object Interactions (HOI) is critical for creating embodied avatars and functional virtual environments. However, current data-driven approaches primarily rely on motion capture datasets, which are expensive to scale and limited in functional diversity. Models trained with these datasets fail to generalize to unseen objects and maintain physical consistency over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that leverages a physics simulator to overcome the data-scarcity bottleneck in HOI generation. Specifically, we propose a scalable pipeline, called \ours, which leverages policies trained with reinforcement learning in a physics simulator for task-oriented data generation and trains a generative model on the augmented dataset for generalizable HOI generation. To seamlessly utilize the synthetic data, we introduce a coarse-to-fine retargeting process that bridges the representation gap between the simplified model used in physics simulator and the standard parametric body models required for generative training. Validated through comprehensive experiments, our method demonstrates enhanced generalization to unseen objects and the capability of long-horizon generation, while exhibiting greater dynamic diversity and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [194] [DBT-Bleed: Dual-Branch Temporal Modeling with Key-Frame Selection for Surgical Bleeding Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22829)
*Sudhanshu Mishra,Jialang Xu,Jensen Ang,Evangelos B. Mazomenos,Beng Ti Ang,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DBT-Bleed双分支多尺度时序建模框架，结合HiRED分层熵驱动帧选择策略，用于手术视频中出血类术中不良事件检测，在MultiBypass数据集和新建的神经内镜垂体手术数据集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中不良事件（IAEs）检测对提升手术安全性至关重要，出血是最常见的事件类型之一。现有方法难以区分出血IAE与视觉相似的手术残留血液，且长手术视频的高效建模与细粒度时序信息保留之间存在计算挑战。

Method: 提出DBT-Bleed双分支多尺度时序建模框架，通过层级时序适配器解耦出血与正常表示，分别建模短期和长期出血进程；引入HiRED分层熵驱动帧选择策略，在保留时序信息的同时去除冗余帧以高效处理长视频。

Result: 在MultiBypass数据集上，出血IAE检测的F1提升6.53%、Recall提升5.62%、MCC提升9%；在跨术式零样本迁移实验中，F1提升6%、MCC提升8%。同时构建了神经外科首个IAE标注数据集EndoPit-IAE。

Conclusion: DBT-Bleef框架在出血IAE检测任务上显著优于视频级基线方法，且具有良好的跨术式零样本泛化能力，为手术视频分析提供了高效的时序建模方案。

Abstract: Intraoperative Adverse Events (IAEs) detection is critical for improving surgical safety, with bleeding being among the most frequent events across many surgery types. Existing methods struggle to distinguish bleeding IAE from visually similar residual blood due to limited temporal reasoning. Moreover, modeling long surgical videos while preserving fine-grained temporal dynamics remains computationally challenging. We propose DBT-Bleed, a dual-branch multi-scale temporal modeling framework disentangling bleeding and normal representations using layer-wise temporal adapters for short- and long-term bleeding progression. To efficiently process long surgical videos without sacrificing fine-grained temporal information, we introduce HiRED, a Hierarchical Entropy-Driven frame selection strategy that retains temporally informative segments while removing redundancy. Experiments on the MultiBypass dataset demonstrate gains of 6.53% in F1, 5.62% in Recall and 9% in MCC values for bleeding IAE detection, consistently outperforming video-level baselines. Additionally, we evaluate cross-procedure generalization on a newly curated dataset from a different surgical procedure type, where DBT-Bleed demonstrates robust transferability by achieving gain of 6% in F1 and 8% in MCC under zero-shot setting. To support this evaluation, we introduce EndoPit-IAE, an Endonasal Pituitary Surgery dataset annotated for IAEs, representing the first IAE-annotated dataset in neurosurgery. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [195] [Homographic Navigation: Geometry-Driven Camera Guidance for Deterministic Planar Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22834)
*Dominik Kroupa,Marek Vaško,Muh Yuzril Ihza Baharuddin,Adam Herout*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何的平面区域导航框架，通过单应性变换统一了学习、对齐和评估，从单张标注参考图像生成无限训练数据，使用稀疏关键点预测实现多目标识别与定位，并引入两阶段推理和稳定扭曲训练策略提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将单应性仅作为输出而非组织变量，且在高精度平面区域捕获场景下，缺乏从极少标注数据实现精确对齐的有效框架。

Method: 提出homographic navigation框架：(1) 通过单应性增强从单张标注参考图生成无限合成训练数据；(2) 单次模型联合识别与定位多个人造物（矩形平面目标）的稀疏关键点；(3) 两阶段推理：全局检测+局部精化；(4) Stable Warp训练策略提升高精度区域性能；(5) 模型同时预测关键点级和样本级置信度。

Result: 实验表明，在极少监督条件下即可实现精确的平面对齐，为几何驱动的摄像机引导和从野外视频数据中学习奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该框架以单应性为核心组织变量，通过数据增强、两阶段推理和稳定训练策略，有效解决了有限输入分辨率下的精度问题，为未来利用视频数据的学习提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We present homographic navigation, a geometry-centric framework for guiding camera acquisition toward precise capture of planar regions. Rather than treating homography as an output, we use it as an organizing variable that unifies learning, alignment, and evaluation. From a single annotated reference image, we generate unlimited synthetic training data via homographic augmentation and train a single-shot model for joint recognition and localization of multiple artifacts (physical objects with a rectangular planar target) through sparse keypoint prediction. To address precision under limited model input resolution, we introduce a two-pass inference scheme with global detection followed by localized refinement, and a Stable Warp training strategy that significantly improves accuracy, particularly in the high-precision regime. The model also predicts confidence estimates per predicted keypoint and per the whole sample. Experimental results demonstrate that accurate planar alignment can be achieved from minimal supervision, providing a foundation for geometry-driven camera guidance and future learning from in-the-wild video data.

</details>


### [196] [OrthoMotion:Disentangling Camera and Subject Motion via Geometry Semantics Orthogonal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22835)
*Zijie Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OrthoMotion通过将相机运动（旋转RoPE相位）和主体运动（门控值注入）分配到注意力算子的正交子空间中，从构造上保证了2D视频生成中相机与主体的解耦控制，无需依赖模型涌现。该方法在保持生成质量的前提下，将串扰降低了2.4倍以上，并在多个骨干网络上泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成中，2D条件将相机运动与主体运动耦合在一起，二者共享相同的逆深度(1/Z)缩放，无法从图像证据中唯一分离。作者证明这种纠缠是表征层面的非可识别逆问题，而非架构问题，因此需要从算子设计层面解决解耦。

Method: 提出OrthoMotion方法，在注意力算子层面实现解耦：(1) 将相机运动编码为几何通道——对旋转位置编码(RoPE)相位进行保范旋转；(2) 将主体运动编码为语义通道——在交叉注意力中引入门控值注入。这两个子算子在代数学上互补（旋转 vs. 平移），通过轻量级解耦正则化器驱动其响应子空间正交，从而消除干扰。

Result: 同时达到了相机控制和主体控制的SOTA准确度；提出新的Cross-Talk Error (CTE)指标量化串扰，相比基线串扰降低2.4倍以上，且不损失生成保真度，在各骨干网络上均能泛化。

Conclusion: OrthoMotion是首个从构造上保证解耦（而非寄希望于涌现）的视频生成方法，证明了通过注意力算子的代数互补性设计可以从根本上解决条件控制中的纠缠问题。

Abstract: Controllable video generation demands independent command of the camera and the subject, yet 2D conditioning entangles them: camera- and object-induced optical flow share the same inverse-depth (1/Z) scaling and cannot be separated from image evidence alone. We first prove that this entanglement is representational, not architectural -- the 2D camera/object split is a non-identifiable inverse problem -- and therefore reframe decoupling as a question of operator design. We resolve it at the level of the attention operator. OrthoMotion routes camera motion into a geometric channel, a norm-preserving rotation of the rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase, and subject motion into a semantic channel, a gated value injection in cross-attention. Because these sub-operators are algebraically complementary -- a rotation versus a translation of the affine action on tokens -- a lightweight decoupling regularizer provably drives their response subspaces to orthogonality, so the two controls stop interfering. To our knowledge OrthoMotion is the first method to guarantee disentanglement by construction rather than hope for it to emerge. It attains state-of-the-art camera and subject accuracy at once while minimizing cross-talk, which we quantify with a new Cross-Talk Error (CTE) metric, cutting cross-talk by more than 2.4x with no loss in fidelity and generalizing across backbones.

</details>


### [197] [G-MASt3R-SfM: Graph-based View Pruning and Multi-stage Optimization for Robust SfM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22856)
*Toshiki Watanabe,Shintaro Ito,Natsuki Takama,Koichi Ito,Takafumi Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对MASt3R在非重叠图像对中产生错误匹配导致SfM位姿估计精度下降的问题，提出G-MASt3R-SfM，通过图视角剪枝模块和多阶段优化模块，在ETH3D数据集上实现相机位姿估计和3D重建的最先进精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MASt3R在非重叠图像对上会产生错误对应关系，现有基于MASt3R的SfM方法（如MASt3R-SfM）将这些不可靠匹配直接纳入优化，导致位姿估计精度显著下降。

Method: 提出G-MASt3R-SfM管道，包含两个核心模块：1) 基于图的视角剪枝模块，从匹配置信度构建场景图并几何剪枝异常视角；2) 多阶段优化模块，从局部一致性逐步扩展到全局一致性来精细化相机参数。

Result: 在ETH3D数据集上，该方法在相机位姿估计和3D重建上均达到最先进精度，有效抑制了异常值带来的噪声。

Conclusion: 通过图视角剪枝和多阶段优化，有效解决了非重叠图像对错误匹配对SfM的干扰问题，提升了整体鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Structure from Motion (SfM) is essential for multi-view 3D reconstruction, however, its accuracy heavily relies on the accuracy of image matching. While the recent correspondence matching method, MASt3R, enables robust matching even under challenging conditions, it tends to generate incorrect correspondences for non-overlapping image pairs. Consequently, existing SfM methods using MASt3R, such as MASt3R-SfM, suffer from significant degradation in pose estimation accuracy as they incorporate these unreliable matches directly into optimization. To address this issue, we propose G-MASt3R-SfM, a novel SfM pipeline that enhances robustness through two key modules. First, the Graph-based View Pruning (GVP) module constructs a scene graph from matching confidence and geometrically prunes outlier views. Second, the Multi-Stage Optimization (MSO) module progressively refines camera parameters by expanding the optimization scope from local consistency to the global consistency. Experiments on the ETH3D dataset demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy in both camera pose estimation and 3D reconstruction, effectively suppressing noise caused by outliers.

</details>


### [198] [Chains That See, Answers That Don't: A Multi-Aspect Evaluation Recipe for Forced Chain-of-Thought on Video-MME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22862)
*Zhichao Fan,Yanhang Li,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过三探针评估方法检验强制思维链（CoT）在视频问答中是否提升视觉语言模型可靠性，发现Qwen2.5-VL的CoT链确实依赖视频内容，但强制CoT并未提升多项选择准确率，且在小模型上甚至有统计学显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑“强制思维链（CoT）能使视觉语言模型在视频问答中更可靠”这一广泛假设，通过系统化评估验证其真实性。

Method: 提出三探针评估方案：(1) 配对准确率（直接、CoT、答案优先、无视频四种条件）；(2) 反事实视频交换诊断（检验CoT链对视频的依赖性）；(3) 四阶视觉退化阶梯。每个探针使用严格和宽松两种正则评分器，并对主族假设进行多重比较校正。应用于Qwen2.5-VL在Video-MME子集上。

Result: CoT链强烈依赖输入视频（视频交换后链重叠崩溃、答案翻转），但强制CoT未提升MCQ准确率；7B小模型上强制CoT甚至产生小幅度但统计显著的准确率下降。

Conclusion: 在Qwen2.5-VL/Video-MME实例化中，强制CoT并未带来预期收益，结论不泛化到其他模型/数据集；代码与原始响应将开源确保可复现。

Abstract: Forced chain-of-thought (CoT) is widely assumed to make vision-language models more reliable on video question answering. We propose a small three-probe evaluation recipe to test that assumption: paired accuracy across direct, CoT, answer-first, and no-video conditions; a counterfactual video-swap diagnostic over the CoT chains; and a four-rung visual-degradation ladder. Each probe is reported under both a strict and a permissive regex scorer, with multiplicity correction over a manuscript-declared primary family. Applied to Qwen2.5-VL on Video-MME subsets, the recipe returns a two-part finding. The CoT chains are strongly video-conditioned: swapping the input video collapses chain overlap and flips most final letters, the opposite of what a "boilerplate-chain" null would predict. Yet on the same data, forced CoT does not improve MCQ accuracy, and on the smaller 7B model it produces a small but statistically supported drop under a post-hoc primary scorer choice. We do not claim this generalizes beyond the Qwen2.5-VL / Video-MME instantiation; the raw responses and a single recomputation script will be released with the supplementary material so every number can be re-derived.

</details>


### [199] [VideoLatent: Video-Language Learning via Latent Self-Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22870)
*Zi-Yuan Hu,Zicong Tang,Shijia Huang,Yanyang Li,Michael R. Lyu,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoLatent，一种通过潜在自强制训练范式进行视觉潜在推理的多模态大语言模型，仅需标准视频-问答三元组即可训练，在14个基准上超越现有方法，相比Video-R1减少训练/推理开销约6倍/68倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于思维链的MLLMs需要大量CoT标注且训练推理开销大；而视觉潜在推理方法主要针对图像任务，依赖额外监督信号，难以扩展到视频任务。

Method: 提出VideoLatent，包含潜在注入模块和潜在自强制训练范式，该范式结合潜在对齐和潜在多样性目标，仅依赖标准视频-问答三元组进行训练。

Result: 在14个基准上一致优于现有标准和潜在MLLMs；相比Video-R1，训练开销降低约6倍，推理开销降低约68倍；具有良好的跨骨干网络和跨模型规模泛化能力。

Conclusion: VideoLatent通过高效的视觉潜在推理机制，在减少标注和计算开销的同时，显著提升了视频理解与推理性能，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning have shown promise in enhancing video understanding and reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing CoT-based MLLMs require labor-intensive CoT annotations and incur substantial training and inference overhead. While visual latent reasoning has emerged as a more efficient alternative, existing methods primarily focus on image tasks and heavily rely on additional supervision signals for visual latent generation (e.g., CoT traces, auxiliary images, or fine-grained annotations), limiting their scalability and transferability to video tasks. To bridge this gap, we introduce VideoLatent, a novel MLLM equipped with a latent injection module tailored for video understanding and reasoning. Specifically, VideoLatent learns to perform visual latent reasoning using a new latent self-forcing training paradigm, which comprises latent alignment and latent diversity objectives, and relies solely on standard video-question-answer triplets. Extensive experiments across 14 benchmarks demonstrate that our model consistently outperforms existing standard and latent MLLMs on general video understanding and complex video reasoning. Compared with Video-R1, our VideoLatent achieves superior computational efficiency, reducing training/inference overhead by $\sim$6$\times$/$\sim$68$\times$. Moreover, experiments demonstrate that our method has strong generalizability to different MLLM backbones and different model scales.

</details>


### [200] [Fursee: Hybrid YOLO-DINOv3 Framework for Fursuit Identity Retrieval and Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22872)
*Jundi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对毛绒变装（fursuit）场景的身份检索与聚类问题，构建了专用数据集，并提出三阶段混合流水线Fursee：YOLO检测裁剪头部区域、ArcFace优化DINOv3嵌入以扩大类间角度间隔、DBSCAN结合轮廓系数自动搜索最优超参数进行无监督聚类。实验表明该方法在各项指标上优于GPT5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Qwen3.7-Plus等多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球毛绒变装大会产生海量fursuit照片，人工分类成本高，亟需自动化身份检索与聚类方案；通用多模态模型缺乏针对复杂fursuit场景的专项优化，且缺乏公开基准数据集。

Method: 三阶段混合流水线Fursee：(1) YOLO检测并裁剪高分辨率fursuit头部区域以提升小目标和遮挡目标的定位；(2) ArcFace优化DINOv3特征嵌入，在特征超球面上扩大不同身份间的角度间隔；(3) DBSCAN进行无监督聚类，采用轮廓系数驱动的自动搜索替代固定手动半径来选择最优超参数。

Result: 在检索和聚类实验中，Fursee在各项评估指标上均优于GPT5.5、Claude Opus 4.8和Qwen3.7-Plus等主流多模态模型，在fursuit头部检索与分组中取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 构建了专用fursuit数据集，提出的三阶段混合流水线有效解决了fursuit场景的身份检索与聚类问题，性能超越当前主流多模态大模型。

Abstract: Global furry conventions produce massive fursuit photographs, while manual sorting brings heavy labor costs and calls for automatic identity retrieval and clustering solutions. General multimodal models lack dedicated optimization for complex fursuit scenes, and no public benchmark dataset exists for this task. To fill this gap, we build a specialized fursuit image dataset and present a three-stage hybrid pipeline Fursee for fursuit identity retrieval and clustering. First, YOLO detects and crops high-resolution fursuit head patches to improve localization of small and overlapping targets. Second, ArcFace optimizes DINOv3 embeddings to enlarge angular separation between different identities on the feature hypersphere. Third, DBSCAN performs unsupervised clustering, with silhouette-coefficient-driven search automatically selecting optimal hyperparameters rather than fixed manual radius. Retrieval and clustering experiments verify that our pipeline outperforms mainstream multimodal models including GPT5.5, Claude Opus 4.8 and Qwen3.7-Plus on all evaluation metrics, achieving competitive performance for fursuit head retrieval and grouping.

</details>


### [201] [SingGuard: A Policy-Adaptive Multimodal LLM Guardrail with Dynamic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22873)
*SingGuard Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SingGuard，一个策略自适应的多模态安全护栏模型族，可接收自然语言规则作为运行时输入，对多模态对话内容进行逐规则安全检查，并支持从快速直接判断到慢速推理的多种推理模式。同时构建了包含56,340个样本、80+细粒度风险类型的多模态护栏评测基准SingGuard-Bench。在六个基准族（35个数据集）上取得了平均F1的SOTA结果，动态规则评估下策略遵循准确率从0.6465提升至0.7415。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数多模态安全护栏要么依赖固定分类体系，要么仅针对狭窄的交互场景，无法适应部署时安全规则的变化。随着视觉语言模型在消费、医疗、金融和企业应用中的广泛部署，风险面扩大，而审核策略可能因产品、地区和部署阶段而异，亟需一种能自适应运行时策略变化的多模态安全评估方法。

Method: SingGuard将活动策略作为运行时输入，逐规则检查目标内容并预测安全标签和触发的规则。支持快速、混合和慢速三种推理模式，实现从直接安全判断到基于策略的审慎推理的连续谱系，并通过快慢解耦强化学习优化推理行为。同时构建了SingGuard-Bench基准，涵盖多模态QA、对抗攻击和动态规则评估等场景，包括跨模态组合风险样本。

Result: 在六个基准族（35个数据集）上，SingGuard在每个族中均取得平均F1的SOTA结果。动态规则评估显示，在运行时策略变化下，策略遵循准确率从0.6465提升至0.7415。

Conclusion: SingGuard通过将策略作为运行时输入、支持多推理模式和解耦强化学习，有效解决了多模态安全护栏在策略动态变化场景下的适应性问题，显著提升了策略遵循准确率，为多模态对话的安全评估提供了灵活且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in consumer, medical, financial, and enterprise applications. This broad deployment expands the safety surface: risks can arise from multimodal question answering, assistant responses, and cross-modal composition, while moderation policies may vary across products, regions, and deployment stages. Most existing guardrails either rely on fixed taxonomies or target only a narrow set of interaction settings, which limits their adaptability when safety rules change at deployment time. We present \textbf{SingGuard}, a policy-adaptive multimodal guardrail model family for safety assessment in multimodal conversations. SingGuard treats the active policy as a runtime input: given natural-language rules, it checks the target content against the active policy rule by rule and predicts both the safety label and the triggered rule. To balance efficiency and interpretability, SingGuard supports fast, hybrid, and slow inference regimes along a fast-to-slow reasoning spectrum, ranging from direct safety judgments to policy-grounded deliberation. We further optimize this behavior with fast--slow decoupled reinforcement learning. We also introduce \textbf{SingGuard-Bench}, a multimodal guardrail benchmark with 56{,}340 examples spanning 80+ fine-grained risk types across multimodal QA, adversarial attack, and dynamic-rule evaluation settings, including cross-modal joint-risk cases where each modality is harmless in isolation but their composition implies unsafe intent. Across six benchmark families (35 datasets), SingGuard achieves state-of-the-art average F1 in every family. Dynamic-rule evaluation further shows improved policy-following accuracy from 0.6465 to 0.7415 under runtime policy shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/Sing-Guard.

</details>


### [202] [FedOT: Ownership Verification and Leakage Tracing via Watermarks for Federated LDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22875)
*Wenlong Cheng,Yuan Gan,Yunqiu Xu,Jiaxu Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedOT框架，首个在联邦LDM中实现所有权验证和泄漏追溯的方案，通过分块水印和潜向量变换技术抵御VAE替换攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦LDM面临恶意客户端未经授权分发或转售模型的风险，现有VAE水印方法无法追溯泄漏源且易被替换攻击移除。

Method: 设计分块水印（所有权验证+客户端识别），提出潜向量变换（LVT）技术增强VAE与U-Net潜空间连接，修改VAE原始潜分布以防御替换攻击。

Result: 实验证明FedOT在所有权验证和可追溯性上均达到优异性能。

Conclusion: FedOT有效解决了联邦LDM场景下的模型版权保护和泄漏溯源问题。

Abstract: Training Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) within Federated Learning (FL) has attracted increasing attention due to its ability to combine the powerful generative capacity of LDMs with the privacy-preserving properties of FL. However, FL requires sharing the global model with multiple participants, which risks unauthorized model distribution or resale by malicious clients. While an intuitive approach is to adopt existing VAE-based watermarking techniques for LDMs in FL, this strategy falls short in addressing such threats due to two fundamental challenges: (1) Existing methods support ownership verification but lack the ability to trace model leakage to a specific malicious client; (2) VAE-based watermarks are vulnerable, as they can be removed simply by replacing the decoder with a clean counterpart. In this paper, we propose FedOT, the first framework for ownership verification and leakage tracing in federated LDMs. Specifically, to address the first challenge, we design a chunked watermark, where the first part is for ownership verification, and the second part is used for client identification. Furthermore, to overcome the second challenge and secure the model against VAE replacement attack, we introduce Latent Vector Transformation (LVT), which strengthens the connection between the VAE and U-Net latent spaces by modifying the original latent distribution of the VAE. Consequently, any attempt to replace the VAE for watermark removal leads to significant image quality degradation, making the LDM model unusable. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedOT achieves superior performance in both ownership verification and traceability. Project page: https://spyzixuan.github.io/FedOT/.

</details>


### [203] [Full-Body Golf Swing Kinematic Reconstruction From a Smartwatch IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22876)
*Yuanshuo Tan,Kezhe Zhu,Xiujie Sun,Chunping Liang,Shuoyang Zhu,Chenquan Xu,Licheng Zhong,Huiming Pan,Yinri Jin,Chang Liu,Bo Xiao,Shenglong Le,Bryndan W. Lindsey,Peter B. Shull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用单手腕IMU（WIT-KinNet）估算高尔夫挥杆全身体姿关节角度的方法，在36名球手、7种球杆和3种挥杆幅度下验证，平均绝对误差8.11±1.84°，骨盆和躯干旋转相关系数达0.98和0.97，首次实现单腕戴设备估算全身体姿运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高尔夫挥杆测量方法（光学动捕、摄像机、多传感器IMU）均不便于球场上实际使用，缺乏一种实用、便携的全身体姿测量方案。

Method: 提出Wrist-IMU Temporal Kinematic Network (WIT-KinNet)，利用模态特定IMU嵌入和时序运动编码学习手腕到身体的运动依赖关系，从单手腕IMU估算全身体姿关节角度。在36名球手（不同技能水平）、7种球杆、3种挥杆幅度下，以智能手表IMU数据和光学动捕地面真值进行受试者交叉验证。

Result: 全身体姿关节角度平均绝对误差8.11±1.84°；骨盆旋转和上躯干旋转时序相关性分别达r=0.98和0.97；X-factor和S-factor相关性均为r=0.96；挥杆幅度、技能水平和球杆类型均显著影响测量误差(p<0.05)。

Conclusion: 首次证实单手腕IMU可有效估算高尔夫挥杆全身体姿运动学，为真实比赛场景下的实用化挥杆分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quantitative measurement of the golf swing is critical for evaluating technique and enabling individualized feedback. However, existing methods are impractical to use on the golf course: optical motion capture is laboratory-bound, camera-based methods require impractical camera placement, and multi-sensor inertial measurement unit (IMU) systems require multi-segment setup and calibration. We thus propose a single wrist-worn IMU approach for estimating full-body joint angles during golf swings. The proposed Wrist-IMU Temporal Kinematic Network (WIT-KinNet) leverages modality-specific IMU embeddings and temporal kinematic encoding to learn wrist-to-body motion dependencies and estimate full-body joint angles during golf swings. Thirty-six golfers spanning beginner and skilled players, performed full, half, and quarter swings using seven club types: driver, 3-wood, 5-hybrid, 5-iron, 7-iron, 9-iron, and sand wedge. The proposed WIT-KinNet was evaluated under subject-wise cross-validation using synchronized smartwatch IMU data and ground-truth kinematics derived from an optical motion capture system. The proposed approach achieved a mean absolute error of 8.11 $\pm$ 1.84$^\circ$ across full-body joint angles. High temporal correlation was observed for pelvic rotation and upper torso rotation (r = 0.98 and 0.97, respectively), with X-factor and S-factor also showing strong correlation (r = 0.96 and 0.96). Linear mixed-effects models of the error revealed that swing amplitude, skill level, and club type all significantly affected measurement differences (p $<$ 0.05). The results establish the first single wrist-worn IMU approach for estimating full-body golf swing kinematics, enabling practical swing analysis during real gameplay.

</details>


### [204] [PHOEBI: An Open-World Benchmark for Bacterial Identification in Phase-Contrast Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22890)
*Aaditya Baranwal,Md Jahid Hasan,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PHOEBI是一个包含12万张相差显微镜图像的数据集，覆盖6种杆状细菌的40种组合，并提出留组合评估协议。研究发现现有聚合器在开放世界场景中系统性失败，而视觉表征本身是可靠的。作者提出基于锚点的轻量化解码器，在未见组合上表现优于分布内验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显微镜下细菌鉴定的计算机视觉方法主要针对单一物种或已知组合，但实际临床和环境样本通常是多微生物混合且包含训练中从未出现的物种组合。缺乏一个标准化的基准来评估模型在开放世界多标签细菌识别场景下的泛化能力。

Method: 构建了PHOEBI数据集（12万张PCM图像，6种杆状细菌的40种组合），设计了留组合评估协议。系统测试了多种梯度训练的逐图像聚合器，并提出了三种基于锚点的轻量化解码器，在共享的冻结瓷砖特征池上通过几何方式捕获每个物种的存在性。

Result: 在留组合评估中，所有梯度训练的聚合器在分布内到保留分摏上F1下降0.39-0.57；13种编码器的线性探针F1差异仅约6个百分点，表明表征本身可靠；提出的锚基解码器在保留组合上得分高于分布内验证集。

Conclusion: 当前基于梯度训练的聚合器存在系统性的开放世界识别失败问题，问题出在聚合器而非视觉表征；基于锚点的几何式解码方法能有效解决该未见组合泛化难题。

Abstract: Optical microscopy enables rapid, label-free imaging of live bacteria and is the standard instrument for species identification across clinical, environmental, and industrial microbiology. Yet field samples are routinely polymicrobial and may contain organisms that were never seen during system training, and no computer-vision benchmark tests multi-label species identification from phase-contrast microscopy (PCM) of such mixtures. We introduce Phase-contrast Optical bEnchmark for Bacterial Identification ($\textbf{PHOEBI}$), a wet-lab-prepared dataset of $120{,}000$ PCM images covering $40$ combinations of six rod-shaped species, paired with a leave-combinations-out (LCO) evaluation protocol that holds out entire species combinations to mirror the practical scenario of a model trained on catalogued mixtures that must generalise to unseen ones. On LCO, every gradient-trained per-image aggregator we test drops $0.39$ to $0.57$ F1 from the in-distribution to the held-out split, a systematic open-world recognition failure in the aggregator, not the visual representation. A linear probe of thirteen different encoders over the same features spreads only about six percentage points of F1 across general-purpose and biomedical pretraining objectives, confirming the representation is sound. We propose three lightweight $\textit{anchor-based}$ decoders that capture per-species presence geometrically over a shared frozen tile-feature pool, scoring $\textit{higher}$ on held-out combinations than on in-distribution validation.

</details>


### [205] [InteractiveAvatar: Real-Time Streaming Video Generation for Consistent and Intent-Aware Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22905)
*Quanyue Song,Yishan He,Yanfei Zhang,Shihao Cheng,Zhixiang He,Zhizhi Guo,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li,Caigui Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出InteractiveAvatar，一种支持实时无限流式生成的视频框架，通过自回归蒸馏实现长时生成，利用长短视觉记忆机制保持视觉一致性，并通过推理-反应模块实现意图感知交互，在多种场景下达到最先进的视觉一致性和实时交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的音频驱动虚拟人生成方法在实时流式场景中难以保持视觉时间一致性，且无法显式感知复杂交互场景中的用户意图。

Method: 1) 自回归蒸馏实现实时无限时长流式生成；2) 长短视觉记忆机制(LSVM)将历史视觉信息压缩为紧凑令牌，保持短期连贯性和长期一致性；3) 推理-反应模块(RRM)包含状态循环策略和缓存切换机制，生成与用户意图一致的语音和动作。

Result: 在多种场景下，该方法在长时生成中达到了最先进的视觉一致性，并实现了实时复杂的用户-虚拟人交互。

Conclusion: InteractiveAvatar通过记忆机制和意图感知模块有效解决了流式虚拟人生成中的视觉一致性和交互意图理解问题，为实时交互式虚拟人应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based models have enabled realistic audio-driven avatar generation in real-time streaming. However, existing approaches struggle to maintain visual temporal consistency and fail to explicitly perceive user intent in complex interactive streaming scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose InteractiveAvatar, a real-time infinite-streaming video generation framework that supports visually consistent avatar video generation and intent-aware interactions. With autoregressive distillation, InteractiveAvatar achieves real-time str-eaming generation of human avatars over arbitrarily long durations. For visual consistency, we introduce a Long-Short Visual Memory (LSVM) mechanism that flexibly compresses historical visual information into compact tokens, preserving both short-range coherence and long-term consistency. To generate avatars with speeches and actions aligned with user intent, we propose a Reasoning-Reaction Module (RRM), which incorporates a State-Cycling strategy and a Cache-Switching mechanism. Extensive experimental results over diverse scenarios demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art visual consistency in long-duration generation, while enabling complex user-avatar interaction in real time.

</details>


### [206] [Intend, Reflect, Refine: An Adaptive Multimodal Reflection Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22913)
*Zisheng Chen,Yuping Qiu,Jianhua Han,Tao Tang,Xiuwei Chen,Likui Zhang,Ying-Cong Chen,Hang Xu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IRR-Drive提出了一种自适应多模态反思框架，通过生成初步文本意图并预测未来语义BEV表示来构建反思空间，实现对初始决策的自我修正，最终生成更可靠的轨迹。该方法在NAVSIM基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在端到端自动驾驶中直接生成最终轨迹，缺乏对未来后果的显式检验，限制了其在复杂动态环境中的可靠性。

Method: IRR-Drive首先生成初步文本意图并预测未来语义BEV表征，构建"文本+BEV"双模态反思空间；通过该空间显式建模场景演化，实现自我纠正和意图优化；同时设计自适应反思奖励，使模型能根据场景复杂度自动选择推理模式。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准的PDMS和EPDMS评估中均取得了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 将自适应反思机制直接集成到规划框架中，能够实现基于场景复杂度的、接地气的、决策感知的轨迹校正，有效提升自动驾驶规划的可信度与表现。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by incorporating reasoning for better interpretability and planning quality. However, most existing approaches directly generate the final trajectory without explicitly examining its future consequences, which limits their reliability in complex and dynamic environments. To address this limitation, we propose IRR-Drive (Intend, Reflect, Refine), an adaptive multimodal reflection framework for autonomous driving. Specifically, to tightly couple high-level reasoning with physical constraints, IRR-Drive first generates a preliminary textual intention and anticipates potential interactions by predicting future semantic bird's-eye view (BEV) representations. This dual-modality (Text + BEV) reflection space explicitly models anticipated scene evolution, enabling the model to rigorously self-correct and refine its initial intent before generating the final trajectory. Furthermore, to balance planning performance and computational efficiency, we construct reflection-oriented training data and design an adaptive reflection reward, enabling the model to adaptively select its reasoning mode according to scene complexity. Instead of using reasoning primarily as an auxiliary interpretation, IRR-Drive directly integrates an adaptive reflection mechanism into the planning framework, enabling grounded, decision-aware trajectory correction that is driven by scene complexity. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on the NAVSIM benchmark in both PDMS and EPDMS. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our multimodal reflection framework and validate the efficacy of the proposed adaptive reflection strategy.

</details>


### [207] [Each Judge Its Own Yardstick: Discovering Per-VLM Taxonomies for Physical Video Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22918)
*Yu Cao,Ziquan Liu,Zhensong Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Shaogang Gong,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JudgeFit方法，通过迭代精炼为每个VLM定制评估维度体系，相比全局统一评估方案在16个VLM上平均提升约32%性能，并揭示了模型家族间的盲点差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用统一的全局评估方案对所有VLM进行物理一致性评估，忽略了不同VLM在训练数据和架构上的差异，导致评估无法准确反映每个模型的实际感知能力。

Method: JudgeFit包含两步迭代过程：(1) 初始构建——用目标VLM对少量视频列举物理错误，聚类生成初始评估分类体系；(2) 诊断精炼——将VLM各维度得分与人类物理常识评分校准，诊断不可靠或冗余维度，由LLM修复，迭代至收敛。

Result: 在16个VLM（涵盖8个模型家族）上，JudgeFit精炼后的评估体系在所有模型上均优于全局统一基线方案，平均相对提升约32%；同时揭示了不同模型家族在可靠性模式上的显著差异和特定盲点。

Conclusion: 为VLM定制化评估维度体系能有效提升物理一致性判断的准确性，全局排名无法反映模型特定盲点，个性化的评估方案更为合理。

Abstract: Maintaining physical consistency in video generators and world models increasingly relies on vision-language models (VLMs) as automated judges that provide reward signals, ranking decisions, and data-filtering criteria. Yet VLMs differ substantially in training data and architecture, encoding physical phenomena through distinct internal representations. A single global evaluation schema therefore gives every VLM the same axes of competence, regardless of what each can actually perceive. We propose JudgeFit, an iterative refinement procedure that discovers a per-VLM evaluation taxonomy. An initial taxonomy is constructed by prompting the target VLM to enumerate physics errors on a small set of videos and clustering the resulting descriptions. The taxonomy is then refined through a diagnostic step: we calibrate the VLM's per-dimension scores to human physical-commonsense ratings, diagnose which dimensions it scores unreliably or redundantly, and prompt an LLM to repair them, iterating until convergence. We further instantiate this procedure as a benchmark and apply it to 16 VLMs spanning eight model families. The refined taxonomy outperforms the global-schema baseline on held-out videos for every VLM tested, with a mean relative improvement of approximately 32%. Beyond aggregate accuracy, the per-VLM profiles expose model-specific blind spots that overall rankings cannot anticipate, with reliability patterns differing markedly across model families.

</details>


### [208] [MythraGen: Two-Stage Retrieval Augmented Art Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22924)
*Quang-Khai Le,Cong-Long Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MythraGen是一个结合检索增强生成和LoRA微调的文本到艺术图像生成框架，通过从外部艺术数据库中检索与查询最相似的图像并用LoRA微调Stable Diffusion，在WikiArt数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成在艺术领域仍面临挑战，难以生成高质量、上下文准确且与文本描述精确匹配的艺术图像。

Method: 提出MythraGen框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 从大规模艺术数据集中提取特征，检索与查询提示最相似的外部艺术图像；(2) 利用检索到的图像通过LoRA微调Stable Diffusion模型，实现特定艺术家风格和内容的生成优化。

Result: 在WikiArt数据集上的实验和用户研究显示，该方法能生成与用户输入高度匹配的艺术作品，显著超越现有解决方案。

Conclusion: MythraGen通过检索增强生成与LoRA微调相结合，有效解决了文本到艺术图像生成中的风格与内容对齐问题，是一种简单高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation has seen rapid advancements, especially with the development of generative models. However, challenges remain in achieving high-quality, contextually accurate image outputs that faithfully match the provided textual descriptions, especially in artistic generation. In this paper, we present a simple yet efficient retrieval augmented generation framework, namely MythraGen, for text-to-artistic image generation by integrating an art retrieval mechanism with LoRA-based model fine-tuning. Our method extracts features from a large-scale art dataset, optimizing the generation process by combining artist-specific styles and content. Particularly, retrieved images from an external art database that have the highest similarity to the query prompt are used to finetune Stable Diffusion using LoRA for desired art generation. Experimental results and user studies on the WikiArt dataset show that our proposed method can generate artworks that closely match the user's input, significantly outperforming existing solutions.

</details>


### [209] [BEV-Denoise: Learning Intrinsic Noise for Accurate Bird's-Eye-View Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22931)
*Dooseop Choi,Kyounghwan An,Kyoung-Wook Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BEV-Denoise框架，利用扩散模型的噪声估计能力去除BEV特征中的固有噪声，实现更精确的BEV语义分割，并在nuScenes数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV特征在视图变换过程中会引入固有噪声，影响下游语义分割任务的准确性。现有方法缺乏对这类噪声的系统性去除机制。

Method: 设计基于UNet的噪声估计模块（受DDPM启发），从学习到的BEV特征中估计噪声并减去；采用任务分解（TD）序贯学习范式，利用预训练的BEV地图自编码器来训练视图变换（VT）编码器，以提供对噪声估计模块的监督。

Result: 将框架应用于四种现有模型（覆盖三种主要VT范式），在nuScenes大规模真实数据集上的实验证明了有效性。

Conclusion: BEV-Denoise能够有效去除BEV特征中的固有噪声，提升语义分割精度，且可泛化应用于不同视图变换范式。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a framework dubbed \textbf{BEV-Denoise} that estimates and removes intrinsic noise from learned Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) features to achieve accurate BEV semantic segmentation. Inspired by the noise estimation capability of Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), we design a UNet-based noise estimation module that learns to estimate the noise from the learned BEV features. The estimated noise is then subtracted from the BEV features and fed to BEV map decoders for the final prediction results. To facilitate supervision for the noise estimation module, we follow a sequential learning paradigm called Task Decomposition (TD) where a pre-trained BEV map autoencoder is employed to train a view transformation (VT) encoder. We share three key insights learned from our intensive experiments that are critical for improved performance. We apply our framework to four existing models, encompassing the three major VT paradigms. Experimental results on a large-scale real-world dataset, nuScenes, demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [210] [Hybrid Compression: Integrating Pruning and Quantization for Optimized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22935)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Long-Bao Nguyen,Van-Hieu Huynh,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段的模型压缩方法，先通过剪枝和量化压缩模型，再用混合专家（MoE）路由多个压缩模型以提升性能，在保持推理效率的同时实现了FLOPs和参数量的大幅降低，仅带来极小的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在嵌入式和边缘设备上面临内存和计算资源受限的挑战，现有模型压缩方法存在模型大小与性能之间的权衡问题。

Method: 两阶段方法：第一阶段使用剪枝和量化等技术压缩模型；第二阶段利用混合专家（MoE）对多个压缩后的模型进行路由，在保持推理效率平衡的同时提升性能。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法成功压缩了CNN模型，实现了FLOPs和参数量的显著减少，且精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 两阶段压缩方法能够有效解决边缘设备上的资源限制问题，在模型大小和性能之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have witnessed remarkable advancements in recent years and have become integral to various applications. However, alongside these developments, training and deployment of neural network models on embedding and edge devices face significant challenges due to limited memory and computational resources. These problems can be addressed with deep neural network compression, which involves a trade-off between model size and performance. In this paper, we propose a novel method for model compression through two phases. First, we utilize model compression techniques, such as pruning and quantization, to significantly reduce the model size. Then, we use Mixture of Experts to route the previously compressed models to enhance performance while maintaining a balance in inference efficiency. MoEs consist of multiple expert models (i.e., compressed models) that are moderately sized and deliver stable performance. Experimental results on several benchmark datasets show that our method successfully compresses CNN models which achieves substantial reductions in FLOPs and parameters with a negligible accuracy drop.

</details>


### [211] [Evaluating self-supervised echocardiographic representations across downstream extraction strategies for left-ventricular segmentation and ejection fraction estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22943)
*Sylwia Majchrowska,Philip Teare*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了自监督表征在超声心动图分析中的质量，发现下游提取策略的选择会显著影响对表征质量的结论，提出应使用多策略评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习在医学影像中应用广泛，但表征质量通常仅通过单一下游评估任务来判断，这对于密集临床任务（如左心室分割和射血分数估计）而言，可能混淆表征质量与下游模型恢复任务相关信息的容量。

Method: 对两种自监督表征（通用DINOv3和任务适应BYOS）采用四种递增表达力的提取策略：启发式提取、冻结线性探针、冻结轻量化解码器探针、部分微调，在EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上进行左心室分割和EF估计的系统评估。

Result: 启发式提取严重低估了冻结表征的可恢复信息量。DINOv3从启发式提取的Dice 0.684、EF MAE 13.01提升至冻结轻量化解码器的Dice 0.906、EF MAE 9.65，接近有监督U-Net基线（0.915/9.72）；BYOS从0.687/17.83提升至0.902/8.74。

Conclusion: 密集超声心动图分析中，关于自监督表征质量的结论高度依赖于下游提取策略，多策略评估应成为密集医学图像分析SSL的重要方法论考量。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is increasingly used in medical imaging to reduce annotation requirements, but representation quality is often judged using a single downstream evaluation setting. For dense clinical tasks, this can confound representation quality with the capacity of the downstream model used to recover task-relevant information. We present a systematic evaluation of self-supervised representations for left-ventricular segmentation and ejection fraction (EF) estimation from apical four-chamber echocardiography on EchoNet-Dynamic. Rather than relying on a single downstream probe, we compare a hierarchy of extraction strategies with increasing expressivity: heuristic extraction without mask-supervised training, frozen linear probes, frozen lightweight decoder probes, and partial fine-tuning. We apply this framework to two complementary representation families: generic frozen self-DIstillation with NO labels (DINOv3) features and a task-adapted dense self-supervised representation, Bootstrap Your Own Segmentation (BYOS). In both families, heuristic extraction substantially understated what was recoverable from the frozen representation. For DINOv3, performance improved from Dice 0.684 and EF mean absolute error (MAE) 13.01 under heuristic extraction to Dice 0.906 and EF MAE 9.65 with a frozen lightweight decoder, approaching a supervised U-Net baseline (Dice 0.915, EF MAE 9.72). For BYOS, performance improved from Dice 0.687 and EF MAE 17.83 under heuristic extraction to Dice 0.902 and EF MAE 8.74 with a frozen lightweight decoder. These results show that conclusions about self-supervised representation quality in dense echocardiographic analysis depend strongly on the downstream extraction strategy used for evaluation. We therefore argue that multi-strategy evaluation is an important methodological consideration for SSL in dense medical image analysis.

</details>


### [212] [Evo-RAD: Navigating Rare Retinal Disease Diagnosis via Self-Evolving Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22955)
*Wangding Xia,Ye Du,Jiashi Lin,Meng Wang,Danli Shi,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evo-RAD提出了一种自进化智能体框架，将证据获取转化为动态决策任务，通过基于图的智能体在马尔可夫决策过程中执行删除不一致证据、插入病理一致样本或终止操作，并利用组相对策略优化与同质性感知奖励进行训练，显著提升了罕见眼病的诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练基础模型在通用医学筛查中表现优异，但在罕见疾病上常因数据代表性不足而失败；检索增强诊断虽有一定缓解，但传统静态方法存在枢纽性问题（hubness problem），即检索到视觉相似但语义错误的常见疾病。

Method: 将检索过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），基于图的智能体观察参考集状态并执行删除（DELETE）、插入（INSERT）或终止（TERMINATE）动作；通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）结合同质性感知奖励来优化智能体，最大化支持参考集的诊断同质性。

Result: 在视网膜疾病基准上，Evo-RAD显著提升罕见病诊断：超越视网膜基础模型21.04%，同时超过检索增强方法和参数高效微调方法3.56%。

Conclusion: 提出的自进化智能体框架Evo-RAD通过动态证据获取有效缓解了枢纽性问题，在罕见病诊断上取得显著提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained foundation models have revolutionized general medical screening, but often falter on rare diseases because such conditions are underrepresented in real-world clinical datasets. While retrieval-augmented diagnosis attempts to mitigate this, conventional static methods frequently succumb to the hubness problem, retrieving visually similar but semantically incorrect common diseases. To address this, we propose Evo-RAD, a self-evolving agentic framework that transforms evidence acquisition into a dynamic decision-making task. We formulate retrieval as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where a graphbased agent observes the reference set state and executes actions to purge discordant evidence (DELETE), acquire pathologically consistent samples (INSERT), or conclude the evolution (TERMINATE). Optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a homogeneityaware reward, the agent learns to maximize the diagnostic homogeneity of the support reference set. Experiments on retinal disease benchmarks show that Evo-RAD substantially improves rare-disease diagnosis, outperforming retinal foundation models by +21.04%, while also surpassing retrieval-based and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods by +3.56%. Code is available at https://github.com/SDH-Lab/Evo-RAD.

</details>


### [213] [Concept Alignment Contrast and Long-Short Prompt Memory for Test-Time Adaptation of SAM3 in Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22963)
*Yubo Zhou,Jianghao Wu,Ping Ye,Shaoting Zhang,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-TTA框架，通过概念对齐对比（CAC）指标和长短提示记忆（LSPM）模块，实现SAM3在医学图像上的测试时自适应，显著提升了前列腺和皮肤病变分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3在自然图像上表现优异，但在医学图像上因域差距导致性能下降。现有视觉-语言TTA方法主要依赖图像级不确定性最小化，无法反映医学分割中的区域级语义正确性，且缺乏稳定持续适应的机制。

Method: 提出CM-TTA框架，包含三个核心模块：1）概念对齐对比（CAC）指标，利用文本-视觉语义一致性评估预测质量并选择最佳增强视图作为监督；2）长短提示记忆（LSPM）模块，短记忆动态融合近期提示实现局部适应，长记忆维持全局稳定提示生成增强伪标签；3）密集监督提示更新（DSPU）策略，利用增强伪标签作为密集监督优化提示嵌入。

Result: 在前列腺和皮肤病变分割任务上的大量实验表明，CM-TTA框架显著优于现有的SAM3测试时自适应方法。

Conclusion: 通过CAC指标和LSPM模块，CM-TTA有效解决了医学图像分割中区域级语义正确性评估和稳定持续适应的问题，为SAM3在医学领域的应用提供了有效方案。
